First, the rules that position the layer as version 2.40 states: the wrapper the reference the layer is placed against, the layer laid over the drawing with its top-left corner at the drawing's top-left corner, each `walk-node` box at its stated position and size, and the drawing visible through every box; a page that also holds a walk carries them once. Second, the zoom rules: each `zoom-link` set at the top right of its box in the 13 px figure text, the pointer cursor shown over it, an outline while it has the keyboard focus, by the `:focus-visible` pseudo-class, that does not rely on colour alone, and the drawing left visible around it. Third, the pass-through rules: inside a `zoom-only` wrapper the layer and every box let the pointer through to the drawing, and only a `zoom-link` takes it. The other rules version 2.40 gives a walked figure (the layer receiving the pointer, the outline of a box under the pointer, the `:target` highlight, the badge and the `walk-source` link) SHALL match no element inside a `zoom-only` wrapper, by their own selectors or by pass-through rules that take precedence over them, so that on a page holding a walked figure and a zoom-only figure the walked figure's layer behaves as version 2.40 states and the zoom-only figure's as this paragraph states; and a page that holds no `zoom-only` wrapper carries version 2.40's rules byte for byte as version 2.40 does. A page holding no `zoom-link` element SHALL NOT carry the zoom or pass-through rules, whatever `zoom-text` link it holds, and carries the positioning rules only where it holds a walk, as version 2.40 states. A zoom link adds no read, no source, no claim and no text of the draft but the labels and titles the figures already render.

Brief v25 SHALL issue schema v17; it is brief v24 changed only in its version line, its draft-schema line, which names schema v17 and that schema's digest, and one paragraph placed immediately after the walk paragraph brief v24 carries, which reads: "A graph or containment `view` may say which level it shows and what it is about: a `level`, `context`, `container` or `component`, with a `scope`, the id of one element. A context view is about a `system` and shows it with the people and other systems around it (types system, external and person); a container view is about a `system` and shows its parts (directory, package, service, job and store), with the systems, people and externals they deal with; a component view is about one of those parts and shows the modules inside it, with parts, people and externals but no system. Every part a container view shows, and every module a component view shows, has the view's scope among its parents. Every other figure that draws a container view's system, or a component view's part, as a box then links that box, and its list item, to the first such view, so a reader can zoom in; a context view is never the view a box zooms to. Show a container or component view's scope only as the outer box of a containment or with `contains` arrows to what it holds, or leave it out. A scope is an element of the model, never a base element, so a context view over a base map also selects the system it is about." Every statement above of what brief v24 carries, equals or differs by holds of brief v25 apart from those three differences. The success report and the brief record SHALL name brief v25.

A draft in schema v16 or earlier SHALL be checked and rendered exactly as version 2.44 checks and renders it, the same findings and the same bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes, except for the `field-version` findings above in place of the `schema` findings at their paths; wherever this requirement says that a draft of an earlier schema is checked or rendered exactly as an earlier version checks or renders it, that exception holds too. A v17 draft that carries no `level` or `scope` SHALL be checked and rendered exactly as the same draft in schema v16, apart from any byte that names the draft's schema or digest. A view's `level` and `scope` move the findings above and their figure's two attributes; a zoom link moves the bytes of a page holding a figure that draws an element with a zoom target (the list item's link; for a figure with a layer, the wrapper, the layer and its boxes, a walked figure's wrapper keeping its bytes; and, on a page holding a `zoom-link` element, the three sets of style rules above), that page's measures in the size report and the rendered-design packet that carries them, and nothing else: no `machine.json`, `dossier.json`, check record, `run-advice.json`, reading ledger, figure-fit entry or figure advice, no byte of a paragraph and no byte of a drawing. The figure plan of version 2.33 names diagrams alone and is never compared with a view's `level` or `scope`, and a planned figure's `kind` is no level. The provider mode is unaffected: the provider stages' closed draft schema gains no `level` or `scope`, and the rendered bytes the provider golden pins do not change. The migration of version 2.24 is unchanged and yields a schema-v12 draft; no migration yields a v17 draft.

From version 2.15 a draft in schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v5` or later MAY carry a top-level member `taskRoutes`: 8 to 15 task routes, each `{"id", "task", "steps"}`. A route's `id` is an identity, unique in the draft as every `id` is. Its `task` is a task a reader brings to the project, phrased as the reader's task, such as "Add a command" or "Change how data is persisted": it is a short label under the rules above for an element's short label, with their findings at `$.taskRoutes[<i>].task`. Its `steps` are 1 to 8 route steps in the order the reader takes them. A route step is a claim block of exactly one form, `{"id", "label": "inferred", "basis": "source", "text", "citations", "quotations"}` with 1 to 50 citations, and MAY carry `see`, `{"kind": "section" or "deep-dive", "target": <identity>}`, naming the section or deep dive of the draft that explains the step; that key is `target`, never `id`, for the reason a diagram home's is. A step has no children, holds no element, and has no Unknown, non-normative or execution-based form, so every step cites at least one file and the lines to read or change. Every rule this requirement and the review requirements state for an Inferred claim block resting on sources SHALL apply to a route step unchanged, at the step's own path `$.taskRoutes[<i>].steps[<k>]`: the path and range checks, the quotation checks, the withheld-item rule, review reach, its row in a fidelity verdict and its entry among `machine.json`'s claims. A draft holding a step whose citation names no blob at the pinned revision, whose cited range lies outside the blob, or whose quotation is not the cited text therefore carries the finding any block would (`path-absent`, `range-beyond-blob`, `range-invalid` or a quotation finding), does not pass check and is not rendered. Syzygy SHALL also report, each beside whatever the schema check reports at that path: `label` at `…steps[<k>].label` for a step labelled other than Inferred; `citation-by-kind` at `…steps[<k>].citations` for a step citing nothing; `unresolved-reference` at `…steps[<k>].see.target` for a `see` whose target is no section of the draft (kind `section`) or no deep dive of it (kind `deep-dive`), with the detail `no section has the identity "<target>"` or `no deep dive has the identity "<target>"`; and, for a draft in schema v4 or earlier that carries the member, `field-version` at `$.taskRoutes` with the detail `` `taskRoutes` needs draft schema polaris-dossier-local-draft-v5 ``. A count outside its bounds is the schema check's finding at `$.taskRoutes` or `$.taskRoutes[<i>].steps`. A draft without the member, in any schema, SHALL be checked and rendered with no finding for its absence. Syzygy SHALL NOT write, complete, reorder or drop a route: a route is the authoring agent's claim.

From version 2.15 the brief SHALL ask for task routes. The brief issued SHALL be `polaris-dossier-brief-v13`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v7`, and SHALL equal brief v12 for the same run except in its version line, in the schema digest its draft-schema line states (the schema document now describes `taskRoutes`), and in one added section, headed `## Task routes` and placed after the Poles section and before the understanding record's. Every statement above of what brief v12 carries, equals or differs by holds of brief v13 apart from those three differences. The section's one paragraph reads: "Write `taskRoutes`: 8 to 15 tasks a reader brings to this project when they mean to work on it, each answered by a short route. Phrase `task` as the reader's own task, in the imperative and in at most 120 characters, such as "Add a command" or "Change how data is persisted": a short label under the rules for an element's short label, never a topic or a file name. Choose the tasks this repository's own structure makes likely (adding the kind of thing it has many of, changing its central behaviour, changing how it stores, sends or checks data, adding a test, changing its build or configuration), and cover different parts of the code rather than one part many times. Each route has 1 to 8 `steps`, in the order the reader takes them. A step is a claim: `label` is `inferred`, `basis` is `source`, and it cites at least one file and the lines the reader reads or changes at that step. Say in the step's text what to do there and name the function, type, table or key to look for, in backticks; the citation carries the file and lines, and Syzygy shows them beside the step. Cite the narrowest range that holds what the step names, never a whole file. Where a section or a deep dive of your draft explains the step, name it in `see` (`{"kind": "section", "target": …}` or `{"kind": "deep-dive", "target": …}`). Syzygy checks a step as it checks any claim block, and a step whose citation names no file or lines at the pinned revision is refused; the fidelity review judges whether the cited lines support the step. A task you cannot route from the code is left out, never written from guesswork; if fewer than 8 can be routed, omit `taskRoutes` and the page says this run has none. Syzygy renders the routes on a "Find by task" page linked beside the overview." The readability guidance stays `polaris-dossier-readability-v5`.

So that a link can reach it, from version 2.15 every claim block of the argument that an operator-agent page draws (the introduction, a block of a section or deep dive, a block inside a presentation element, and a route step) SHALL carry `id="block-<its identity>"` as the last attribute of its own HTML element, unless it is drawn inside a collapsible section (a collapsible element of the draft, or the list Syzygy collapses because it repeats a diagram), in which case it SHALL carry no `id` and is reached through that collapsible section's own element, which carries `id="element-<its identity>"` outside the collapsed part. Every figure drawn from the draft SHALL carry `id="figure-<the diagram's identity>"` as the last attribute of its `<figure>` element; a notice standing in a figure's place carries none. No identity this version adds lies inside a collapsible section, and no page SHALL hold two elements with one `id`. Understanding items, glossary entries and a figure's listed components are not blocks of the argument and gain no identity. For a draft without `taskRoutes`, these identities, the two pages below and the links to them are the only changes version 2.15 makes to an operator-agent page. A draft's route steps are claim blocks, so they are also counted wherever its claims are counted: in each page's count of Unknown claims, in the appendix and in the machine form. The style rules this version adds for the two pages SHALL be carried by those two pages and by no other page. Wherever this requirement states that a render's pages, `dossier.json`, `machine.json`, size report, brief or draft schema equal those an earlier version writes, or makes a change to a byte of one a falsifier, the additions of version 2.15 are excepted: these identities, the two finding pages, the links to them in each page's navigation and on the contents page, their entries in the `pages` lists and the size report, the `taskRoutes` and `nameIndex` members of `machine.json`, the brief's three differences, and the `taskRoutes` member the draft schema document describes.

In the operator-agent mode Syzygy SHALL render, for every draft whatever its schema, the page `find-by-task.html`, titled "Find by task": second in the reading order, directly after `index.html`; linked from every page's navigation directly after the link to `index.html`, with the words "Find by task"; and the first entry of the contents page. Its main content is `<section id="find-by-task" data-topics="" data-task-route-count="<n>">` under the `<h1>` heading "Find by task", where n is the number of routes it draws. For a draft that carries `taskRoutes` and whose draft layer is in force it SHALL hold, in order: one paragraph of Syzygy's words, `<p class="route-note">`, reading "<n> tasks a reader may bring to this project, each answered by a short route. Every step is the authoring agent's claim, labelled and checked like any block of this dossier: it cites the file and lines to read or change at the pinned revision. Names are listed on the index." ("1 task" where it draws one route), its last two words linking the index page; a list, `<nav class="route-list" aria-label="Tasks">` holding one `<ol>` with one `<li>` per route in draft order, linking each task to its route; and each route in draft order as `<section id="route-<route identity>" class="task-route" data-task-route="<route identity>">`, headed by its task in an `<h2>`, holding its steps as one ordered list, `<ol class="steps route-steps">`. Each step SHALL render as the claim block it is, through the rendering every block takes: its text with inline code and glossary links, its quotations as located spans, its label and its numbered sources. After those, inside the step's element, Syzygy SHALL write `<span class="route-where">` reading "Read or change: " and, for each distinct file the step cites that has a source page, in citation order and separated by "; ", a link to that source page's exact text showing the path as inline code, followed, where the render holds the cited lines, by ", " and those lines in the words a citation mark states them ("line 4", "lines 2–3"), a file admitted as several source pieces being one file, linked once at the first of its pieces the step cites and followed by the lines of each of its cited pieces in citation order, separated by ", "; and, where the step carries `see`, `<span class="route-see">` reading "In this dossier: " and a link showing the title of that section, linking the section where it is drawn, or of that deep dive, linking its page or, where it has no page of its own, its section (every section of a draft is drawn on exactly one page). A step that is a withheld item SHALL render as any withheld item does, in Syzygy's words, with no file and no dossier link beside it; a task all of whose steps are withheld items reads "Withheld item", and a task a secret detector matches is withheld as any short label is. For a draft without the member the section SHALL hold, after its heading, exactly `<p class="no-routes" data-task-routes="none">` reading "This run's draft declares no task routes, so this page has none. Syzygy writes no route of its own: a route is the authoring agent's claim, checked like any other. To find where something is named, use the index.", its last two words linking the index page; and while the draft layer is Unknown, exactly `<p class="no-routes" data-task-routes="withheld">` reading "The draft layer is Unknown, so the agent's task routes are not shown.", with n = 0 in both cases.

In the operator-agent mode Syzygy SHALL also render, for every draft whatever its schema, the page `name-index.html`, titled "Index": third in the reading order, directly after `find-by-task.html`, and linked directly after the link to `find-by-task.html` in every page's navigation and on the contents page, with the word "Index". Syzygy SHALL build it at render from what that render already holds, calling no model and reading nothing the render does not otherwise read, and two renders of the same input SHALL give the same bytes. It lists names of five kinds, in this order, the names of one kind ordered without regard to case and then by code unit, as the name is shown (a directory with its final "/"), two equal names keeping the order the site drew them, each with its home and with every block that names it. A `term` is every entry of the rendered glossary, by its title; its home is that entry on `glossary.html`; a block names it where the block's shown text holds the title as a whole word in any case, with or without a final "s", a hyphen of the title also matching a space. A `unit` is every top-level directory the derived structure states as Observed at this render, written with a final "/" (a directory whose fact is Unknown, or any directory while that structure is not established, is not listed); its home is the `derived-structure` region of `index.html`; a block names it where its shown text holds that directory and its "/" at the head of a path. A `path` is every file that has a source page and that at least one block cites or writes out whole; its home is the exact text on its source page, the first of its pieces in source order where it is admitted as several; a block names it where it cites the file or its shown text holds the whole path, neither as the tail nor as the head of a longer path or file name. An `identifier` is every span a block's own text writes in backticks, the span the page draws as inline code, that holds no white space, 2 to 80 Unicode code points and at least one letter, that holds no "=" (an assignment such as a build flag written `NAME=value` is not an identifier), that is not exactly one of the lower-case words `call`, `client`, `data`, `false`, `file`, `get`, `id`, `key`, `make`, `name`, `no`, `none`, `null`, `ok`, `run`, `server`, `set`, `test`, `true`, `type`, `use`, `user`, `value` and `yes`, and that is not a path or directory of this index (with or without its final "/") nor, in any case, a term's title, nor a span the screen's secret detectors match as a path; it is matched in exact case, has no home, and a block names it where the block's own text writes it in backticks. A backticked span is read by one rule wherever this requirement reads one: two backticks on one line of one run of the block's own text between its quotations, so a backtick never pairs across a quotation or a line end. A `figure` is every figure drawn from the draft, by its title, a sequence and a swimlane as a graph figure (a figure of a computed map page is Syzygy's, not the draft's, and is not listed); its home is the figure; it is named first by each list that repeats it (a list drawn inside a collapsible section by that section's place), and then by each block whose shown text holds the title exactly, as a whole word. A word boundary here is any character that is not a letter, a digit or "_"; a path boundary also excludes ".", "/" and "-" before the name, and "/", "-" and a "." followed by a letter, digit or "_" after a path.

A block's shown text is its own text and the quotations Syzygy located for it, and nothing else. A withheld item shows none of the agent's text and names nothing, and a block whose own text a secret detector matches is a withheld item; a path a secret detector matches has no source page and is never a name of any kind; and while the draft layer is Unknown no term, path, identifier or figure is listed, each of those kinds saying "Not indexed: the draft layer is Unknown, so none of the agent's names is shown." Every name on the index is therefore text the same render shows on another page, where it passed that page's screening; the index adds no text of the agent's or of the repository. Blocks are taken in this order: the introduction, the sections' blocks in the order their pages are drawn, the deep-dive pages' blocks, then the route steps. An entry links each place once: a place is a block's own fragment identity or, for a block inside a collapsible section, that section's, so several blocks of one collapsed list are one place. A link shows the title of the block's section or deep dive, "Overview" for the introduction, or the route's task, and a further place of the same words in one entry, a repeating list among them, adds " (2)", " (3)" and so on. A name no block names is still listed, with its home and the words "named by no block", which say nothing about the project. A `path` that more than 24 places name is listed with its home link and, in `<span class="index-many">`, the words "cited or named in <n> places, too many to list: open its source text", and with no block link; its `nameIndex` entry then has an empty `blocks` list and the member `namedBy`, that number, which no other entry carries. The page's main content is `<section id="name-index" data-topics="" data-index-entries="<names>" data-index-links="<links>">` under the `<h1>` heading "Index", where the links are every home link and every block link it draws; then `<p class="index-note">` reading "Every name this dossier shows, on one page, so the browser's own find works over it. Syzygy built it at this render by matching each name against the text of the dossier's blocks; no model was called. It is navigation and makes no claim: a name with no block beside it is written out by no block, which says nothing about the project."; then one sentence of counts, `<p class="index-counts">`, reading "<names> names and <links> links: glossary terms <t>, top-level directories <u>, files <p>, identifiers <i>, figures <f>." ("1 name", "1 link" for one); then links to the five kinds; then, per kind, `<section id="index-<kind>" data-index-kind="<kind>" data-index-count="<count>">` under the `<h2>` heading "Glossary terms", "Top-level directories", "Files", "Identifiers" or "Figures", holding `<ul class="name-index">` with one `<li data-index-name="<name>">` per name (the name, a path, directory or identifier as inline code; its home link, reading "Glossary entry", "Derived structure", "Source text" or "Figure"; and its block links separated by ", ") or, where the kind has no name, `<p class="index-empty">` reading, by kind, "The glossary holds no term.", "No top-level directory is listed: the derived structure is not established at this render, or the tree has none.", "No block cites or names a file with admitted text.", "No block writes an identifier in backticks." or "The dossier draws no figure." The index states no claim and carries no label. Every link on it and on the find-by-task page SHALL name a page of the same render and, where it names a fragment, an element with that `id` on that page, never one inside a collapsible section. Neither page carries a script. In every operator-agent render `machine.json` SHALL carry `taskRoutes`, a list of `{"id", "page": "find-by-task.html", "steps": [<step identities>]}` for the routes drawn (empty where none is), each step also one of its claims, and `nameIndex`, `{"page": "name-index.html", "entries", "links", "names": [{"kind", "name", "home": {"page", "fragment"} or null, "blocks": [{"page", "fragment"}]}]}`, holding exactly what the page draws; `dossier.json` and `machine.json`'s `pages` list carry both pages and the size report measures them. The reading ledger of version 2.4 has no entry for either page and counts no route step: they are pages a reader looks things up on. The provider mode is unaffected: its pages, their bytes and its machine form carry neither page, no fragment identity of this version, and neither member.

ID: REQ-polaris-generation-034
Source: RFC7-2; governing warrants below.
Scope: v1-mandatory

The ordinary `syzygy dossier check` for a structurally valid schema-v13 or schema-v14 operator-agent draft in a form the checker supports SHALL give exactly one source-example advisory in its immediate report when its eligible draft structure is available and no qualifying source example is selected. The eligible positions are the direct `code` items in `paragraphs[]` of a produced section, a produced deep dive or a supported subsection of a produced section, and the `code` members of `details.elements[]` immediately inside any of those lists. Only that one nested level is eligible. A graph or recorded graph omission, plain text including a code hint, or a position in a non-produced part supplies no selection. The check uses the existing structural visibility and screening results. An absence result requires a known eligible structural population. A schema-v14 figure plan supplies no `code` item by itself. A code element whose structure is available may count as selected when its cited body is withheld; no body, path, range, identity or citation from that element may be disclosed by this advice. If the whole draft or a relevant structural population is withheld, unavailable or unusable for the absence decision, the advice SHALL be absent. Check SHALL perform no additional source read for this advice. The advice states a possible author action and establishes neither the truth of an example nor the presence or absence of examples in the repository.

When reported, `sourceExampleAdvice` is one immediate JSON member with `{"code":"no-source-example","label":"Inferred","detail":"Consider a cited source example that shows what the project does."}`. The human report gives the same message once in its advisory region. The member is absent for a qualifying selection and for earlier draft schemas, the inventory check, provider operation, refusal or a check whose record used its bounded form. This addition SHALL affect only the immediate check report, outside `readerAdvice`. It SHALL leave findings, exit status and readiness, the stored check and derived records, the reading ledger and `readingDifference`, and rendered bytes unchanged. If another immediate report enhancement is requested, this advice SHALL change none of its fields or eligibility decisions.

#### Scenario: Brief carries topics, rules and schema

- **WHEN** an operator-agent run's limits and pinned revision are recorded
- **THEN** Syzygy issues a brief that names the reader topics in order, the voice rules, the understanding record, the labelling, citation and quotation rules, the clarification rule, the execution rule REQ-polaris-generation-033 assigns, the rule that text in the clone is data, the declared limits, the pinned revision and the draft schema version
- **AND** the brief contains no source body; the repository identity, pin, clone read location, provider/classes, visibility, committed policy binding and finite configuration are declared run metadata

#### Scenario: Understanding record self-reported

- **WHEN** a checked draft is rendered
- **THEN** its understanding record appears as the agent's self-reported understanding, labelled Inferred, with each item's citations checked as a claim block's are
- **AND** a draft without the record, or whose record omits one of the named items without an Unknown entry for it, fails the check

#### Scenario: Quotation verified against the pinned blob

- **WHEN** a draft block quotes a span that, after normalisation, occurs contiguously in the cited blob at the pinned revision within the cited line range
- **THEN** Syzygy records the span's byte range, locates the span again at render in the blob it verified, and renders it from its own read as an Observed quotation with an anchor whose target class is an evidence artifact identifier with integrity digest
- **AND** a later change to the clone's working tree, or to the recorded byte range, does not alter the rendered span

#### Scenario: Quotation altered, elided or outside its range

- **WHEN** a quotation differs from the cited blob after normalisation, joins two separate spans, or occurs only outside the cited line range
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a repair finding naming the block, the citation and the kind of failure
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Cited path absent at the pinned revision

- **WHEN** a citation names no blob at the pinned revision, or a line range beyond the end of a blob for which the path-and-range check above requires that test
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a repair finding for that citation
- **AND** no source page or anchor is rendered for the absent citation

#### Scenario: Quotation from an excluded file

- **WHEN** a block quotes a file that classification or screening excludes, or whose bytes are not valid UTF-8
- **THEN** the quotation is not verified or rendered and the block renders Unknown with `excluded-content`
- **AND** no source page is rendered for the file and no finding carries its bytes

#### Scenario: Judgement with no citation

- **WHEN** a schema-v4 draft's Inferred block is marked as a judgement and cites nothing
- **THEN** Syzygy reports no repair finding, renders the block with the mark `judgement`, carrying its inference provenance, and counts it in the appendix and machine form
- **AND** the same block with no mark, or in a schema-v3 draft, is a repair finding
- **AND** a fidelity verdict finding that it asserts a project fact is blocking, and otherwise its entry is advisory

#### Scenario: Claim with only withheld citations

- **WHEN** a claim block, understanding item, diagram node or edge, or inventory entry cites at least one span and only spans of an excluded blob, an invalid UTF-8 blob or a range touching a redacted line, or a block relies on a quotation from such a blob
- **THEN** the block, item, node or edge renders Unknown with `excluded-content` in Syzygy's words with its count of withheld sources, none of the agent's text for it renders or enters the machine form, the entry renders nowhere, and neither blocks readiness
- **AND** a short label over only such items renders `Withheld item`, and a path a secret detector matches is counted and never named
- **AND** all are counted in the appendix and machine form and never as verified or as supported, and a verdict marking one accurate or supported fails validation
- **AND** a citation that names no blob at the pinned revision still fails the path check

#### Scenario: Mixed block and a partly redacted file

- **WHEN** one block cites both an admitted span and a withheld span and quotes neither, and another cites a range of a retained file that holds a redacted line elsewhere
- **THEN** both keep their labels and every other rule, the first states the count of its withheld spans in its evidence view and machine form, and its citation mark names no secret-matching path
- **AND** the second's citation carries `†` with the file's count of redacted lines, and no span of it counts as withheld

#### Scenario: Reading view keeps every marking

- **WHEN** a page opens with scripts off
- **THEN** a narrative page's block text holds no label token or file-and-line aside written by Syzygy, the page carries the legend once, and the appendix, review, understanding, discovery and source pages keep their labels
- **AND** for every block, each RFC7-33 distinction it has is in its machine attributes, in DOM text kept by plain-text extraction, and in the evidence view, with equal values across all three
- **AND** a citation mark is reachable by keyboard and shows its file and line on focus and on hover, and an uncited judgement and a framing block each carry their mark

#### Scenario: Disclosure placed in the appendix

- **WHEN** a dossier is rendered
- **THEN** every page carries one sentence of human text and a link to the appendix, and its machine disclosure in full
- **AND** the appendix is the last page of the reading order, and an Unknown affecting a passage still shows at that passage
- **AND** the appendix holds the whole disclosure and the open questions, and is reachable from every page without a pointing device

#### Scenario: Poles within and over the limit

- **WHEN** a schema-v4 draft declares one Pole, five Poles, or more Poles than the run's limit
- **THEN** a page renders for each Pole, other than one holding only the why section, beside a landing page holding the named why section and a map, and more than the limit is a repair finding
- **AND** a section in no Pole or in two, an empty Pole and an unnamed why section are each a repair finding
- **AND** no claim is lost or repeated against the machine form across pages, and a schema-v3 draft keeps its page division under the reading view, the one sentence and the appendix

#### Scenario: Script rule follows the committed policy

- **WHEN** the bound committed policy has no recognized version7 script renderCondition or equally restrictive successor
- **THEN** every page carries no script and its content security policy is unchanged

#### Scenario: Permitted script adds behaviour only

- **WHEN** the exactly bound committed policy has a recognized version7 script renderCondition or equally restrictive successor and a page carries only the script that condition describes
- **THEN** the page's text, claims, markings and links with scripts off equal those with scripts on, and navigation works with scripts off

#### Scenario: Repository that states no reason

- **WHEN** a repository states no reason for its existence
- **THEN** a draft stating Unknown on the why topic renders that Unknown, and a draft stating a reason with no citation and no judgement mark is a repair finding
- **AND** whether the repository truly states no reason, and whether a marked reason asserts a fact, are review judgements

#### Scenario: List repeating a diagram

- **WHEN** a list carries `repeatsDiagram` naming a diagram in its section
- **THEN** it renders collapsed by default with its Unknown count, and each item stays a claim block
- **AND** `repeatsDiagram` naming no diagram in the section is a repair finding

#### Scenario: Invalid Pole limit

- **WHEN** a run declares a Pole limit of zero, a negative number or a non-integer
- **THEN** Syzygy refuses the configuration

#### Scenario: Agent labels its own claim Observed

- **WHEN** a draft block carries the label Observed, carries no label, is Unknown without a reason from the closed list, is Inferred with no citation and no judgement mark, is non-normative with a citation, or is marked as resting on execution without naming a reported command
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a label or citation finding for that block
- **AND** Observed appears on the page only on a quotation Syzygy verified, on a derived structural fact in the region Syzygy computes, or, since version 2.19, on an arrow of a schema-v9 draft that Syzygy finds witnessed by such a fact, or, since version 2.23, on the list item of a containment figure's nesting that Syzygy so finds witnessed, or, since version 2.24, on the arrow and list item of a derived relationship that Syzygy so finds witnessed, or, since version 2.35, on a base element a view draws from a computed map whose fact so renders, and an Unknown block with no source to cite passes

#### Scenario: Repair loop counted

- **WHEN** the agent resubmits a repaired draft
- **THEN** the resubmission is a new revision whose check counts against the repair-cycle limit, and earlier check results and dependent reviews are retired
- **AND** each finding, its repair and both revision identities remain traceable

#### Scenario: Claim inside a presentation element checked as a block

- **WHEN** a table cell, a block in a callout or a collapsible section, a step, a definition or a card's body is unlabelled, is Inferred with no citation and no judgement mark, is Unknown without a reason from the closed list, or carries a quotation that fails
- **THEN** Syzygy reports the finding for that claim by its own identity, as for any claim block

#### Scenario: Short label carries no claim

- **WHEN** an element's caption, column heading, title, term, card label, summary or language is longer than 120 Unicode code points, or carries a citation, a quotation or markup
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a finding for that element's label

#### Scenario: Code excerpt rendered from Syzygy's read

- **WHEN** a draft carries a code excerpt whose citation names at most 60 lines of a blob that Syzygy read and verified at the pinned revision and screening admitted
- **THEN** Syzygy renders those lines from its own read as an Observed quotation with an anchor whose target class is an evidence artifact identifier with integrity digest
- **AND** no text the agent supplied appears in the excerpt, a later change to the clone's working tree does not alter it, and HTML text escaping keeps `<`, `&` and `</pre>` inert

#### Scenario: Code excerpt over its limit

- **WHEN** a code excerpt's citation is malformed or reversed, names more than 60 lines, or names lines beyond the end of a blob Syzygy actually read, including a blob then excluded by content screening or invalid UTF-8
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a repair finding for that excerpt's citation, with no excluded bytes or blob line count
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Code excerpt of an excluded file

- **WHEN** a code excerpt cites a blob that classification or screening excludes, or whose bytes are not valid UTF-8
- **THEN** the excerpt renders Unknown with `excluded-content` in its place and carries none of the blob's bytes
- **AND** its exclusion is not a repair finding

#### Scenario: Presentation element rendered inert

- **WHEN** a checked draft that uses presentation elements is rendered
- **THEN** each element renders as the HTML element whose meaning matches it, with header cells scoped, and reads and navigates completely with scripts off
- **AND** each callout states its tone in words, each empty table cell shows the marker "-", and no fragment target lies inside a collapsible section
- **AND** each claim renders with its label's marking and has its own entry in the fidelity verdict, and each short label renders as plain text with no label marking and no anchor
- **AND** fidelity review judges table cells with their row and column labels and judges callout tone and step order within the presentation residual
- **AND** an agent-supplied language value is only a presentation hint; without one, a language label is derived from the path where one can be determined

#### Scenario: Empty table cell states nothing

- **WHEN** a rendered table contains an empty cell alongside a cell that states an absence or a negative
- **THEN** the empty cell shows only the marker "-" without a label, citation or quotation
- **AND** the absence or negative is a claim with its own Inferred citations or Unknown reason, judged with the row and column labels

#### Scenario: Path-excluded code excerpt left unread

- **WHEN** a code excerpt has an existing blob path excluded before any body read, and a well-formed, non-reversed citation of at most 60 lines
- **THEN** Syzygy leaves the body unread, performs no body-end check and renders the excerpt Unknown with `excluded-content` and no blob bytes
- **AND** a displayed submitted range is disclosed as agent-supplied and unverified, without disclosing the blob's length

#### Scenario: Collapsed section discloses Unknown claims

- **WHEN** a rendered collapsible section contains Unknown claims
- **THEN** its summary shows the count of those Unknown claims even while collapsed
- **AND** no claim loses its own label or fidelity entry

#### Scenario: Figure homed on a deep dive

- **WHEN** a schema-v5 draft's diagram has the home `{"kind": "deep-dive", "target": "d1"}` and `d1` is a produced deep dive of the draft
- **THEN** the check reports no finding for the home, and the figure renders on the deep dive's page after its heading and before its first paragraph, with its caption, legend and tally, and on no other page
- **AND** each of its nodes and edges appears once in the machine form

#### Scenario: Figure homed on a deep dive with no page

- **WHEN** a schema-v5 diagram is homed on a deep dive whose disposition is not `produced`
- **THEN** the check reports no finding for the home, and the figure renders where a figure of that deep dive's section renders, and on no other page

#### Scenario: Figure homed on the overview

- **WHEN** a schema-v5 draft's diagram has the home `{"kind": "overview"}`
- **THEN** the figure renders on `index.html` after the page's header and before the first section, the Pole map and the list of sections not generated, with its caption, legend and tally, and on no other page

#### Scenario: Unknown home refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v5 diagram's home has a `target` naming a section or a deep dive the draft does not have
- **THEN** Syzygy reports an `unresolved-reference` repair finding located within that diagram and naming its identity
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Two homes or a malformed home refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v5 diagram carries both `home` and `sectionId`, or no `home`, or a home whose kind is not `section`, `deep-dive` or `overview`, a section or deep-dive home without a `target`, an overview home with a `target`, or a home with any other member, such as `id`
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a `diagram-home` repair finding located within that diagram and naming its identity
- **AND** a well-formed home whose `target` equals the identity of the section or deep dive it names raises no `duplicate-id` finding

#### Scenario: Schema-v4 draft unchanged

- **WHEN** a draft in schema v4, v3 or v2 is checked and rendered under this version
- **THEN** its findings and the bytes of every rendered page equal those version 2.1 produces for the same draft, except for the additions of version 2.3: the record `derived/rev-<n>.json`, the `derived-structure` region on `index.html`, `data-visual-provenance="curated"` on each figure, the file `derived-structure.json`, the `derivedStructure` member of `machine.json` and the size report's measures of the pages so changed; and the additions of version 2.9: the three computed map pages, the region's `derived-maps` line, their three items on `contents.html`, `computedMaps` in `machine.json`, the three pages' entries in the `pages` lists of `dossier.json` and `machine.json`, and the size report's entries and measures they change; and, since version 2.11, the drawing of each graph of two or more layers, which the readable layout stacks, and the size report's measures of the pages holding one; and the additions of version 2.15: the fragment identities, the two finding pages, the links to them in each page's navigation and on the contents page, their entries in the `pages` lists of `dossier.json` and `machine.json` and in the size report, and the `taskRoutes` and `nameIndex` members of `machine.json`
- **AND** a diagram of such a draft that carries `home`, such a draft that carries `thesisBasis`, such a draft that carries `inventoryOmissions`, and a diagram of such a draft whose kind is `sequence` or `swimlane` or that carries `participants`, `messages`, `lanes` or `steps`, is each a `field-version` repair finding

#### Scenario: Figure too wide at 390 px

- **WHEN** a produced figure is laid out wider than 261 px and not exactly 1048 px wide
- **THEN** `figureFit` holds an entry for that diagram at viewport 390 with its laid-out width W, drawn width W, column width 261 and the width rule failed
- **AND** the entry is no repair finding, and the check's outcome is what it would be without it

#### Scenario: Fitting figure reports nothing

- **WHEN** a produced figure is laid out no wider than 261 px
- **THEN** `figureFit` holds no entry for it at either viewport, and the count of figures examined includes it

#### Scenario: Scaled text below the minimum

- **WHEN** a produced figure is laid out exactly 1048 px wide (three columns at the readable spacing, which only a layout other than the readable one, supplied to check, gives)
- **THEN** at viewport 390 `figureFit` holds an entry for it with drawn width 860, column width 261, effective text size 10.67 and both rules failed
- **AND** at viewport 1440 its drawn width is 966 and its effective text size 11.98, so it holds no entry for it there

#### Scenario: Figure fit stored and deterministic

- **WHEN** the same draft bytes are checked twice, as two revisions
- **THEN** both check records carry a `figureFit` member and the two members' bytes are identical, though the records' revision and check time differ

#### Scenario: Derived tree figure renders Observed

- **WHEN** an operator-agent dossier is checked and rendered for a pinned tree holding files at the root and under two top-level directories
- **THEN** `index.html` ends its main content with the `derived-structure` region, whose figure carries `data-visual-provenance="computed"` and draws the root, one box per directory and a `contains` arrow to each, all solid, each carrying its fact's `data-derived-id`
- **AND** each fact's list entry reads `Derived by Syzygy` and carries `data-epistemic="observed"`, rule `top-level-unit`, version `1`, its path, the record digest and the revision, and the evidence view shows the same values
- **AND** `derived-structure.json` is byte-identical to `derived/rev-<n>.json`, holds its source, capture time, scope and provenance, and its SHA-256 equals the digest the figure and `machine.json` name

#### Scenario: Rule without a declared currency bound renders Unknown

- **WHEN** the currency policy at the Syzygy checkout is absent, uncommitted or of another digest, or declares no bound for `top-level-unit` version 1
- **THEN** every derived element renders Unknown with `no-currency-bound-declared`, drawn dotted with its label ending "?", none is solid or labelled Observed, and `currencyPolicy` is null where no policy applies
- **AND** the figure's legend has no solid entry and its tally counts 0 observed

#### Scenario: Derived record not current renders Unknown

- **WHEN** the run directory's `derived/rev-<n>.json` for the rendered draft revision names another revision, or holds facts other than those the render re-derives from the pinned tree, every path its facts name being the path of a `top-level-unit` fact the render re-derives
- **THEN** every derived element renders Unknown with `stale-beyond-currency-bound`, and none is solid or labelled Observed

#### Scenario: No derived record, or a tree that cannot be listed

- **WHEN** the rendered draft revision has no readable `derived/rev-<n>.json`, its record's state is `unknown`, or the render cannot list the pinned tree
- **THEN** the region holds its heading and the `Not established.` sentence with `missing-evidence` or `source-uncaptured-or-unreachable`, and no figure, list or `derived-structure.json`
- **AND** `derivedStructure` states `missing`, `unusable` or `unknown` with no facts, and its `file` and `sha256` are null

#### Scenario: No size or object id derived for a private repository

- **WHEN** a private repository's pinned tree holds a blob under a denied path, an indeterminate blob, a blob whose body a secret detector matches, and a path a secret detector matches
- **THEN** neither the record, `machine.json` nor any page carries a size or object identifier of any blob, and no fact states one
- **AND** the secret-matching path is counted only in `secretPathsLeftOut` and named nowhere, and a top-level directory holding only secret-matching paths has no fact

#### Scenario: Authored element stays dashed

- **WHEN** a draft figure of schema v8 or earlier, or of schema v9 with no `unit` on its nodes, has a box and an arrow naming a top-level directory and its containment that the record also holds
- **THEN** both keep the draft's marking and are not drawn solid, the figure carries `data-visual-provenance="curated"`, and its legend has no solid entry

#### Scenario: Draft cannot set Observed

- **WHEN** a draft diagram's node or edge carries the label `observed`, or a draft carries a member naming the derived figure or its record
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a finding for that element or member and the draft does not proceed to review
- **AND** no figure drawn from a draft is solid in any element other than an arrow of a schema-v9 or later draft that Syzygy finds witnessed (version 2.19), a derived relationship's arrow of a schema-v12 draft among them (version 2.24), or a base element of a schema-v15 view whose fact renders Observed (version 2.35), and no `<figure>` of a draft carries `data-visual-provenance="computed"`, which only a base element's box, arrow, list item and chip inside one carry

#### Scenario: Derived legend and tally agree with the drawing

- **WHEN** the derived figure is rendered with one top-level directory, and again with several
- **THEN** its legend lists exactly the line styles its drawing uses, its tally's box, arrow, observed and unknown counts equal those of the drawn elements, and a count of one is written in the singular

#### Scenario: More top-level directories than the figure draws

- **WHEN** the pinned tree has fourteen top-level directories
- **THEN** the figure draws the twelve with the most files, ties ordered by path, each with its arrow, and says "2 more top-level directories are listed below and not drawn."
- **AND** the list holds a fact for each of the fourteen

#### Scenario: Derived structure stable across renders

- **WHEN** one checked draft revision is rendered twice under the same policies, in renders in which the reader refuses no read
- **THEN** the two `derived-structure.json` files are byte-identical, and so is `index.html` outside its review-status region
- **AND** a rendered-design review of the first render still counts at the second, and two checks of the same draft bytes write records that differ only in `capturedAt`

#### Scenario: Provider mode unchanged by derived structure

- **WHEN** a provider-mode dossier is rendered under this version
- **THEN** its pages and machine form are byte-identical to those version 2.2 renders for the same input, with no derived region, record, member or provenance attribute

#### Scenario: Reading ledger stored with the check

- **WHEN** a draft with two Poles, a why section, two produced deep dives, a deep dive that is not produced, a section no Pole lists and figures homed in sections, a produced deep dive, the unproduced deep dive and the overview is checked
- **THEN** its check record carries `readingLedger` with one page entry for `index.html`, each Pole page and each deep-dive page, in that order, each with its units, prose words, reading minutes, figures, advisory counts per standard and figure-fit counts, and a `draft` entry with the totals, the unplaced units and the advisories on no page
- **AND** the figure homed on the unproduced deep dive counts on its section's page, the unplaced section counts in `unplacedUnits` and in `draft` alone with its words, figures and advisories, RS-14's advisory counts in `draft` alone, and each advisory and figure counts once in `draft`
- **AND** every number equals the one computed from the draft by the stated rules, and check's report carries the same ledger

#### Scenario: Reading ledger deterministic

- **WHEN** the same draft bytes are checked twice, as two revisions, and an inventory is checked
- **THEN** both draft check records carry byte-identical `readingLedger` members, and the inventory check record carries none

#### Scenario: Difference from the previous run of the repository

- **WHEN** a draft is checked in a run whose state root holds an earlier run of the same repository identity whose latest check record carries a reading ledger, the two ledgers sharing a page with equal numbers and a page with one changed number, and each having a page the other lacks
- **THEN** check's report carries `readingDifference` as `compared`, naming the earlier run, its draft revision, pinned revision and check time, and lists those pages as `unchanged`, `changed` with each delta, `added` and `removed`
- **AND** no difference is written to the check record or to any other file

#### Scenario: No previous run gives an Unknown difference

- **WHEN** a draft is checked in a run whose state root holds no other run of its repository identity with a reading ledger, though it holds a run of another repository with one
- **THEN** `readingDifference` is `unknown` with its words and no ledger number, never zero, and the other repository's run is not compared
- **AND** a run directory of the same form whose `run.json` cannot be read is counted in `unreadableRuns`, never silently passed over

#### Scenario: Server pack keeps the brief

- **WHEN** an operator-agent run's pinned tree holds a path the `server-entry` rule matches, such as `src/server.c`
- **THEN** the brief record's `shapePacks` selects `default@1` and then `server@1`, the brief carries the reader standards byte for byte as `polaris-reader-standards-v1` under that version's heading, and `readerStandards` names that version and its digest
- **AND** the brief equals, byte for byte, the brief `polaris-dossier-brief-v10` that version 2.3 issues for the same run, except its version line, its draft-schema line, the one thesis-basis paragraph and, from version 2.15, the task-routes section

#### Scenario: Default pack drops server-only dimensions

- **WHEN** the pinned tree holds no path the `server-entry` rule matches, or matches only under a vendored, generated, test or example path
- **THEN** only `default@1` is selected, and the brief and the design packet carry the composed standards under their composed heading and digest, with four dimensions and none of the strings `Networking:`, `State and persistence:`, `Concurrency:`, `deployed and divided` or `(server and`
- **AND** the brief's sentence before the standards names the shape packs and the composed digest, `readerStandards` lists the packs, and a design packet whose brief record names a pack this Syzygy does not ship, or a digest the recomposed text does not have, is refused, never built with version 1

#### Scenario: Pack selection recorded and deterministic

- **WHEN** one pinned tree, whose only path with a basename `server-entry` lists is one a secret detector matches, is briefed in two runs
- **THEN** both brief records' `shapePacks` name the same traits, matched paths, packs, versions, digests and reasons, and the secret-matching path is neither listed nor counted
- **AND** `serverEntry.holds` is false and only `default@1` is selected
- **AND** a provider-mode run's profile, reader questions and requested assets are unchanged

#### Scenario: Code-read thesis marked

- **WHEN** a schema-v6 draft of a repository with no README sets `thesisBasis` to `code-reading`, and its introduction is an Inferred block resting on sources that quotes nothing and cites two different code files outside documentation, test, vendored and generated paths
- **THEN** check reports no `thesis-basis` finding, and `index.html` carries the line `The authoring session found no maintainer statement of purpose, so this summary is Polaris's reading of the code, not the maintainers' words.` immediately before the introduction's block and outside it, in both views
- **AND** `machine.json` carries `thesisBasis` with the value `code-reading` and `lineRendered` true, and a `stated` or `none` thesis renders no line

#### Scenario: Unsupported thesis basis refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v6 draft's `code-reading` introduction cites one code file, cites one file twice, cites a code file and a Markdown file, or quotes; or its `stated` introduction cites nothing; or its `none` introduction is Inferred
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a `thesis-basis` repair finding at `$.thesisBasis`, and the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Thesis basis in an earlier schema

- **WHEN** a draft in schema v5 or earlier is checked and rendered under this version, once without and once with a `thesisBasis` member
- **THEN** without it, its findings and the bytes of every rendered page and of `machine.json` equal those version 2.3 produces for the same draft apart from the additions of version 2.15, and with it, it carries a `field-version` repair finding
- **AND** a schema-v6 draft without `thesisBasis` fails its schema

#### Scenario: Withheld thesis carries no basis line

- **WHEN** a schema-v6 draft's `code-reading` introduction cites only spans screening withholds
- **THEN** the introduction renders as a withheld item, with none of the agent's text and no thesis-basis line, and `lineRendered` is false

#### Scenario: Sequence figure drawn

- **WHEN** a schema-v7 draft's produced `sequence` homed in a section has three participants and four messages, one of them to itself, each resolving in its own diagram
- **THEN** check reports no finding for it, and the section's page renders it as a `<figure>` carrying `data-figure-kind="sequence"` and `data-visual-provenance="curated"`, its participants left to right and its messages top to bottom in draft order, laid out 592 px wide and 392 px high, the message to itself drawn as a loop
- **AND** its tally counts 3 participants and 4 messages by marking, its list holds every participant and message once as a claim, and its declarative source is a Mermaid `sequenceDiagram`
- **AND** `machine.json`'s `figureStructures` holds its participants and its messages with their senders and receivers, in draft order, and laid-out width 592

#### Scenario: Swimlane figure drawn

- **WHEN** a schema-v7 draft's produced `swimlane` has three lanes and five steps, two consecutive steps in one lane and the others in turn across lanes
- **THEN** check reports no finding for it, and its page renders it with `data-figure-kind="swimlane"`, each lane a column with its heading box, each step a box in its lane's column, one row to a step in draft order, laid out 592 px wide and 536 px high, with a straight arrow between the two steps in one lane and a curved arrow between steps in two
- **AND** its tally counts 3 lanes and 5 steps by marking, and none of the four order arrows enters the tally, the list or the machine form as a claim
- **AND** its declarative source is a Mermaid `flowchart TB` with one `subgraph` per lane

#### Scenario: Figure kinds fit at both widths

- **WHEN** check computes figure fit for a schema-v7 draft holding a produced sequence of five participants and a produced swimlane of five lanes
- **THEN** both figures are counted in `examined` and `figureFit` holds no entry for either: at 1440 each is drawn at its laid-out 960 px in a 966 px column with 13 px text, and at 390 each shows its phone form at the 261 px column with 13 px text
- **AND** a sequence one of whose messages names no participant of its diagram is listed Unknown, never fitting

#### Scenario: Swimlane reads as a numbered stepper on a phone

- **WHEN** a page holding a swimlane figure is opened in a browser 390 px wide with scripts off
- **THEN** the figure displays an ordered list numbering every step in draft order, each item headed by its lane's label, and does not display the drawing, and neither the figure's box nor the page scrolls sideways, each scroll width being no greater than its client width
- **AND** at 1440 px the same page displays the drawing and not the list, and the page carries no script
- **AND** a sequence figure at 390 px displays its messages as a numbered list, each headed by its sender and receiver, under the same rules, each item's text the stated form with whole labels
- **AND** no item of either list carries an identity, citation, anchor or label token

#### Scenario: Message naming an unknown participant refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v7 sequence's message names in `from` or `to` no participant of its own diagram, one naming a participant of another diagram, or a swimlane's step names in `lane` no lane of its own diagram
- **THEN** Syzygy reports an `unresolved-reference` repair finding at that message's `from` or `to` or that step's `lane`, naming the diagram's identity
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Members that do not fit the kind refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v7 `sequence` carries `nodes` or lacks `messages`, a `swimlane` carries `participants`, a `flow` carries `lanes`, or a participant no message names or a lane no step names stands in an otherwise valid figure
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a `figure-kind` repair finding for each mismatch, at the carried member (`$.diagrams[<i>].nodes`) or, for a missing one, at `$.diagrams[<i>]` naming it, with no `schema` finding at those paths, and a `figure-unused` finding at that participant or lane, each naming the diagram's identity
- **AND** a graph-kind diagram with `nodes` and `edges` alone raises neither, and a sequence of six participants yields a `schema` finding at its `participants`

#### Scenario: Figure kinds in an earlier schema

- **WHEN** a draft in schema v6 or earlier carries a diagram of kind `sequence` or `swimlane`, or a diagram carrying `participants`, `messages`, `lanes` or `steps`
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a `field-version` repair finding at that diagram's `kind` when it is new and at each new member it carries, each in place of the `schema` finding version 2.4 reports at that path, and keeps every other finding version 2.4 reports
- **AND** a stored draft of schema v6 or earlier without them validates, and its findings and the bytes of every rendered page and of `machine.json` equal those version 2.4 produces for the same draft apart from the additions of version 2.15

#### Scenario: Graph figures unchanged by figure kinds

- **WHEN** a schema-v7 draft whose diagrams are all of the six graph kinds is checked and rendered, and the same diagrams are checked and rendered in a schema-v6 draft
- **THEN** each diagram's findings, figure-fit entries and figure bytes are the same in both, its pages carry none of the figure-kind style rules, and `machine.json` carries no `figureStructures`

#### Scenario: Brief v12 states the figure kinds

- **WHEN** an operator-agent run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v12`, from version 2.15 `polaris-dossier-brief-v13` with its task-routes section, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v7`, and, that section and its version line apart, equals brief v11 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one figure-kinds paragraph placed immediately after the thesis-basis paragraph
- **AND** its readability guidance is still `polaris-dossier-readability-v5`

#### Scenario: Provider mode unchanged by figure kinds

- **WHEN** a provider-mode dossier is rendered under this version
- **THEN** its draft schema and diagram kinds are unchanged, and its pages and machine form are byte-identical to those version 2.4 renders for the same input, with no figure-kind attribute, phone form, style rule or `figureStructures`

#### Scenario: Workspace manifests read into the check record

- **WHEN** a draft of a run that relies on no per-project statement, or on one listing `code-content`, is checked under declared-source policy 2.0.0 against a fixture tree whose root `package.json` names a package, declares `workspaces` `["packages/*"]` and one `devDependencies` member, whose package.json files under packages/a and packages/b each name a package and declare one `dependencies` member, and whose root `Cargo.toml` declares `workspace.members` and a `workspace.dependencies` table
- **THEN** the check record's last member is `manifestFacts`, `state` `derived`, with the policy's identity, the recognizer entries in the stated order (ten since version 2.14), each fixture file named Inferred with its fact count, and the facts `package`, `workspace-member` and `dependency` with their rule, rule version 1, path, line span, members and `"epistemic": "inferred"`, in the stated order with identities `_manifest-1` onward
- **AND** every line span is a range the path-and-range check accepts as a citation of its path, the workspace pattern is stated as declared and resolved to no directory, no fact states a version requirement, and check's report carries the member once

#### Scenario: Python project and its dependency sections

- **WHEN** a checked tree's `pyproject.toml` declares `project.name`, two `project.dependencies` strings, one extra under `project.optional-dependencies`, one group under `dependency-groups` holding a string and an include table, and a `tool.poetry` table
- **THEN** `python-project-manifest` records one `package` fact and one `dependency` fact per requirement string, with sections `project.dependencies`, `project.optional-dependencies` and `dependency-groups`, the extra's and the group's key as `group`, and each name the requirement's leading name as declared
- **AND** the include table and the `tool.poetry` table yield no fact

#### Scenario: Workflow jobs and their needs

- **WHEN** a checked tree holds a workflow ci.yml directly under .github/workflows with a `name`, `on` as a mapping of two events, and jobs `build` and `test`, `test` needing `build`, and a workflow x.yml one directory deeper, under .github/workflows/nested
- **THEN** `github-actions-workflow` records one `workflow` fact spanning the whole file, two `workflow-trigger` facts, two `job` facts and one `job-needs` fact from `test` to `build`
- **AND** the nested path is no candidate, and no fact states that a job runs, succeeded or is required

#### Scenario: Malformed manifest is Unknown, never empty

- **WHEN** a checked tree holds a `package.json` that is not valid JSON, a `Cargo.toml` whose `package.name` is an integer, a workflow whose root is a sequence, and a `package.json` repeating its `dependencies` member
- **THEN** each is named with `"epistemic": "unknown"`, `unknownReason` `unparsed` and the `parseFault` `syntax`, `field-type`, `field-type` and `duplicate-key` respectively, with `facts` and `valuesLeftOut` null, and counted in its recognizer's `unknown`
- **AND** no fact is recorded for any of them, and none of them is counted or shown as declaring nothing

#### Scenario: Empty manifests

- **WHEN** a checked tree holds an empty `Cargo.toml`, an empty `pyproject.toml` and an empty `package.json`
- **THEN** the two TOML files are named Inferred with zero facts, and the `package.json` is named Unknown, `unparsed`, `syntax`

#### Scenario: Excluded or withheld blob never read

- **WHEN** a draft is checked against a tree holding manifests whose paths are denied, indeterminate, of a class the run does not admit, a symbolic link, past the read limit, matched by a secret detector, or under a vendored, generated or tests segment, with an object reader double that fails the test on any read of those paths
- **THEN** the double reads none of them, the secret-matching path is counted in `secretPathsLeftOut` and named nowhere in the check record or report, the set-apart paths are counted in `setApart` and named nowhere, and every other one is named Unknown with its reason
- **AND** a manifest whose body a secret detector or the active-content rule excludes is read once, screened, named Unknown with its outcome word and never parsed, and, under a screen double that retains a body with lines withheld, such a manifest is named Unknown `redacted-body` and never parsed

#### Scenario: Go modules Unknown under the bound policy

- **WHEN** a tree holding a `go.mod` at the root and one under cmd/x is checked under declared-source policy 1.0.0 and under 2.0.0
- **THEN** under each, both are listed Unknown with `unknown-extraction-class`, are never read, and `go-module-manifest` records no fact, with `unknown` 2 and `facts` 0
- **AND** under 1.0.0 every named `package.json`, `Cargo.toml`, `pyproject.toml` and workflow path is Unknown for the same reason

#### Scenario: Manifest facts deterministic and independent of the draft

- **WHEN** one run checks two different drafts, one of which does not parse, against the same pinned revision
- **THEN** where the reader refuses no read, the two check records' `manifestFacts` members are byte-identical, and so are those of two runs that bind the same tree, policy bytes, placement subject, visibility and content classes

#### Scenario: Manifest facts stay Inferred

- **WHEN** a draft labels an element Observed and cites a manifest line, and a mutation-run evidence record for a recognizer exists in the repository
- **THEN** every manifest fact and every recognizer entry is labelled Inferred, no draft element is upgraded, and no page, `machine.json`, brief or published file of the run carries a manifest fact but the computed maps of versions 2.9 and 2.14 and their `computedMaps`

#### Scenario: More manifests than the member lists

- **WHEN** a tree holds 260 readable `package.json` files declaring 1,200 facts in all
- **THEN** the first 250 in path order are read and listed, the other 10 are Unknown `over-read-limit` and counted in `filesNotListed` and in `unknown`, the first 1,000 facts in order are listed, and `factsNotListed` counts the rest
- **AND** `candidates` equals the sum of its four parts, and each count is the one the stated rules give

#### Scenario: Unread files sorting first do not hide read ones

- **WHEN** a tree holds 250 readable `package.json` files and, sorting before all of them in path order, 5 `package.json` files that are symbolic links and 5 under a denied path
- **THEN** all 250 readable files are read and are the first 250 entries of `files`, the 10 others are Unknown with `symbolic-link` or `denied-path` and counted in `filesNotListed` and in `unknown`, and `inferred` + `unknown` is 260

#### Scenario: Pages and provider unchanged by manifest facts

- **WHEN** a run whose check records carry `manifestFacts` is rendered, and a provider-mode dossier is rendered, under this version
- **THEN** every page, `machine.json`, `derived-structure.json` and the provider's pages are byte-identical to those the predecessor composition (version 2.5's REQ-034, with version 2.6 in force) renders for the same run, except for the additions of version 2.9, which render those facts on the unit map and the CI pipeline map, and those of version 2.14

#### Scenario: Largest manifest facts read back with the check

- **WHEN** a draft whose check passed has a check record carrying a `manifestFacts` member at every listing cap in its widest form, every count a safe integer, the record taking more than 4,194,304 bytes and at most 16,777,216
- **THEN** the member takes fewer than 13,000,000 bytes, and review, render and close each take that revision as the latest passed draft, so review builds its packets and render renders the site where version 2.7 refused both
- **AND** a check record of 16,777,216 bytes is read, and one of 16,777,217 bytes, of more than 2,000,000 values or with a value nested 17 levels below the root is reported as a check record that cannot be read, never as a passed draft, and the end screen counts it neither as passed nor as refused

#### Scenario: Derived record naming a directory not in the tree

- **WHEN** the run directory's `derived/rev-<n>.json` for the rendered draft revision is exactly what check writes in form, names the run's repository and the pinned revision, carries no secret-matching path, and holds a `top-level-unit` fact and its `contains` fact for a directory with a non-secret name that the pinned tree does not hold
- **THEN** the region holds its heading and the `Not established.` sentence with `missing-evidence` and the words for an entry that is no readable record, and no figure, list or `derived-structure.json`
- **AND** `derivedStructure` states `unusable` with no facts, its `file` and `sha256` null, and that directory's name appears on no page, in no `machine.json` and in no published file
- **AND** so does a record whose every path the tree holds but one of whose facts names a rule other than `top-level-unit` or a version other than 1, and that rule's name appears nowhere

#### Scenario: Deep-dive page named per code point

- **WHEN** a draft that fails the schema is checked with a produced deep dive whose identity holds an ASCII letter, a character outside the Basic Multilingual Plane and a lone surrogate
- **THEN** its reading ledger names that deep dive's page with the letter kept and one `_` for each of the other two, the path the renderer's rule gives that deep dive
- **AND** a deep dive whose identity holds only `A-Z`, `a-z`, `0-9`, `_`, `.` and `-` keeps its page name byte for byte

#### Scenario: Graph diagram with a missing or foreign member in schema v7

- **WHEN** a schema-v7 draft carries a `flow` diagram that lacks `edges` and a `dependency` diagram with `nodes` and `edges` that also carries `steps`
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a `figure-kind` finding at the flow's `$.diagrams[<i>]` naming `edges`, with no `schema` finding at its `edges` path, and a `figure-kind` finding at the other diagram's `steps`, with no `field-version` or `schema` finding there
- **AND** the same two diagrams in a schema-v6 draft report the `schema` finding at the flow's `edges` path and the `field-version` finding at the other's `steps`, as version 2.5 states

#### Scenario: Records Syzygy wrote and the provider unchanged by the repairs

- **WHEN** a run whose derived records and check records are those check wrote under the same screen and Syzygy version as the render, whose check records each take at most 4,194,304 bytes, and whose deep-dive identities hold only `A-Z`, `a-z`, `0-9`, `_`, `.` and `-`, is checked and rendered, and a provider-mode dossier is rendered, under this version
- **THEN** every check record, page, `machine.json`, `derived-structure.json` and provider page is byte-identical to those the predecessor composition (version 2.7's REQ-034) writes and renders for the same run, except for the additions of version 2.9

#### Scenario: Directory map of a current record

- **WHEN** an operator-agent draft revision whose derived record is current at the render, holding 30 top-level directories, files at the root, 2 secret-matching paths and 1 submodule, is rendered
- **THEN** `maps/directories.html` holds the region's note, a coverage line whose counts of files at the root, under listed directories, under directories counted and not listed, and left out for a secret detector sum to the record's `files`, three figures captioned `Top-level directories 1 to 12 of 30, by path`, `Top-level directories 13 to 24 of 30, by path` and `Top-level directories 25 to 30 of 30, by path`, each drawing the root and its directories with solid `contains` arrows, and the list of every fact of the record
- **AND** each figure carries `data-visual-provenance="computed"`, every drawn box and arrow carries its fact's `data-derived-id`, each list entry is byte for byte the region's entry for that fact, and the page names no path or rule the region could not name
- **AND** `index.html`'s region ends with the `derived-maps` line linking the three maps, and `contents.html` lists them after `Sources`

#### Scenario: Directory map counts are re-derived, never read from the record

- **WHEN** the derived record of the rendered revision is current, and after check wrote it an agent session edited its `files` and nothing else
- **THEN** the directory map's coverage line counts the files the render re-derives from the tree, never the record's `files`, carries `data-epistemic="unknown"` and `data-unknown-reason="stale-beyond-currency-bound"`, and ends with the sentence that the record's counts differ from the tree's
- **AND** no count on the line is negative, and its figures and list are the region's, unchanged

#### Scenario: Large directory map paginated, never shrunk

- **WHEN** the pinned tree holds 300 top-level directories, each with files
- **THEN** the record lists 250 and counts 50 in `unitsNotListed`, and the directory map draws 21 figures, each of at most 13 boxes and laid out at most 648 px wide, and counts in its coverage line the files under the 50 directories counted and not listed
- **AND** taken as figure fit takes a sequence or swimlane figure, every map figure fits at 1440 and at 390: its drawing shows unscaled at 1440, its text form shows at 390 with 13 px text, and neither form scrolls sideways

#### Scenario: Directory map with no readable record

- **WHEN** the rendered draft revision has no readable derived record
- **THEN** the directory map holds its heading and the `Not established.` sentence with `missing-evidence` and the region's words, and no note, coverage line, figure or list
- **AND** the unit map draws each place's box dotted, Unknown with `missing-evidence` and no identity, and its package and module boxes dashed and Inferred

#### Scenario: Unit map of a workspace

- **WHEN** the bound screen admits a root `package.json` naming the package `root` and declaring two `workspaces` patterns, and packages/a/package.json and packages/b/package.json naming the packages `a` and `b`, `a` declaring a dependency named `b` in `dependencies`
- **THEN** the unit map draws a figure for `Repository root` with the box `root` and a figure for `packages/` with the boxes `a` and `b`, each package box and `declares` arrow dashed and Inferred and carrying its fact's `data-manifest-id`, and each place's box carrying the mark and `data-derived-id` of its derived fact
- **AND** it draws no arrow between `a` and `b`; its list states `Declares a dependency named b in dependencies.` under packages/a/package.json with the fact's rule, version, path and lines, labelled Inferred; and its coverage line gives the four recognizers' counts and ends with the sentence that arrows between packages are not drawn

#### Scenario: CI pipeline map of a workflow

- **WHEN** the bound screen admits .github/workflows/ci.yml naming the workflow `CI`, triggered by `push` and `pull_request`, with the jobs `build`, `test` needing `build`, and `deploy` needing `test` and `release`
- **THEN** the CI pipeline map holds a section for that path stating `Workflow CI.`, `Triggered by push.` and `Triggered by pull_request.`, and one figure of three dashed boxes and two dashed `needs` arrows, from `test` to `build` and from `deploy` to `test`
- **AND** its list holds `Job deploy needs release, which names no job of this file.`, which is drawn nowhere, and every element and entry of the map is labelled Inferred and none Observed
- **AND** a workflow file with 25 `job` facts has no figure, the note that its jobs are listed below, and the list of all 25

#### Scenario: Maps under a screen that admits no manifest

- **WHEN** a run bound to declared-source policy 1.0.0 is rendered over a tree holding `package.json`, `go.mod` and workflow files, one of them under a secret-matching path
- **THEN** the unit map and the CI pipeline map draw no figure, their coverage lines count every named file as Unknown with no facts taken, and their `Files whose facts are Unknown` lists name each with `unknown-extraction-class` and the reason `excluded-content`
- **AND** the secret-matching file is counted in its recognizer's coverage clause and named on no page and in no `computedMaps`

#### Scenario: Maps never read the stored check record

- **WHEN** the run directory's check record for the rendered revision carries a `manifestFacts` member naming a package and a job that no manifest file of the pinned tree declares
- **THEN** neither map nor `computedMaps` names that package or job: both maps and the member show the facts the render derives itself
