#### Scenario: Draft cannot reach or imitate a computed map

- **WHEN** a draft carries a diagram whose boxes and arrows repeat the directory map's labels, and an unknown member naming a map page
- **THEN** the draft's figure renders only where its home places it, with `data-visual-provenance="curated"` and the draft's own markings, never solid where the draft's element is not Observed, and the unknown member is a schema finding
- **AND** no text of the draft appears on a map page or in `computedMaps`, and no figure of the draft renders on a map page

#### Scenario: Provider mode has no computed maps

- **WHEN** a provider-mode dossier is rendered under this version
- **THEN** it has no map page, no `derived-maps` line, no map item on its contents page and no `computedMaps`, and its pages and machine form are byte-identical to those version 2.8 renders for the same input

#### Scenario: Graph figure too wide at 390 routed to the run file

- **WHEN** a schema-v7 draft one of whose graph figures the figure layout supplied to check lays out 1048 px wide, and one of whose sections holds more than 300 words of its own prose, is checked as revision 0 and, unchanged, as revision 1
- **THEN** each check record's `figureFit` holds the figure's entry at 390 with `width` and `text-size` failed and its `readingLedger` counts it, as before this version
- **AND** each check's report holds the RS-19 advisory and not that entry, its `figureFit.examined` unchanged, and `mechanismAdvice` with `thisRevision` 1, and `inRun` 1 after revision 0 and 2 after revision 1
- **AND** `run-advice.json` then holds that entry once, under rule `graph-without-narrow-form` version 1 with revisions `[0, 1]`, and `occurrences` 2

#### Scenario: Advice the author can act on stays in the packet

- **WHEN** a draft's graph figure has an entry at 1440, or an entry at 390 with a laid-out width under 648 px, or an entry `unknown` because its layout failed, and another run's check is supplied no figure layout
- **THEN** each of the first three entries stays on the report's `figureFit` as `author`-class advice and is in no run file, and each entry of the other run, `unknown` because no layout was supplied, is on no report and is in that run's `run-advice.json` under `no-figure-layout`
- **AND** for every check, the report's `figureFit` entries and its `mechanismAdvice.thisRevision` together number the check record's `figureFit` entries

#### Scenario: Unreadable check record in the run file

- **WHEN** a run holds a draft check record past the read bound, one with no `figureFit` and one whose `figureFit` holds a `mechanism`-class entry, and a later draft is checked
- **THEN** `run-advice.json` counts the first in `checkRecords.unreadable` and the other two in `checkRecords.read`, takes advice from the third alone, and no check outcome, render, review or close reads the file
- **AND** a record whose file name gives revision 2 and whose `revision` is 1, or whose `format` is not `polaris-dossier-check/1`, is counted in `checkRecords.unreadable` and contributes no advice, whatever its `figureFit` holds

#### Scenario: Passed draft whose check record would pass the read bound

- **WHEN** a draft within its byte and value bounds, every quotation of which verifies and which has no other finding, would give a check record of more than 16,777,216 bytes
- **THEN** check writes the record's bounded form, whose `outcome` is `findings` and whose one finding is `check-record-bound`, stating the full record's bytes, values, depth and counts, and which holds no `executionFlags`, `quotations`, `excludedContent`, `citedBlobs`, `figureFit`, `readingLedger` or `manifestFacts`
- **AND** every reader reads that record as a draft check refused, review, render and close take no passed draft from it, and the revision counts against the repair-cycle limit
- **AND** a draft whose full record takes exactly 16,777,216 bytes, at most 2,000,000 values and at most 16 levels is written in full, byte for byte as version 2.9 writes it, and passes

#### Scenario: Check record past the bound for a draft with findings

- **WHEN** a draft with findings would give a check record of more than 16,777,216 bytes
- **THEN** the record written is the bounded form, whose `findings` holds only `check-record-bound`, its detail counting the other findings, and check's report lists every one of them in `findingsLeftOut` and writes them to no file

#### Scenario: Inventory too large for its check record

- **WHEN** an inventory of 2,000 entries with twenty quotations each, every one verified, would give an inventory check record past the read bound
- **THEN** inventory-check writes the bounded form with `counts` and `completeness` and the one finding `check-record-bound`, and the inventory does not count

#### Scenario: Inventory check record read under the bound

- **WHEN** the latest inventory check record is a passed record of 16,777,217 bytes, of more than 2,000,000 values, or with a value nested 17 levels below the root
- **THEN** the inventory does not count, with the reason `the result of inventory revision <n> cannot be read`, and the supervisor takes the inventory as having no checked revision, so it neither proceeds to the fidelity packet nor stops at the repair-cycle limit
- **AND** a record larger than 16,777,216 bytes is refused, by every reader of a draft or inventory check record, before any byte of it is read
- **AND** a device, a FIFO or a directory at a draft or inventory check record's path, or at a derived record's path, and a record file grown past the bound after its size was taken, is each a record that cannot be read, and the reader neither blocks nor reads more than one byte past the bound

#### Scenario: Inventory check record past the earlier reader bound counts

- **WHEN** a passed inventory's check record holds 368 quotations, more than 4,096 values and at most the read bound, and every other rule by which an inventory counts holds
- **THEN** the inventory counts

#### Scenario: Derived record kept but unusable told apart from none kept

- **WHEN** the draft revision's `derived/rev-<n>.json` is the record check wrote, and the render's bound screen matches one of its directory paths as a secret path, or derives one of its directories under no `top-level-unit` fact
- **THEN** the region and the directory map each hold the sentence `Not established. The top-level directories of the tree at revision <full revision> are Unknown at this render: this draft revision's derived-record path holds something this render cannot use or cannot examine; Syzygy or its screen may have changed since the revision was checked, or the path may have been altered.` with `missing-evidence`, and `derivedStructure.state` and `computedMaps.directories.state` are `unusable`, with no facts, `file` or `sha256` and no published record
- **AND** where a directory stands at that path the same words and state appear, and where no entry stands there the sentence ends `no derived record was kept when this draft revision was checked.` and the state is `missing`, byte for byte as version 2.9 renders it

#### Scenario: CI pipeline map lists parse as lists

- **WHEN** a workflow file the bound screen admits declares a name, two triggers and two jobs, and another declares a name, no trigger and no job, and the CI pipeline map is parsed by an HTML parser
- **THEN** each file's section holds `<ul class="map-workflow">` whose items are its workflow entry and either its trigger entries or `No trigger is declared.`, every item of its workflow list is a child of that `<ul>`, every `<li>` of the section is a child of a `<ul>` or an `<ol>`, and no `<li>` is a child of a `<p>`
- **AND** the first file's section holds its figure and `<ul class="map-facts">` with its two `Job <job>.` entries, and the second's holds no figure and no `map-facts` list

#### Scenario: Unknown files counted and not listed on a manifest map

- **WHEN** a tree holds 260 readable `package.json` files, so that the first 250 in path order are read and listed and the other 10 are Unknown `over-read-limit` and counted in `filesNotListed` and in `unknown`, and the unit map is rendered
- **THEN** its `Files whose facts are Unknown` list holds no file entry and the one item `10 files whose facts are Unknown are counted and not listed.`, and never `Every candidate file not left out or set apart was read for facts.`
- **AND** where its recognizers count no Unknown file it holds that sentence, where they have no candidate file it holds `No candidate file.`, and where they list some Unknown files and count one more, it holds those entries and then `1 file whose facts are Unknown is counted and not listed.`

#### Scenario: Reading difference of a bounded check record

- **WHEN** a draft that parses is checked, an earlier run of the same repository in the state root holds a readable reading ledger, and the draft's full check record would be past the read bound
- **THEN** check's report carries `readingDifference` as `{"state": "unknown", "why": "this check's record was written in its bounded form, which keeps no reading ledger", "unreadableRuns": 0, "unreadableCheckRecords": 0}`, with no ledger number, and never the words `this draft does not parse, so it has no reading ledger`

#### Scenario: Readable layout stacks every graph of two or more layers

- **WHEN** a draft whose graph figures are a chain of three boxes and a fan-out of one box to three is checked with the readable layout and rendered in the operator-agent mode
- **THEN** each figure is drawn top to bottom, one box to a row, and laid out 248 px wide, and `figureFit` holds no entry for either at either viewport
- **AND** no entry of that check is classed under rule `graph-without-narrow-form`, and `run-advice.json` holds no advisory under that rule from it

#### Scenario: Uncovered inventory entry listed before review

- **WHEN** an inventory counts and the draft's reading content cites no line a citation of one of its entries cites, no claim block's `text` contains a path that entry cites, and the draft records no omission of it
- **THEN** check's report lists the entry in `inventoryCoverageAdvice.uncovered` with its identifier and the distinct paths it cites, and `counts.uncovered` counts it
- **AND** the advice carries none of the entry's statement, kind, label, Unknown reason, quotations, citation identifiers or line ranges, the check's outcome, findings and exit status are those of the same draft without the advice, and the check record, the step log and every packet and rendered file lack the advice

#### Scenario: Entry covered by overlapping lines or a named path

- **WHEN** an entry cites lines 20 to 40 of a file and the draft's reading content cites lines 10 to 20 of that file, or a claim block's `text` contains a path the entry cites, alone or inside a longer path
- **THEN** the entry is covered and not listed
- **AND** an entry citing lines 21 to 40 of that file, which no claim block names, is listed, as is an entry whose path only the understanding record or the discovery account cites

#### Scenario: Compared advice names its rule, its inventory and its counts

- **WHEN** check compares a draft with an inventory of record
- **THEN** the advice carries `rule` `{"id": "overlap-or-path-named", "version": 2}`, `inventory` with the revision and digest of the frozen inventory read, labelled Inferred with its basis, and `counts` whose `covered`, `omitted` and `uncovered` sum to `entries`
- **AND** its `note` says that the advice sets no outcome and is not stored, what the rule compares, that only identifiers and paths are given, how an omission is recorded, that a represented entry needs no record, and that the rule is mechanical
- **AND** the report of an inventory check carries no `inventoryCoverageAdvice` member

#### Scenario: Justified omission recorded by the author

- **WHEN** a schema-v5 draft's `inventoryOmissions` names an uncovered entry's identifier with a reason
- **THEN** the draft passes as it would without the member, the entry is not listed, `counts.omitted` counts it, `omissions.rows` counts the record's objects that carry an identifier and a non-empty reason, and `omissions.recorded` their distinct identifiers
- **AND** an identifier that is no entry of the inventory is in `omissions.unmatched`, one naming a covered entry is in `omissions.alsoCovered` and that entry counts as covered, the fidelity packet's draft carries the record, and no rendered file carries it or any `reason`
- **AND** an identifier two such objects name is in `omissions.duplicated`, an object whose `reason` is missing, empty or not a string omits no entry, and the fidelity packet's coverage criterion tells the reviewer that the record exists and that a `reason` settles nothing

#### Scenario: No inventory of record gives Unknown coverage

- **WHEN** a draft is checked while no inventory counts, or the highest-numbered checked inventory revision has findings though an earlier one passed, or the frozen inventory is not the bytes its check recorded, or reading or comparing it fails
- **THEN** the advice is `{"state": "unknown", "why", "note"}` with a `why` naming the case, no `uncovered` list and no count
- **AND** the check's outcome, findings and written files are those of the same check without the advice

#### Scenario: Omission record in an earlier schema or malformed

- **WHEN** a draft in schema v2, v3 or v4 carries `inventoryOmissions`
- **THEN** it carries a `field-version` repair finding at `$.inventoryOmissions`
- **AND** a schema-v5 draft whose record has an entry without a `reason`, with a member other than `entryId` and `reason`, with an `entryId` of 101 code points or a `reason` of 8001, or with 2001 entries, carries a schema repair finding, while one that names the same `entryId` twice carries none

#### Scenario: Secret-matching path of an entry not named

- **WHEN** an uncovered entry cites a path that a secret detector of the bound screening policy matches, whether or not a denied-path rule also names it, beside or without other paths
- **THEN** the advice names no such path anywhere and counts it in that entry's `unnamedPaths`
- **AND** the entry is still listed by its identifier

#### Scenario: Compose services, images, ports and dependencies

- **WHEN** a draft is checked under declared-source policy 2.0.0 against a tree whose root `compose.yaml` declares services `web` and `db`, `web` with `image` `nginx:${TAG}`, `ports` holding the string `8080:80`, the integer `443` and a mapping with `target` `9000` and `published` `"9001"`, and `depends_on` the sequence `[db]`, and `db` with `depends_on` a mapping naming `cache` with a condition
- **THEN** `compose-file` records `service` facts for `web` and `db`, one `service-image` fact whose image is `nginx:${TAG}` as written, `service-port` facts whose ports are `8080:80`, `443` and `9001:9000`, and `service-depends-on` facts from `web` to `db` and from `db` to `cache`, eight facts, each Inferred with its line span
- **AND** the condition is not read, and no fact states that a service runs or is reachable, or that `cache` is a service of the file

#### Scenario: Kubernetes objects across documents

- **WHEN** under declared-source policy 2.0.0 a tree holds k8s/app.yaml, a stream of a Deployment whose `metadata.name` is `api`, an empty document and a Service with no `metadata`; deploy/values.yaml, a mapping with neither `apiVersion` nor `kind`; and deploy/chart/templates/svc.yaml
- **THEN** `kubernetes-manifest` records two `kubernetes-object` facts, kind `Deployment` named `api` and kind `Service` with a null name, each spanning its own document's lines, and deploy/values.yaml is named Inferred with zero facts
- **AND** deploy/chart/templates/svc.yaml is a candidate of no recognizer and is counted in the coverage remainder

#### Scenario: Terraform resources and modules, variable and state files never read

- **WHEN** a tree holds infra/main.tf declaring two `resource` blocks, a `module` block whose `source` is `./modules/vpc`, a `variable` block, a `data` block and an attribute whose heredoc holds a line `resource "x" "y" {`, beside infra/prod.tfvars and infra/terraform.tfstate, checked under 2.0.0 with an object reader double that fails the test on any read of the last two
- **THEN** `terraform-configuration` records two `terraform-resource` facts and one `terraform-module` fact, with name and source as declared, and the heredoc, the `variable` block and the `data` block yield none
- **AND** the double reads neither of the other two files, which are a candidate of no recognizer, named nowhere in the member, and counted in the remainder's `notCandidates`

#### Scenario: Make targets

- **WHEN** under declared-source policy 2.0.0 a tree's root `Makefile` holds `.PHONY: build test`, a rule `build: deps`, a rule `test:: build`, a pattern rule `%.o: %.c`, a rule `$(BIN): main.o`, an assignment `CC := gcc`, a target-specific assignment `build: CFLAGS += -O2`, a recipe line holding `deploy: x`, and, inside an `ifeq` conditional, a second rule naming `build`
- **THEN** `makefile` records exactly two `make-target` facts, `build`, spanning its first rule's line, and `test`
- **AND** a `Makefile` holding a line `$(eval $(call rule,x))` is Unknown, `unparsed`, `beyond-reader`, and one holding a tab-led line before any rule is Unknown, `unparsed`, `syntax`

#### Scenario: CMake lists and other makefile names Unknown under the bound policy

- **WHEN** a tree holding a `CMakeLists.txt` at the root and one under src, a `GNUmakefile` and a `makefile` under tools is checked under declared-source policy 1.0.0 and under 2.0.0
- **THEN** under each, all four are listed Unknown with `unknown-extraction-class` and never read, `cmake-lists` records no fact with `unknown` 2 and `facts` 0, and `makefile` counts the other two in its `unknown`
- **AND** under 1.0.0 every named file of the five recognizers of version 2.14 is Unknown for the same reason

#### Scenario: Malformed infrastructure file is Unknown, never empty

- **WHEN** a tree checked under declared-source policy 2.0.0 holds a `compose.yaml` whose `services` is a sequence, a `docker-compose.yml` whose service uses a merge key `<<: *defaults`, a Kubernetes manifest whose `kind` is an integer, a `.tf` file with an unterminated string, a `.tf` file whose `module` block has no `source`, and a `.tf` file whose `module` block repeats `source`
- **THEN** each is named Unknown, `unparsed`, with the `parseFault` `field-type`, `beyond-reader`, `field-type`, `syntax`, `field-type` and `duplicate-key` respectively, `facts` and `valuesLeftOut` null
- **AND** no fact is recorded for any of them, and none is counted or shown as declaring nothing

#### Scenario: Infrastructure files past the read limit

- **WHEN** a tree holds 110 readable `.tf` files and the infrastructure map is rendered
- **THEN** the first 100 in path order are read and listed, the other 10 are Unknown `over-read-limit` and counted in `filesNotListed` and in `unknown`, and the map's `Files whose facts are Unknown` list holds the one item `10 files whose facts are Unknown are counted and not listed.`

#### Scenario: Coverage remainder sums to the tree

- **WHEN** a tree holds a submodule and ten blob entries: `compose.yaml`, `Makefile` and `README.md` at the root; ci.yml under .github/workflows; main.tf and prod.tfvars under infra; `package.json`, a.ts and b.ts under src; and one path under keys that a secret detector matches, and a draft is checked under declared-source policy 2.0.0
- **THEN** the member's `remainder` holds `files` 10, `secretPathsLeftOut` 1, `candidates` 5 and `notCandidates` 4, and the places root (files 3, not candidates 1), `.github` (1, 0), `infra` (2, 1) and `src` (3, 2), in that order, with `placesNotListed` 0
- **AND** `secretPathsLeftOut` + `candidates` + `notCandidates` equals `files`, the places' `files` sum to 9 and their `notCandidates` to 4, the submodule is counted nowhere in it, and neither the secret-matching path nor keys is named in the member

#### Scenario: Coverage remainder on the directory map's page

- **WHEN** the tree of the scenario before is rendered, and so is a tree of 260 top-level directories each holding one source file
- **THEN** the first render's `maps/directories.html` holds, after its `computed-map` section, the `coverage-remainder` section whose paragraph gives the counts 10, 5, 1 and 4 and whose items read `Repository root: files 3; candidates of no rule 1.`, `.github/: files 1; candidates of no rule 0.`, `infra/: files 2; candidates of no rule 1.` and `src/: files 3; candidates of no rule 2.`
- **AND** the second render lists the root and the first 250 directories and ends `More top-level directories, counted and not listed: 10; their files 10; candidates of no rule among them 10.`, and a render whose derived record is missing holds the same section beside the directory map's `Not established.` sentence

#### Scenario: Infrastructure map rendered

- **WHEN** under declared-source policy 2.0.0 a render's pinned tree holds the `compose.yaml` of the scenario "Compose services, images, ports and dependencies" and a root `Makefile` declaring one target
- **THEN** `maps/infrastructure.html`, titled `Infrastructure map`, holds its note naming the five rules, its coverage line ending `No figure is drawn: no rule of this version draws one from these facts.`, no figure, under `Every declared fact` the entries `compose.yaml: 8 facts.` and `Makefile: 1 fact.` with their facts in Syzygy's words, each Inferred and `Derived by Syzygy`, and its closing list
- **AND** the region's `derived-maps` line on `index.html` and the map items on `contents.html` link it fourth, and `machine.json`'s `computedMaps.infrastructure` holds `figures` 0 and those facts and file entries exactly as the member holds them

#### Scenario: Provider mode unchanged by infrastructure recognizers

- **WHEN** a provider-mode dossier is rendered under this version
- **THEN** its pages and machine form are byte-identical to those version 2.12 renders for the same input, with no map page, link, `computedMaps` member or remainder

#### Scenario: Bounded form of a draft that does not parse

- **WHEN** a draft that does not parse is checked and the record check would write is past the read bound, a case no tree within the stated caps reaches and which is tested through an injected measure
- **THEN** check's report carries `readingDifference` as `{"state": "unknown", "why": "this check's record was written in its bounded form, which keeps no reading ledger", "unreadableRuns": 0, "unreadableCheckRecords": 0}`, and never the words `this draft does not parse, so it has no reading ledger`

#### Scenario: Draft, frozen revisions and run file read under their bounds

- **WHEN** check is given, as the draft, a FIFO and then a regular file of 4,194,305 bytes; the frozen draft revision of a passed check is replaced by a FIFO and then by a regular file of 4,194,305 bytes; and `run-advice.json` is replaced by a FIFO and then by a regular file of 16,777,217 bytes
- **THEN** check refuses the first two with `<path> is not a regular file` and `<path> is larger than the 4194304-byte draft bound`, freezing no revision; review, render and close give `the frozen draft drafts/rev-<n>.json cannot be read (not a regular file)` and then `the frozen draft drafts/rev-<n>.json cannot be read (larger than 4194304 bytes)` and take no revision as passed; an inventory checked while that revision is the run's only frozen revision is refused because no frozen draft revision declares a session identifier; and the run file's reader gives no run file for either
- **AND** a FIFO at `brief.json` makes check refuse with `the run holds no readable brief.json: no brief was issued, so the deadline clock has not started and no draft is checked` and the reverification refuse with `declared-changed`, and no reader blocks, and none reads more than one byte past its bound

#### Scenario: Task routes checked as claim blocks

- **WHEN** a draft in schema v5 or later carries `taskRoutes` with 8 routes, each step labelled Inferred on a source basis and citing a file and a line range that exist at the pinned revision, one step quoting its cited lines and one naming a section in `see`
- **THEN** check reports no finding for the routes, records the quotation's byte range as for any block, and the draft passes
- **AND** each step has a row in a counted fidelity verdict as any claim block has, and a verdict without one is not validated

#### Scenario: Route step with an unverifiable citation refused

- **WHEN** a route step cites a path that names no blob at the pinned revision, or a line range beyond the blob, or quotes text that is not the cited text
- **THEN** Syzygy reports `path-absent`, `range-beyond-blob` or the quotation finding at that step's own path under `$.taskRoutes`, exactly as for a block of a section
- **AND** the draft does not pass check and nothing is rendered from it

#### Scenario: Route step without a citation or with another label refused

- **WHEN** a route step cites nothing, or is labelled Unknown, non-normative or Observed
- **THEN** Syzygy reports `citation-by-kind` at its `citations` or `label` at its `label`, beside the schema check's finding, and the draft does not pass

#### Scenario: Route count outside its bounds refused

- **WHEN** a draft carries `taskRoutes` with 7 or 16 routes, or a route with no step or 9 steps
- **THEN** the schema check reports a finding at `$.taskRoutes` or at that route's `steps`, and the draft does not pass

#### Scenario: Route step naming an unknown section refused

- **WHEN** a route step's `see` names a section, or a deep dive, that the draft does not hold
- **THEN** Syzygy reports `unresolved-reference` at `$.taskRoutes[<i>].steps[<k>].see.target` with the detail `no section has the identity "<target>"` or `no deep dive has the identity "<target>"`

#### Scenario: Task routes in an earlier schema

- **WHEN** a draft in schema v4, v3 or v2 carries `taskRoutes`
- **THEN** Syzygy reports `field-version` at `$.taskRoutes` with the detail `` `taskRoutes` needs draft schema polaris-dossier-local-draft-v5 ``, and the draft does not pass

#### Scenario: Draft without task routes still validates and renders

- **WHEN** a stored draft of any schema version without `taskRoutes` is checked and rendered under this version
- **THEN** check reports no finding for the absence and the draft renders
- **AND** `find-by-task.html` holds exactly the paragraph `<p class="no-routes" data-task-routes="none">` saying the run's draft declares no task routes and that Syzygy writes none, with a link to the index, and no route; `machine.json`'s `taskRoutes` is empty

#### Scenario: Find-by-task page renders the routes

- **WHEN** a checked draft with task routes is rendered
- **THEN** `find-by-task.html` is second in the reading order, linked directly after the overview in every page's navigation, and holds each route in draft order under its task, each step with its label, its numbered sources, the words "Read or change: " with each cited file linked to its source page and its cited lines, and, where the step names one, "In this dossier: " with a link to that section or deep dive
- **AND** each step is one of `machine.json`'s claims on that page, and `taskRoutes` lists each route with its steps in order

#### Scenario: Withheld route step

- **WHEN** a route step cites only a file that screening withholds, or a path a secret detector matches
- **THEN** the step renders as a withheld item in Syzygy's words with no file beside it, none of its text appears on the find-by-task page or the index, and the path a secret detector matches appears on neither

#### Scenario: Generated index lists every name

- **WHEN** an operator-agent dossier with a glossary, an established derived structure, cited files, backticked identifiers and figures is rendered
- **THEN** `name-index.html` lists every entry of the rendered glossary, every top-level directory the derived structure states as Observed, every file with a source page that a block cites or writes out, every identifier a block writes in backticks, and every figure title, each with its home and with a link to every block that names it by the stated rules
- **AND** a name no block names is listed with its home and the words "named by no block", and `machine.json`'s `nameIndex` holds exactly the names, homes and blocks the page draws

#### Scenario: Every index link resolves

- **WHEN** the links of `name-index.html` and `find-by-task.html` are followed in the rendered site
- **THEN** each names a page of that render and, where it names a fragment, an element with that `id` on that page
- **AND** no such element lies inside a collapsible section: a block inside one is reached through the collapsible section's own element, and no page holds one `id` twice

#### Scenario: Index built at render without a model

- **WHEN** the same checked draft is rendered twice
- **THEN** the two `name-index.html` and `find-by-task.html` files are byte-identical outside the review-status region, no model is called to build them, and no page carries a script

#### Scenario: Unknown draft layer shows no agent names

- **WHEN** an operator-agent dossier is rendered while its draft layer is Unknown
- **THEN** the index lists no term, path, identifier or figure and says why, and the find-by-task page holds exactly the paragraph `<p class="no-routes" data-task-routes="withheld">`

#### Scenario: Brief asks for task routes

- **WHEN** an operator-agent run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v13`, and it carries the section `## Task routes` after the Poles section and before the understanding record's, asking for 8 to 15 tasks phrased as a reader's task, each step citing the file and lines to read or change
- **AND** it differs from brief v12 for the same run only in its version line, the schema digest of its draft-schema line and that section

#### Scenario: Block a secret detector matches names nothing

- **WHEN** a secret detector matches the own text of a block, or of a route step
- **THEN** the block renders in Syzygy's words, the index lists it under no name, and as a route step it shows no file and no dossier link

#### Scenario: Much-cited file listed once with a count

- **WHEN** more than 24 places cite or name one file
- **THEN** the index lists the file with its source-page link and the words "cited or named in <n> places, too many to list: open its source text", draws no block link for it, and `nameIndex` carries `namedBy` for it with an empty `blocks` list
- **AND** a file 24 places name is listed with all 24 links

#### Scenario: Route step covers an inventory entry

- **WHEN** an inventory counts, one of its entries cites lines that a route step of the draft cites, or a route step's `text` contains a path that entry cites, and no other block of the draft does either
- **THEN** the coverage advice names rule version 2, counts that entry covered and does not list it
- **AND** for a draft without `taskRoutes` the advice's counts and lists are those rule version 1 gives

#### Scenario: Provider mode unchanged by the finding pages

- **WHEN** a provider-mode dossier is rendered under this version
- **THEN** its pages and machine form are byte-identical to those version 2.12 renders for the same input, with no find-by-task page, no index page, no `block-` or `figure-` identity, and neither `taskRoutes` nor `nameIndex`

#### Scenario: Subsections with answers, headings and a contents list

- **WHEN** a schema-v8 draft's produced section `s2`, on a Pole page, carries its own paragraphs and subsections `q1` and `q2`, each with a one-sentence answer and one paragraph, and the page's other section carries none
- **THEN** the Pole page renders `s2`'s own paragraphs, then `<section class="subsection" id="subsection-q1">` holding `<h3>` with `q1`'s title, its answer in `<div class="subsection-answer">` and its paragraph, then the same for `q2`, then `s2`'s deep-dive links
- **AND** immediately before the page's first section it carries `<nav class="page-contents" aria-label="On this page">` listing both sections in page order, `s2`'s item holding links to `#subsection-q1` and `#subsection-q2`, each naming an element of that page
- **AND** every claim of the subsections is checked, reviewed and listed as a claim of `s2`'s own paragraphs is, and that page carries the subsection style rules while a page that renders no subsection carries none

#### Scenario: An answer of more than one sentence

- **WHEN** one schema-v8 subsection's answer reads `The daemon caches reads. It never writes.`, a second's `Reads are cached, e.g. on start.`, a third's `The cache is bounded, as The project states: "Entries expire. Then they are fetched again."`, a fourth's `Reads are cached (on start.) It never writes.`, and a fifth answer is non-normative
- **THEN** check reports a `subsection-answer` repair finding at `$.sections[<i>].subsections[<j>].answer` for each of the first, second, fourth and fifth, naming the subsection's identity, and none for the third, whose inner full stops lie inside a quotation
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Subsections in an earlier schema

- **WHEN** a draft in schema v7 or earlier carries `subsections` on a section
- **THEN** Syzygy reports a `field-version` repair finding at `$.sections[<i>].subsections` in place of the `schema` finding version 2.15 reports there, and keeps every other finding version 2.15 reports
- **AND** a stored draft of schema v7 or earlier without them validates, and its findings and the bytes of every rendered page and of `machine.json` equal those version 2.15 produces for it

#### Scenario: A draft without subsections renders as today

- **WHEN** a schema-v8 draft none of whose sections carries `subsections` is checked and rendered, and the same draft is checked and rendered in schema v7
- **THEN** both yield the same findings and the same bytes of every page and of `machine.json`, other than where a page or a record names the draft's schema version, and no page carries a contents list, a subsection or the subsection style rules
- **AND** a provider-mode render is byte for byte what it was before this version, as the provider golden pins

#### Scenario: Subsection titles and withheld subsections

- **WHEN** one schema-v8 subsection's title is 121 Unicode code points long, a second's title carries a backtick, and a third subsection's answer and paragraphs cite only spans that screening withholds
- **THEN** the first raises a `schema` finding and the second a `label-markup` finding, each at its `$.sections[<i>].subsections[<j>].title`, and the third renders its heading and its contents-list link as `Withheld item` and its answer and claims as withheld items, with none of the agent's words
- **AND** a section that is not generated or not produced, or any section where the draft layer is withheld, renders no subsection, and a page that renders no subsection carries no contents list

#### Scenario: Makefile recipe inside a conditional

- **WHEN** a `Makefile` admitted as code content reads `build:\nifdef V\n\tscp out host:/srv\nendif\n`, and another reads `build:\n\techo start\nifeq ($(V),1)\n\tscp out host:/srv\nelse\n\tcp out /srv\nendif\n` (each `\n` a line feed and each `\t` a tab character)
- **THEN** each file is Inferred with exactly one `make-target` fact, `build`, and no fact names `scp`, `out`, `host`, `cp` or `echo`
- **AND** under the reading of versions 2.14 and 2.15 the first yielded `build`, `scp`, `out` and `host`, which this version corrects

#### Scenario: Makefile tab-led line that is no recipe line

- **WHEN** a `Makefile` admitted as code content reads `A = 1\n\tscp out host:/srv\nbuild:\n`
- **THEN** its tab-led second line follows neither a rule's logical line nor a recipe line, with only blank, comment or conditional-directive lines between, so it is no recipe line and the file is Unknown with the `makefile` fault `syntax`
- **AND** the same lines with `build:` first and `A = 1` last yield the one `make-target` fact `build`, and `A = 1\n\t\nbuild:\n`, whose tab-only line is blank, yields it too

#### Scenario: Makefile continuation lines and define bodies

- **WHEN** one `Makefile` reads `SRCS = a.c \\\n\tb.c\n`, a second `build: a \\\n\tb\n`, and a third `define compile\n\t$(CC) -c $<\nendef\n` with no rule (each `\\` a backslash)
- **THEN** each is Inferred: the first with no fact, its tab-led physical line continuing the assignment's logical line; the second with the one `make-target` fact `build`, its tab-led physical line continuing the rule's logical line; and the third with no fact, its tab-led line lying inside a `define` body
- **AND** none of the three has the `syntax` fault

#### Scenario: Makefile directive lines are never rules

- **WHEN** a `Makefile` reads `ifeq (x,a:b)\nbuild:\nendif\nvpath %.c src:lib\ninclude C:/rules.mk\n`
- **THEN** it is Inferred with the one `make-target` fact `build`: the conditional-directive line and the lines whose first word is `vpath` or `include` hold a `:` but are never rules

#### Scenario: Brief v14 states subsections

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v14`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v8`, and equals brief v13 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one subsections paragraph placed immediately after the figure-kinds paragraph

#### Scenario: Arrow witnessed by a fact of its identical type

- **WHEN** a schema-v9 graph figure has a node `repo` whose unit is `{"kind": "repository-root"}`, a node `src` whose unit is `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "src"}` and an Inferred edge `e1` from `repo` to `src` with relation `contains` and label `holds the code`, over a pinned tree of three files, README.md, src/a.ts and web/package.json, whose paths no secret detector matches, rendered while the currency policy of version 2.3 applies
- **THEN** check reports no finding for `e1`, and the render draws its arrow solid, carrying `data-epistemic="observed"`, `data-relation="contains"`, `data-witness-fact="_tree-contains-1"`, `data-witness-rule="top-level-unit"` and `data-witness-rule-version="1"`, labelled `contains` and never `holds the code`
- **AND** its list item reads the two nodes' labels, `: contains` and `Witnessed by Syzygy`, with evidence text naming `_tree-contains-1`, rule `top-level-unit` version 1, the pinned revision and the record's digest; both nodes keep the draft's marking; the figure keeps `data-visual-provenance="curated"`, its legend lists the solid style and its tally counts the arrow observed
- **AND** `machine.json` carries `e1` with `relation` `contains` and `witness` `{"state": "witnessed", "reason": null, "fact": "_tree-contains-1", "rule": "top-level-unit", "ruleVersion": 1}`
- **AND** `e1` carries no claim anchor and is in none of the draft's counts on its page or in the appendix, which equal those of the same draft without `e1`, while the fidelity verdict's entry for `e1` is kept as for any edge

#### Scenario: A fact of another relation type never witnesses

- **WHEN** that edge `e1` carries the relation `relates-to`, `calls` or `imports` in place of `contains`; and another figure of the draft has a node `pkg` whose unit is `{"kind": "package-manifest", "path": "web/package.json"}`, a node `lp` whose unit is `{"kind": "dependency-name", "name": "left-pad"}`, that manifest declaring `left-pad` in `dependencies`, and Inferred edges `e2`, relation `calls`, and `e3`, relation `declares-dependency`, each from `pkg` to `lp`
- **THEN** no edge is drawn solid or labelled Observed and check reports no finding for any of them; `e1` carrying `relates-to` or `calls`, and `e2`, are `unknown` with `no-witness-rule`, `e1` carrying `imports` is `unknown` with `endpoint-kind-mismatch` (version 2.44), and `e3` is `unknown` with `recognizer-not-eligible`, though a `dependency` fact joins its two units
- **AND** a `contains` edge from a `repository-root` node to a `package-manifest` node is `unknown` with `endpoint-kind-mismatch`, and a `contains` edge from `repo` to a node with no `unit` is `unknown` with `endpoint-without-unit`

#### Scenario: Complete coverage with no witnessing fact

- **WHEN** a schema-v9 Inferred edge with relation `contains` runs from `repo` to a node whose unit is `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "docs"}`, over the same pinned tree, which holds no `docs` directory, no path a secret detector matches and no top-level directory past the listing of 250
- **THEN** check reports a `relation-challenged` repair finding at `$.diagrams[<i>].edges[<j>]` whose detail names the edge's identity, `contains`, the fact kind `contains` and rule `top-level-unit` version 1, and does not name `docs`
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Partial coverage is Unknown, never a finding

- **WHEN** that `contains` edge to `docs` is checked over a pinned tree that also holds a path a secret detector matches, again over one of 251 top-level directories none of which is `docs`, again over one whose `docs` is a submodule (mode 160000), and again over one whose `docs` directory holds only a submodule
- **THEN** check reports no finding for the edge, its state is `unknown` with `coverage-partial`, and it renders with the draft's marking
- **AND** a `contains` edge from `repo` to `src` over the first of those trees is still `witnessed`, its fact being found

#### Scenario: Relation types and unit forms outside the lists

- **WHEN** a schema-v9 graph edge carries no `relation`, a second carries `relation` `invokes`, a node's unit is `{"kind": "module", "path": "src"}` and a `top-level-directory` unit carries a member `name`; and a schema-v8 draft's graph edge carries `relation` `contains` and one of its nodes a `unit`
- **THEN** the v9 draft has a `schema` finding at each of those edges and units, and the v8 draft a `field-version` finding at its edge's `relation` and at its node's `unit`, in place of the `schema` findings version 2.16 reports there, beside every other finding version 2.16 reports for it
- **AND** a sequence message carrying `relation`, in any schema, is a `schema` finding

#### Scenario: A unit that names no unit of its kind

- **WHEN** one schema-v9 node's unit is `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "src/lib"}`, a second's `{"kind": "package-manifest", "path": "lib/package.json"}` where the pinned tree holds no such blob, a third's `{"kind": "package-manifest", "path": "README.md"}`, and a fourth's `{"kind": "workflow-job", "path": "ci.yml", "job": "build"}`
- **THEN** each is a `unit-reference` repair finding at its `$.diagrams[<i>].nodes[<j>].unit`, naming the node's identity and not the path
- **AND** a `top-level-directory` unit naming a directory the tree does not hold is no `unit-reference` finding, and a `contains` edge from `repo` to the first node is `unknown` with `endpoint-unit-invalid` and raises no `relation-challenged` finding
- **AND** a `package-manifest` unit whose path a secret detector matches raises no `unit-reference` finding whether or not the pinned tree holds it

#### Scenario: An arrow the draft does not assert is never upgraded or challenged

- **WHEN** the edge `e1` that the first scenario witnesses, and the `contains` edge to `docs` that the third challenges, are each labelled Unknown with a reason, and again each cite only spans that screening withholds
- **THEN** neither is drawn solid, neither raises a finding, and both are `unknown` with `edge-not-inferred`

#### Scenario: A witness needs a fact that renders Observed

- **WHEN** the first scenario's draft is rendered where the currency policy's bytes are missing or of another digest
- **THEN** `_tree-contains-1` renders Unknown with `no-currency-bound-declared`, `e1` keeps the draft's marking and is not drawn solid, and `machine.json` gives it `unknown` with `fact-not-current`
- **AND** check's outcome for `e1` is unchanged, check reading no currency policy for this rule

#### Scenario: Earlier schemas and the provider keep their bytes

- **WHEN** stored drafts of schema v2 to v8 that carry no `relation` and no `unit` are checked and rendered, and a provider-mode run is rendered
- **THEN** their findings and the bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes equal those version 2.16 produces for them
- **AND** the provider golden passes unchanged

#### Scenario: Brief v15 states relation types

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v15`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v9`, and equals brief v14 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one relation-types paragraph placed immediately after the subsections paragraph

#### Scenario: Hero figure leads the overview after the thesis

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft with no `unresolved` items, whose `hero` is `{"figure": "system"}`, names a produced `boundary` diagram `system` homed on the overview, and a second diagram is homed on the overview after it in draft order
- **THEN** check reports no `hero` finding, and on `index.html` the region of overview figures immediately follows the header, which ends with the introduction, and its first element is the `system` figure carrying `data-hero="figure"`, and the second figure follows it
- **AND** the same draft with one `unresolved` item renders that open question in the header after the introduction, and the region with the `system` figure first immediately after that header
- **AND** the `system` figure renders on no other page, and `machine.json` carries `hero` as `{"form": "figure", "diagram": "system", "rendered": true}`

#### Scenario: Draft with neither hero nor omission refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft carries no `hero`
- **THEN** check reports one repair finding of kind `hero` at `$.hero` with the detail `a v10 draft names its hero figure or declares it omitted with a reason`, and no `schema` finding at `$.hero`
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Declared omission renders its reason

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft's `hero` is `{"omitted": <an Inferred claim block with one citation>}`
- **THEN** check reports no `hero` finding, the block is checked as a claim block and has its own entry in the fidelity verdict
- **AND** on `index.html` the first element of the region of overview figures is `<div data-hero="omitted">` and holds the line `The authoring session left out the overview figure, for the reason that follows.` followed by the block, rendered as the introduction's block is, and `machine.json` carries `hero` as `{"form": "omitted", "diagram": null, "rendered": true}`

#### Scenario: Hero names a figure that cannot lead the overview

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft's `hero` names a graph diagram homed in a section, or a `sequence` diagram homed on the overview
- **THEN** check reports a finding of kind `hero` at `$.hero.figure` with the detail `<identity>: the hero is a figure homed on the overview` for the first and `<identity>: the hero is a figure of a graph kind` for the second, and the draft does not proceed to review
- **AND** a `sequence` diagram homed in a section raises both findings, the home finding first, and a `figure` that is not a string raises one with the detail `` `hero.figure` names a diagram by its identity ``

#### Scenario: Hero names no diagram

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft's `hero` is `{"figure": "missing"}` and no diagram of the draft has the identity `missing`
- **THEN** check reports a finding of kind `unresolved-reference` at `$.hero.figure` with the detail `` no diagram has the identity named by `hero.figure` ``

#### Scenario: Malformed hero refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft's `hero` carries both `figure` and `omitted`, or neither, or a further member, or is a string
- **THEN** check reports a finding of kind `hero` at `$.hero` with the detail `` `hero` is exactly one of {"figure": <diagram id>} and {"omitted": <claim block>} `` in place of any `schema` finding at that path

#### Scenario: Omission that makes no claim refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft's `hero` is `{"omitted": <a non-normative block>}`
- **THEN** check reports a finding of kind `hero` at `$.hero.omitted` with the detail `an omitted hero's reason is an Inferred or Unknown claim block`, and no `schema` finding at or below that path
- **AND** an omission whose value is a presentation element gets a `schema` finding at `$.hero.omitted` and no `hero` finding

#### Scenario: Hero not produced, or draft layer withheld

- **WHEN** a schema-v10 draft's named hero has the disposition `omitted`, or the same draft renders with its draft layer withheld
- **THEN** in the first case check reports no `hero` finding, the notice a schema-v4 diagram renders in its place stands first in the region of overview figures carrying `data-hero="figure"`, and `machine.json`'s `hero` is `{"form": "figure", "diagram": <the identity>, "rendered": true}`
- **AND** in the second case no hero, notice or omission renders, the landing page is the one version 2.19 renders for a withheld draft layer, and `machine.json`'s `hero` is `{"form": "figure", "diagram": null, "rendered": false}`

#### Scenario: Earlier schema keeps its bytes

- **WHEN** a draft of schema v9 or earlier is checked and rendered, once as stored and once with a `hero` member added
- **THEN** the stored draft's findings and the bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes are those version 2.19 gives it, with no `data-hero` and no `hero` member
- **AND** the draft with `hero` added gets a `field-version` finding at `$.hero` with the detail `` `hero` needs draft schema polaris-dossier-local-draft-v10 `` in place of the `schema` finding at that path, and every other finding is unchanged

#### Scenario: Brief v16 asks for the hero

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v16`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v10`, and equals brief v15 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one hero paragraph placed immediately after the relation-types paragraph

#### Scenario: The run's own pages fold under About this run

- **WHEN** an operator-agent dossier with two Poles holding pages, no deep dive and an understanding record is rendered
- **THEN** every page's navigation lists, in this order, the overview, `find-by-task.html`, `name-index.html`, the two Pole pages, the contents page, the glossary and the sources, and ends with one `<details class="about-this-run">` whose summary reads "About this run" and which links `understanding.html`, `size-report.html` and `appendix.html` in that order, open on those three pages and closed on every other
- **AND** the contents page links no run page before `<h2 id="about-this-run">About this run</h2>` and links each run page after it, and every internal link of every page names a page of the render and, where it names a fragment, an element with that `id` on that page

#### Scenario: The landing page leads with the subject

- **WHEN** an operator-agent dossier with Poles and a figure homed on the overview is rendered
- **THEN** the main content of `index.html` holds its header, then the region of overview figures, then the Pole map, then the landing page's sections, then the list of sections not generated where there is one, then the `derived-structure` region
- **AND** for the same draft with no figure homed on the overview, the Pole map immediately follows the header

#### Scenario: The index is read third

- **WHEN** an operator-agent dossier is rendered
- **THEN** the `pages` lists of `dossier.json` and `machine.json` begin `index.html`, `find-by-task.html`, `name-index.html`, every page's navigation and the contents page link the index directly after find-by-task, and the appendix is the last page of the reading order

#### Scenario: A disclosure stays on its claim's page

- **WHEN** an operator-agent dossier with an Unknown claim on the landing page and a withheld item on a Pole page is rendered
- **THEN** each claim occurs once, on the page `machine.json` names for it, and its own element there carries its label, and the `[Unknown]` marking and its reason in its text or, for the withheld item, Syzygy's withheld words
- **AND** moving either disclosure out of that element or onto a run page is reported

#### Scenario: Provider mode keeps its navigation

- **WHEN** a provider-mode dossier is rendered
- **THEN** its pages carry no `about-this-run` entry or heading, and their bytes equal those the provider golden pins

#### Scenario: Three-level containment laid out as boxes within boxes

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 draft's produced containment `parts` has two top-level groups, `a` with the children `a1` and `a2` in that order, `a1` having one child `a11` and `a2` none, and `b` with no children
- **THEN** check reports no finding for it, and its drawing is 408 px wide and 256 px high: `a` at x 24, y 24, 176 by 208 px; `a1` at x 36, y 64, 152 by 100 px; `a11` at x 48, y 104, 128 by 48 px; `a2` at x 36, y 172, 152 by 48 px; and `b` at x 208, y 24, 176 by 48 px
- **AND** the drawing holds no line, arrow or connector between boxes, the figure carries `data-figure-kind="containment"` and `data-visual-provenance="curated"`, and its tally reads `5 groups in 3 levels, as marked: 0 observed, 5 inferred, 0 unknown.` where every group is Inferred

#### Scenario: Containment fits at both widths

- **WHEN** that figure renders and figure fit examines it at 1440 and at 390
- **THEN** at 1440 the stylesheet displays the drawing and at 390 the phone form, a `<ul class="figure-outline">` whose items nest as the groups do, each a whole label with no identity, citation, anchor or label token
- **AND** figure fit lists no entry for it at either viewport, the page carries no script; a page whose only figure with two forms is this containment carries the container query rules and the outline's rules and none of the stepper's, and a page whose only such figure is a swimlane carries the container query rules and the stepper's and none of the outline's

#### Scenario: Containment deeper than three levels refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 containment `parts` has a group at level 4
- **THEN** check reports a finding of kind `containment-depth` at that group's path with the detail `parts: a containment nests at most 3 levels; this group is at level 4`, no `containment-depth` or `unit-reference` finding for any group below it, no `unit-reference` finding for the level-4 group, no `relation-challenged` finding for the containment, and no `schema` finding for its depth, and the draft does not proceed to review

#### Scenario: Containment over its size or with no nesting refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 containment `parts` holds 41 groups across its levels, and another, `flat`, holds three top-level groups and no children
- **THEN** check reports a `containment-size` finding at the first's `groups` with the detail `parts: a containment holds at most 40 groups; this one holds 41`, and a `containment-flat` finding at the second's `groups` with the detail `flat: a containment nests at least one group in another`

#### Scenario: Containment with members of another kind

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 containment carries `edges`, or a graph-kind diagram carries `groups`
- **THEN** check reports a `figure-kind` finding at that member's path naming it, and no `schema` finding at that path

#### Scenario: Nesting witnessed by a contains fact

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 containment nests a group with the unit `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "src"}` in a group with the unit `{"kind": "repository-root"}`, both Inferred, and the structure record holds the `contains` fact joining the root to `src`, rendering Observed
- **THEN** check reports no finding for the nesting, and the figure's list holds the item `<root label> contains <src label>: Witnessed by Syzygy` carrying `data-epistemic="observed"`, `data-relation="contains"` and the fact's `data-witness-*` attributes, with no claim anchor
- **AND** both boxes keep the draft's marking and line style, the tally ends with `1 nesting witnessed by Syzygy.`, and `machine.json`'s entry for the nesting carries `witness` with state `witnessed` and that fact's identity

#### Scenario: Nesting challenged where coverage is complete

- **WHEN** the same nesting, in containment `parts` with the child group `src`, names a top-level directory the tree does not hold, and the structure record is derived with no secret path left out, no submodule and no unit not listed
- **THEN** check reports a `relation-challenged` finding at the child group's path with the detail `parts: no contains fact joins the units of group src and the group it is drawn in, in what rule top-level-unit version 1 derived at the pinned revision; correct the nesting or its groups' units`, naming no unit path

#### Scenario: Nesting of other units is Unknown, never a finding

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 containment nests a group with a `package-manifest` unit in a group with a `top-level-directory` unit, and nests a group with no unit in another
- **THEN** the first nesting is `unknown` (`endpoint-kind-mismatch`) and the second `unknown` (`endpoint-without-unit`), check reports no finding and no advice for either, and both boxes render with the draft's marking

#### Scenario: Earlier schema keeps its bytes

- **WHEN** a draft of schema v10 or earlier is checked and rendered, once as stored and once with a diagram of kind `containment` carrying `groups`
- **THEN** the stored draft's findings and the bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes are those version 2.22 gives it
- **AND** the draft with the containment gets `field-version` findings at that diagram's `kind` and `groups` in place of the `schema` findings at those paths, and every other finding is unchanged

#### Scenario: Brief v17 states the containment kind

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v17`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v11`, and equals brief v16 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one containment paragraph placed immediately after the hero paragraph

#### Scenario: Two views share one element

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 draft's model holds the element `api` and the views of the graph `runtime`, homed on the overview, and the graph `deploy`, homed in a section rendered on `poles/1.html`, both select it
- **THEN** both figures draw `api` with the same label, marking and line style, each box carrying `data-element="api"`, and its claim anchor and citations render once, in `runtime`'s list, and its fidelity entry is one
- **AND** `deploy`'s list item for it reads `<api label>, as shown in` followed by a link to `../index.html#figure-runtime` titled as `runtime` is, carrying no claim anchor, and `machine.json`'s `model` entry for `api` carries `"views": ["runtime", "deploy"]`

#### Scenario: Migration renders the same figures

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 draft that check passes, holding one graph diagram whose nodes carry a `top-level-directory` unit and no unit, none of them rendering as a withheld item, and one containment of three levels, is migrated
- **THEN** the migrated draft is schema v12, its model holds the graph's nodes as elements of types `directory` and `module`, then its edges as cited relationships, then the containment's groups in preorder with each child's `parent`, and each diagram carries a `view` listing them in order
- **AND** check passes the migrated draft with the original's advisories, each path moved to the model or view member, and every `<figure>` element, the figure-fit member, the reading ledger and the `machine.json` figure entries rendered from it are byte-identical to the original's

#### Scenario: View naming a missing element refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 graph view `parts` lists `cache`, which no element of the model is
- **THEN** check reports an `unresolved-reference` finding at `$.diagrams[<i>].view.elements[<k>]` for that entry, and the draft does not proceed to review

#### Scenario: View showing a relationship without its ends refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 graph view `parts` selects the relationship `api-db` but not the element `db` it ends at
- **THEN** check reports a `view-relationship` finding at that relationship's entry with the detail `parts: a view shows a relationship only where it shows both its ends`

#### Scenario: Element parents that loop refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 model's element `a` names `b` as its parent, `b` names `a`, and `c`, on no cycle, names `a`
- **THEN** check reports an `element-parent` finding at each one's `parent`, `a`'s first with the detail `a: an element is part of at most two elements above it and never of itself`, `c`'s because its chain of parents reaches `a` again

#### Scenario: Element type disagreeing with its unit refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 model's element `web` is of type `service` and carries the unit `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "web"}`
- **THEN** check reports an `element-type` finding at its `type` with the detail `web: an element with a top-level-directory unit is of type directory`, naming no path

#### Scenario: Derived relationship stated by Syzygy

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 model holds the elements `repo` with the unit `{"kind": "repository-root"}` and `src` with the unit `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "src"}`, and the derived relationship `repo-src` from `repo` to `src` by `contains`, which a graph view selects, and the structure record holds the `contains` fact joining them, rendering Observed
- **THEN** the arrow is drawn solid and Observed in Syzygy's words, with the fact's provenance and no claim anchor, and both boxes keep the draft's marking
- **AND** where the tree holds no `src` and coverage is complete, check reports a `relation-challenged` finding at `$.model.relationships[<j>]`, and where coverage is partial the arrow renders Unknown with the item `<repo label> → <src label>: Syzygy could not derive this relation (coverage-partial)`

#### Scenario: Derived relationship with nothing to derive it refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 model holds a derived relationship `pkg-dir` from an element with a `package-manifest` unit to one with a `top-level-directory` unit
- **THEN** check reports a `relationship-not-derivable` finding at `$.model.relationships[<j>]` with the detail `pkg-dir: a derived relationship needs two ends whose units a row of rule relation-witness version 1 joins by its relation`

#### Scenario: Element no view shows refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 model holds the element `cli`, which no diagram's view selects
- **THEN** check reports a `model-unviewed` finding at its path with the detail `cli: every element and relationship of the model is shown by at least one view`

#### Scenario: Schema v11 and earlier keep their bytes

- **WHEN** a draft of schema v11 or earlier is checked and rendered, once as stored and once carrying `model` and a diagram carrying `view`
- **THEN** the stored draft's findings and the bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes are those version 2.23 gives it
- **AND** the draft with the members gets `field-version` findings at `$.model` and at that diagram's `view` in place of the `schema` findings at those paths, and every other finding is unchanged

#### Scenario: Derived relationship Syzygy cannot derive drawn Unknown

- **WHEN** a produced graph view of a schema-v12 draft selects the derived relationship `repo-src` from `repo`, with a `repository-root` unit, to `src`, with a `top-level-directory` unit, by `contains`, and the structure record's coverage is partial
- **THEN** its arrow is drawn as an Unknown edge labelled `contains` is, carrying `data-epistemic="unknown"` and `data-relation="contains"`, and its declarative-source line is that edge's
- **AND** its `machine.json` figure entry is labelled `unknown`, its text `contains`, with `"basis": "derived"` and no citation, and it carries no claim anchor, takes no fidelity entry, counts as unknown in the figure's tally and enters none of the draft's own counts

#### Scenario: Findings beside a schema finding not computed

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 containment view selects one element, and the model's element `w` carries the type `widget` and a `top-level-directory` unit
- **THEN** check reports a `schema` finding at that view's `elements` and at `w`'s `type`
- **AND** it reports no `containment-flat` or `view-shape` finding for that view and no `element-type` finding for `w`

#### Scenario: Shared element in a later containment and a witnessed relationship

- **WHEN** the produced graph `runtime` on `index.html` selects the element `api`, whose parent is `svc`, and the cited relationship `repo-src` with both its ends, which Syzygy finds witnessed, and the later produced containment `layout` on `index.html` selects `svc` and `api`, and the later graph `tree` selects `repo-src` with both its ends
- **THEN** `layout`'s list item for `api` reads `<api label> (in <svc label>), as shown in <a href="#figure-runtime"><runtime title></a>`, each label as `runtime` renders it
- **AND** both `runtime` and `tree` render `repo-src` with the arrow and `Witnessed by Syzygy` list item version 2.19 gives a witnessed edge, and neither renders the draft's label for it

#### Scenario: Brief v18 states the system model

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v18`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v12`, and equals brief v17 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one system-model paragraph placed immediately after the containment paragraph

#### Scenario: A worth-knowing block leads its page

- **WHEN** a v13 draft whose second Pole holds a section with a cited Inferred paragraph carrying `worthKnowing: true`, which the counted fidelity review finds supported, is rendered
- **THEN** that Pole's page holds, before its on-page contents list and its first section, the callout opening `<aside class="callout callout-important worth-knowing" id="worth-knowing" aria-label="Worth knowing"><p class="callout-title"><span class="callout-tone">Worth knowing</span> What a newcomer would not guess</p>` with that block once, with its label and citations, followed by a link to `#section-<id>` of its section
- **AND** the block is not rendered again in its section, `machine.json` names that page for it, and a sibling paragraph carrying `worthKnowing: false` renders in its section as before

#### Scenario: A marked block that does not render Inferred stays in its section

- **WHEN** a v13 draft is rendered whose section marks four paragraphs worth knowing: one the counted review finds supported, one it finds unsupported, one citing only a withheld file and one whose text a secret detector matches
- **THEN** the callout holds the supported block alone, as one `worth-knowing-item`
- **AND** the other three render once each, in their section, as Unknown, as they would unmarked; and rendered with no counted review, the same draft renders no `worth-knowing-item`

#### Scenario: A Pole with none found

- **WHEN** a v13 draft one of whose Poles marks no block and carries `worthKnowingNone` is rendered
- **THEN** that Pole's page carries the none-found callout `<aside class="callout callout-note worth-knowing" id="worth-knowing" data-worth-knowing="none" aria-label="Worth knowing"><p class="callout-title"><span class="callout-tone">Worth knowing</span> None found</p><p>The authoring session reports that no fact in this part meets the test of what a newcomer would not guess (Inferred, self-reported).</p></aside>`
- **AND** the text of `worthKnowingNone` appears on no page and in no machine form

#### Scenario: A misplaced, uncited or judged mark refused

- **WHEN** a v13 draft is checked in which `worthKnowing: true` is carried by an Inferred child of a paragraph, by an Inferred section paragraph with no citation, and by an Inferred section paragraph carrying the judgement mark
- **THEN** check reports a `worth-knowing` finding at each block's `worthKnowing`, naming the block, with the details `` only a block that is one of a section's own paragraphs carries the worth-knowing mark: not a child, an element's claim, a subsection, a deep dive, the introduction or a route step ``, `` a worth-knowing block states a fact about the project and cites at least one source `` and `` a worth-knowing block states a fact about the project, never your own judgement: drop one of the two marks `` respectively
- **AND** the same member on an Unknown block or a presentation element is a `schema` finding

#### Scenario: Count advice without a quota

- **WHEN** a v13 draft is checked whose first Pole marks no block and gives no reason, whose second marks four, whose third marks none and gives a reason and whose fourth marks three
- **THEN** check's `readerAdvice.worthKnowing` holds exactly two advisories, at `$.poles[0]` with `marked` 0 and at `$.poles[1]` with `marked` 4, each with its literal detail
- **AND** neither is a finding, and the check passes or fails as it would without the marks

#### Scenario: A pre-v13 draft keeps its bytes

- **WHEN** a draft of schema v12 or earlier is checked and rendered, once as stored and once with `worthKnowing` added to a block and `worthKnowingNone` to a Pole
- **THEN** the stored draft's findings and the bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes are those version 2.24 gives it, with no `worth-knowing` callout and no `worthKnowing` advice
- **AND** the draft with the members added gets a `field-version` finding at each with the detail naming `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v13`, in place of the `schema` finding at that path

#### Scenario: Brief v19 and inventory brief v4 ask for the facts

- **WHEN** a run is briefed and its inventory session is briefed under version 2.26
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v19`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v13`, and equals brief v16 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one worth-knowing paragraph placed immediately before `## Task routes`
- **AND** the inventory brief is `polaris-dossier-inventory-brief-v4`, issuing inventory schema `polaris-dossier-local-inventory-v2`, whose entry kinds include `worth-knowing` with its gloss, and an inventory in schema v1 is still accepted, with a `schema` finding at the `kind` of any entry of kind `worth-knowing`

#### Scenario: Brief v20 teaches reader-spelled terminology IDs

- **WHEN** Syzygy issues a brief under version 2.29 from a controlled operator-agent run and the recorded fixture pin
- **THEN** `brief.md` names `polaris-dossier-brief-v20` and contains the exact terminology paragraph after the existing understanding-record paragraph, while the rest of `brief.md` is the predecessor brief for the same run except for the version line
- **AND** the success report and `brief.json` name brief v20 and schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v13`, and the record's file digests match the issued files


#### Scenario: Unit with no change block advised

- **WHEN** a v13 draft whose introduction cites a file in `src/` declares it `purpose`, and declares `structure` and `mechanism` for a paragraph citing `src/` and `rationale` and `limits` for one citing `docs/`, and the pinned tree's major units are `.`, `src` and `docs`
- **THEN** check passes and its report's `questionKindAdvice` is compared, with `missing` `["change"]` for `.`, `["rationale", "change", "limits"]` for `src` and `["purpose", "structure", "mechanism", "change"]` for `docs`
- **AND** neither the check record nor the step log carries `questionKindAdvice`

#### Scenario: Unit answering all six kinds is not advised

- **WHEN** the counted blocks citing files in one major directory together declare all six kinds
- **THEN** that unit's `missing` is empty and its `answered` counts every kind at least once

#### Scenario: Nothing declared is Unknown

- **WHEN** a v13 draft carries no `questionKinds`, or only objects naming no kind, or the pinned tree cannot be listed
- **THEN** the advice is `{"state": "unknown", "why", "note"}` with no unit and no count, and the check's outcome is what it would be without the advice

#### Scenario: Declarations in an earlier schema

- **WHEN** a draft of schema v10 carries `questionKinds`
- **THEN** check reports one `field-version` finding at `$.questionKinds` naming `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v13`, in place of the `schema` finding there, and the advice is Unknown
- **AND** a v13 draft whose object repeats a kind gets a `schema` finding at that object's `kinds`, and one naming a kind outside the six a `schema` finding at that kind

#### Scenario: Brief v21 teaches the six kinds

- **WHEN** Syzygy issues a brief under version 2.32
- **THEN** `brief.md` names `polaris-dossier-brief-v21` and schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v13` with the issued schema file's digest, and holds the exact question-kinds paragraph immediately after the worth-knowing paragraph, the rest being brief v20 for the same run
- **AND** no rendered page of any draft changes, and the provider golden's bytes are unchanged

#### Scenario: A figure plan names each place's question and figure

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft with one section `write-path` and one deep dive `replication` carries a `figurePlan` of three entries: `{"home": {"kind": "overview"}, "question": "What are the main parts and what surrounds them?", "kind": "boundary", "figure": "system"}`, where `system` is a `boundary` diagram homed on the overview; a planned figure for `{"kind": "section", "target": "write-path"}` of kind `sequence` naming a `sequence` diagram homed in that section; and `{"home": {"kind": "deep-dive", "target": "replication"}, "omitted": "Two steps read better as one sentence."}`
- **THEN** check reports no `figure-plan`, `unresolved-reference` or `schema` finding for the plan, and the draft proceeds to review
- **AND** no page, `machine.json`, `dossier.json` or size report carries the text of a `question` or an `omitted`, and every page's bytes equal those Syzygy renders for the same draft without `figurePlan`, apart from any byte that names the draft's schema or digest

#### Scenario: Draft without a figure plan refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft carries no `figurePlan`, and again with a `figurePlan` that is an object
- **THEN** check reports one finding of kind `figure-plan` at `$.figurePlan`, with the detail ``a v14 draft plans its figures: one `figurePlan` entry for the overview, each section and each deep dive`` for the first and `` `figurePlan` is a list of plan entries `` for the second, and no `schema` finding at that path
- **AND** the draft does not proceed to review until a resubmitted revision passes

#### Scenario: Every place is planned once

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft with sections `a` and `b` and no deep dive carries well-formed plan entries for the overview, for section `a` and again for section `a`, and none for section `b`
- **THEN** check reports a `figure-plan` finding at `$.figurePlan[2].home` with the detail `section a: a second plan entry for this place`, then one at `$.figurePlan` with the detail `section b: no plan entry for this place`
- **AND** where the third entry's `home` is instead `{"kind": "section", "target": "c"}` and the draft has no section `c`, check reports a finding of kind `unresolved-reference` at `$.figurePlan[2].home.target` with the detail ``no section or deep dive has the identity named by `figurePlan[2].home.target` `` and still reports section `b` unplanned; a fourth entry with that same unresolved `home` gets its own `unresolved-reference` finding and no `a second plan entry` finding; a planned figure in such an entry, naming a diagram of its kind homed in a section, gets no home finding; and a `target` that is not a string, such as `7`, gets the malformed-entry finding at `$.figurePlan[<k>]` and no `unresolved-reference` finding

#### Scenario: Malformed plan entry refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 plan entry carries both `figure` and `omitted`, or neither, or a further member, or a `kind` of `pie`, or an empty `question`, one of 301 Unicode code points or one of white space alone, or a `home` of kind `pole`, or is a string
- **THEN** check reports for that entry one finding of kind `figure-plan` at `$.figurePlan[<k>]` with the detail `a plan entry is {"home", "question", "kind", "figure"} or {"home", "omitted"}`, no other plan finding for it, and no `schema` finding at or below `$.figurePlan`
- **AND** the place that entry was meant for is reported unplanned where no other entry names it, and an entry whose `kind` is `containment` is well formed

#### Scenario: A planned figure is the one drawn at its place

- **WHEN** the fourth of a schema-v14 draft's plan entries plans a figure `missing` that no diagram of the draft is, and, in turn, a `flow` diagram `f` homed in another section under the kind `flow`, a `sequence` diagram `s` homed at the entry's place under the kind `swimlane`, and a `sequence` diagram `s2` homed in another section under the kind `flow`
- **THEN** check reports a finding of kind `unresolved-reference` at `$.figurePlan[3].figure` with the detail ``no diagram has the identity named by `figurePlan[3].figure` `` for the first, and, at `$.figurePlan[3].figure`, the finding of kind `figure-plan` with the detail `f: a planned figure is homed at its plan entry's place` for the second, `s: a planned figure is of the kind its plan entry names` for the third, and both, the home finding first, for the fourth
- **AND** a planned figure whose diagram is homed at the entry's place and of its kind, with the disposition `omitted`, raises no finding

#### Scenario: Earlier schema keeps its bytes with a figure plan added

- **WHEN** a draft of schema v13 or earlier is checked and rendered, once as stored and once with a `figurePlan` member added
- **THEN** the stored draft's findings and the bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes are those version 2.32 gives it, and its check's report carries no `figureAdvice`
- **AND** the draft with `figurePlan` added gets a `field-version` finding at `$.figurePlan` with the detail `` `figurePlan` needs draft schema polaris-dossier-local-draft-v14 `` in place of the `schema` finding at that path, every other finding is unchanged, and a provider-mode render's bytes equal those the provider golden pins

#### Scenario: A chain of three or fewer is advised as a sentence

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft whose check reports no `schema` finding holds, all produced and in this order, a `flow` whose view shows three elements chained by two relationships, a `boundary` whose view shows three elements and two relationships from one of them, a `flow` whose view shows four elements chained by three relationships, a `dependency` whose view shows three elements and three relationships, a `swimlane` of three steps, a `swimlane` of four steps, a `sequence` of two messages, a `sequence` of three messages, a `containment` whose view shows three elements, each the `parent` of the next, and a `containment` whose view shows four elements
