- **THEN** the report's `figureAdvice` has `examined` 10 and holds a `figure-information-floor` advisory for the first, second, fifth, seventh and ninth figures alone, each at `$.diagrams[<i>]` with the detail `<identity>: a chain of three or fewer is a sentence; say it in the prose, or draw what a sentence cannot hold`
- **AND** an eleventh diagram, a `flow` whose view shows three elements and two relationships and whose disposition is `omitted`, gets no advisory and leaves `examined` at 10

#### Scenario: A figure that draws nothing new is advised as a duplicate

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft holds, all produced and in this order, a `flow` `a` whose view shows five elements chained by four relationships; a `dependency` `b` whose view shows five other elements, whose labels differ from those of `a`'s only in letter case and white space, joined as `a`'s are by relationships with labels of their own; a `flow` `c` whose view shows four of `a`'s elements and three of its relationships; a `flow` `d` whose view shows all of `a`'s and one more element; a `flow` `e` whose view shows `a`'s elements with one relationship replaced by one in the opposite direction; a `sequence` whose participants and messages carry `a`'s element labels and relationships' ends; a `containment` `k1` whose view shows an element `Workspace` and elements `api` and `web` whose `parent` it is; a `containment` `k2` whose view shows an element labelled `workspace` and an element labelled `API` whose `parent` it is; two sequences `q1` and `q2` of the same two messages in opposite orders; and a `flow` `g` whose view selects exactly the elements and relationships `d`'s does
- **THEN** the report's `figureAdvice` holds, under rule `figure-duplicate`, an advisory for `a` with the detail `a: everything it draws is drawn by d; keep one figure, or draw what the other does not`, one for `b` naming `a`, one for `c` naming `a`, one for `k2` naming `k1`, one for `q2` naming `q1` and one for `g` naming `d`
- **AND** it holds none under that rule for `d`, `e`, the first sequence, `k1` or `q1`

#### Scenario: A figure that mixes levels is advised

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft's model holds, among others, the elements `root` with the unit `{"kind": "repository-root"}`; `src`, whose `parent` is `root`, with the unit `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "src"}`; `lib`, with no `parent`, with the unit `{"kind": "top-level-directory", "path": "lib"}`; `app`, whose `parent` is `lib`, without `unit`; `pkgs` and `pack` with no `parent`, with the units of the top-level directories `packages` and `pack`; `core`, with no `parent`, with the unit `{"kind": "package-manifest", "path": "packages/core/package.json"}`; `svc`, of type `service`, and `mod`, of type `module` and whose `parent` is `svc`, both without `unit`; and `x` and `y`, with no `parent` and without `unit`; and it holds produced figures whose views show: `f1`, a graph of `lib` and the repository root `root`, joined by one relationship of `relation` `calls`; `f2`, the same with a relationship of `relation` `contains` from `root` to `lib`; `f3`, a graph of `pkgs` and `core` with no relationship between them; `f4`, as `f3` with `pack` in place of `pkgs`; `f5`, a graph of `x` and `y`; `f6`, a containment of `root` and `src`, whose expansion draws `src` inside `root`; `f7`, a containment of `root`, `lib` and `app`, whose expansion draws `root` and `lib` side by side as top-level groups, `lib` holding `app`; `f8`, a graph of `root`, `pkgs` and `core`, with relationships of `relation` `contains` from `root` to `pkgs` and from `pkgs` to `core` and none from `root` to `core`; `f9`, a graph of `svc` and `mod` joined by one relationship of `relation` `calls`; `f10`, as `f9` with a relationship of `relation` `contains` from `svc` to `mod`; and `f11`, a containment of `root` and `app` and no other element
- **THEN** the report's `figureAdvice` holds a `figure-mixed-levels` advisory for `f1` with the detail `f1: root and lib stand for a part and a part within it, and the figure does not show that one holds the other; draw one level, or show the containment`, and one each for `f3`, `f7` and `f9`, the one for `f9` naming `svc` and `mod`
- **AND** it holds none under that rule for `f2`, `f4`, `f5`, `f6`, `f8`, `f10` or `f11`, `app` being of no unit and its parent `lib` not shown

#### Scenario: Figure advice gates nothing

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft that otherwise passes check holds figures that each of the three rules advises on
- **THEN** check passes, the draft proceeds to review, and the advisories are on the report's `figureAdvice` alone: the check record, `run-advice.json`, the reading ledger, the reading difference and every page carry and count none of them
- **AND** the report of a check of a v14 draft with a `schema` finding, of a v13 draft and of an inventory carries no `figureAdvice` member

#### Scenario: Schema v14 joins the system model to the worth-knowing mark

- **WHEN** a schema-v14 draft carries a `model`, a graph diagram and a containment diagram each drawn from a `view`, a section's own paragraph labelled Inferred with one citation and `worthKnowing: true`, a `questionKinds` object naming that paragraph with the kind `mechanism`, and a well-formed `figurePlan` planning both diagrams
- **THEN** check reports no `schema`, `field-version` or `worth-knowing` finding, its report carries `figureAdvice`, the `worthKnowing` member of `readerAdvice` and `questionKindAdvice`, each view renders as its expansion, and the marked paragraph renders in the page's worth-knowing callout where it is lifted
- **AND** `questionKindAdvice` counts that paragraph and no diagram or plan entry; the same members in a schema-v13 draft give `schema` findings at the `model` and the views and a `field-version` finding at `$.figurePlan`; and in a schema-v12 draft they give the `field-version` findings at `worthKnowing`, at `$.questionKinds` and at `$.figurePlan`

#### Scenario: Brief v22 asks for the figure plan

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v22`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v14`, and equals brief v21 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line, brief v17's containment paragraph placed immediately after the hero paragraph, brief v18's system-model paragraph immediately after that, and the one figure-plan paragraph immediately after that, and the success report and the brief record name brief v22

#### Scenario: Pruned view prints its chip

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 graph view homed on the overview starts from the unit map, whose member at the render lists 31 packages and modules, has a `factsNotListed` of 0 and recognizer entries whose `unknown` and `secretPathsLeftOut` are all 0, and the view hides 22 of them under one `hide` entry with the reason `Test fixtures and examples`
- **THEN** the figure draws the 9 others, each labelled with its declared name, Inferred, with a double border, carrying its `data-manifest-id` and `data-visual-provenance="computed"`, and immediately after its caption the chip reads `Showing 9 of 31 packages and modules from the` followed by a link to `maps/units.html` reading `Unit map`, then `; 22 hidden by this dossier.`, with no further sentence
- **AND** the figure's list ends with one item carrying `data-hidden-count="22"` and reading `Not shown here: 22 packages and modules:` followed by the first 12 names joined by `, `, then `, and 10 more. This dossier's reason: Test fixtures and examples`, carrying no anchor, label or citation
- **AND** the legend carries `Double border: computed by Syzygy from the Unit map, in its words and marks.`, and the tally's sentence for the base reads `9 boxes and 0 arrows computed by Syzygy from the Unit map: 0 observed, 9 inferred, 0 unknown.`

#### Scenario: Hide without a reason refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 view's `base` carries a `hide` entry with `paths` and no `reason`, a `hide` entry whose `reason` is three spaces, a `select` with no `restReason`, a `restReason` with no `select`, or both `hide` and `select`
- **THEN** check reports a `schema` finding at that member and the draft does not proceed to review

#### Scenario: Base element keeps Syzygy's words

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 graph view starts from the directory map, the record's `top-level-unit` fact for `src`, counting 40 files, renders Observed, and the view's `names` gives `src` the display name `Server code`, labelled Inferred with a citation
- **THEN** the box reads `src/ (40 files)` alone, is drawn solid with a double border as Observed by the fact, carries the fact's `data-derived-id` and no claim anchor, and below it, outside its border, the drawing carries the italic line `called Server code` with `data-visual-provenance="curated"` and `data-epistemic="inferred"`
- **AND** its list item gives Syzygy's words for it, then `Called Server code by this dossier.` with the name's claim anchor and Inferred marking, and its phone-form item begins `Syzygy: ` and ends `, called Server code`
- **AND** no draft member changes the label `src/ (40 files)`, its mark or its identity, and the map page `maps/directories.html` is byte for byte that the render gives with no draft

#### Scenario: Group places base elements in the draft's element

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 containment view starts from the unit map listing 3 packages, each from its own manifest file, selects the model element `runtime`, which has no parent, and its `group` entry `{"element": "runtime", "paths": ["api/package.json", "worker/package.json"]}` places those two manifests' packages inside `runtime`
- **THEN** the figure draws `runtime` as its first top-level group in the draft's marking with the two packages inside it in Syzygy's words, and the third package as a second top-level group of its own
- **AND** each nesting is the draft's claim, read by rule `relation-witness` as an element's parent is, so none is witnessed and the two package boxes keep their Inferred mark and double border

#### Scenario: Select and group together

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 containment view starting from the unit map selects the model element `runtime`, its `base` carries `{"select": ["api/package.json", "web/package.json"], "restReason": "Tooling"}`, and its `group` entry `{"element": "runtime", "paths": ["api/package.json"]}`
- **THEN** check reports no `base-conflict` finding, and the figure draws `api`'s package inside `runtime` and `web`'s package as a top-level group of its own
- **AND** where the `group` entry is instead `{"element": "runtime", "paths": ["docs/package.json"]}`, a path `select` does not name, check reports a `base-conflict` finding at that `group` path

#### Scenario: Group into a third-level element refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 containment view selects the model elements `system`, `runtime` whose parent is `system`, and `api` whose parent is `runtime`, and its `base` of the unit map has a `group` entry whose `element` is `api`
- **THEN** check reports a `base-group` finding at that entry's `element` with the detail `<diagram>: a group places base elements inside an element this view selects, at level 1 or 2`
- **AND** the same finding is reported where a `group` entry's `element` is one the view does not select

#### Scenario: Directory containment keeps the root

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 containment view selecting no model element starts from the directory map, and its `select` names `src` and `docs` but not `.`
- **THEN** check reports a `base-root` finding at `$.diagrams[<i>].view.base.select` with the detail `<diagram>: the repository root frames a containment of the directory map and is shown, never hidden or grouped`
- **AND** where the `select` also names `.`, the figure draws the repository root as a top-level group holding `src/` and `docs/`, each nesting the record's `contains` fact in Syzygy's words, and the chip begins `Showing 2 of <t> top-level directories, with the repository root, from the`, `<t>` being the top-level directories the record lists

#### Scenario: One manifest declaring two packages

- **WHEN** the unit map lists two packages declared by one manifest file, and a schema-v15 view of the unit map names that file's path, without `#<k>`, in a `names` entry or a base end
- **THEN** check reports a `base-reference` finding at that path with the detail `<diagram>: <path> names 2 elements of the unit map; a display name or an arrow end names one, as <path>#<k>`
- **AND** the same path in `hide` hides both packages, and the path followed by `#2` in a `names` entry names the second of them in the member's order with no finding

- **THEN** check reports a `base-label` finding at that element's or display name's path with the detail `<diagram>: the draft's words never take the name of an element Syzygy draws in the same figure`
- **AND** in a graph view of the directory map, a selected cited relationship labelled `Contains` takes the same finding

#### Scenario: Cited arrow joins two base elements

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 model holds a cited relationship from the base end `{"map": "units", "path": "web/package.json"}` to `{"map": "units", "path": "api/package.json"}` by `depends-on`, labelled Inferred with a citation, each manifest declaring one package, no declared dependency fact of the revision joins the two packages, and a graph view starting from the unit map shows both and selects it
- **THEN** the arrow is drawn between the two packages' boxes in the draft's marking and label, with its claim anchor and citations, and carries `"provenance": "authored"` in `machine.json` while both boxes carry `"provenance": "derived"`

#### Scenario: Path the map does not list refused

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 view starting from the directory map hides the path `vendor`, and the record check derives lists no top-level directory `vendor`
- **THEN** check reports a `base-reference` finding at that path with the detail `<diagram>: vendor names no element of the directory map at this revision`

#### Scenario: Base element hidden and grouped refused

- **THEN** check reports a `base-conflict` finding at the `group` path with the detail `<diagram>: a base element is selected, hidden, grouped or named at most once, and grouped or named only where shown`

#### Scenario: Too many base elements refused

- **THEN** check reports a `base-size` finding at the view's `base` with the detail `<diagram>: a view shows one to 40 boxes, its base elements included`

This figure starts from the Directory map, which is Unknown at this render:` with the map's reason and words and a link to the map, carrying `data-epistemic="unknown"`, no element of the view is drawn, and the view's `machine.json` member `base` is `{"map", "established": false, "reason"}` alone
- **AND** where the base map was not established at check, check reports no `base-reference` finding for the view's paths, and where the render then establishes it and a path names no element it lists, the figure is the notice that its base does not resolve, which names no path

#### Scenario: Chip links from a deeper page

- **THEN** its chip reads `Showing 2 of 250 top-level directories, with the repository root, from the` followed by a link to `../maps/directories.html` reading `Directory map`, then `; 248 hidden by this dossier. The map also counts 3 more top-level directories it does not list, 0 files it leaves out and does not name because a secret detector matches their path, and 0 submodules it does not follow.`
- **AND** the figure's list ends with one item carrying `data-hidden-count="248"` and reading `Not shown here: 248 top-level directories:` followed by the first 12 names, then `, and 236 more. This dossier's reason: Other directories`

#### Scenario: Schema v14 and earlier keep their bytes under base views

- **WHEN** a draft of schema v14 or earlier is checked and rendered, once as stored and once with a view carrying `base`
- **THEN** the stored draft's findings, its `figureAdvice` and the bytes of every page, of `machine.json` and of every record check writes are those version 2.33 gives it
- **AND** a draft of schema v12 or v14 with the member gets a `field-version` finding at that view's `base` in place of the `schema` finding at that path, a draft of schema v13, or of v11 or earlier, gets the findings version 2.33 gives it, and every other finding is unchanged

#### Scenario: Figure advice reads no base view

- **WHEN** a schema-v15 draft that passes check has three produced diagrams, a graph view with a `base` that shows three packages of the unit map and no element of the model, and two graph views without a `base`, and its `figurePlan` names the base view's diagram as the planned figure of its home, of its kind
- **THEN** check reports no `figure-plan` finding for that entry, the report's `figureAdvice` has `examined` 2, and no advisory names the base view's diagram, nor names it as the other figure of a `figure-duplicate` advisory

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v23`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v15`, and equals brief v22 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the one derived-base paragraph placed immediately after the figure-plan paragraph

#### Scenario: Witnessed edge to a withheld node leaves the migration parity

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 draft that check passes holds two graph diagrams, the first with a `contains` edge labelled Inferred and citing a span screening admits, from a node whose unit is `{"kind": "repository-root"}` to a node whose unit is the `top-level-directory` `src`, which rule `relation-witness` witnesses so that the arrow renders Observed, the `src` node citing only a span screening withholds so that it renders as a withheld item, and the second with no node rendering as a withheld item, and the draft is migrated to schema v12
- **THEN** check passes the migrated draft with the original's advisories, each path moved, the `machine.json` figure entry of the relationship the edge migrates to carries `witness` `{"state": "unknown", "reason": "edge-not-inferred", "fact": null, "rule": null, "ruleVersion": null}`, and its arrow and list item render Inferred with the draft's marking and `data-relation` alone
- **AND** the first figure's `<figure>` element and its `machine.json` figure entry, the figure-fit member and the reading ledger are outside the parity and are those version 2.24's rules give the migrated draft, while the second figure's `<figure>` element and `machine.json` figure entry are byte-identical to the original's

#### Scenario: Import facts give a workspace's unit graph

- **WHEN** a draft is checked under declared-source policy 2.0.0 against a fixture tree whose root `package.json` declares `workspaces` `["units/*"]`, whose units/a/package.json and units/b/package.json name the packages `@w/a` and `@w/b`, and whose `units/a/src/index.ts` holds, on lines 1 to 4, `import { b } from '@w/b';`, `import type { T } from '../../b/src/types.js';`, `import './local.js';` and `import fs from 'node:fs';`, beside `units/a/src/local.ts` and `units/b/src/types.ts`, neither of which holds a statement
- **THEN** the check record holds `importFacts`, `state` `derived`, immediately before `manifestFacts`, with `coverage` `complete`, three candidates all `scanned`, `statements` `{"edge": 2, "sameUnit": 1, "external": 1, "noUnit": 0, "unknown": 0}`, the one edge `{"from": "units/a/package.json", "to": "units/b/package.json", "statements": 2}`, and the facts `_import-1` to `_import-4` in line order with forms `import`, `import-type`, `side-effect` and `import`, rules `typescript-package-import`, `typescript-relative-import`, `typescript-relative-import` and `typescript-package-import`, and outcomes `edge`, `edge`, `same-unit` and `external`, each `epistemic` `inferred`
- **AND** `languages` states TypeScript assessed and Python unevaluated, no fact or count carries an operand's text, every line pair is a range the path-and-range check accepts for its path, and the structure record and `manifestFacts` are byte-identical to those the same check writes under version 2.38

#### Scenario: Comments, strings, templates and regular expressions hold no import

- **WHEN** a scanned file holds one genuine `import { a } from './a';` and, at other lines, the same words inside a line comment, a block comment, a single- and a double-quoted string, a template literal and a regular-expression literal, and in paired replays the genuine statement is moved wholly into each of those texts in turn; further files hold the top-level lines `if (c) /import x from 'y'/.test(s);`, `export default /import x from 'y'/;` and `const q = (a + b) / 2;`, and others `const r = x! / 2;` and `i++ / 2;`
- **THEN** the first file gives exactly one fact, at the genuine statement's lines, and each replay gives no fact and no outside-grammar form at the moved text; the `if` and `export default` files are `scanned` with no fact, the `)` closing an `if` head and the word `default` each starting a regular-expression literal, and the `(a + b) / 2` file is `scanned`, its `/` a division
- **AND** the `x! / 2` and `i++ / 2` files are `incomplete`, the scan stopping at the `/` it cannot read either way rather than guessing, and no file states a fact for text inside a regular-expression literal

#### Scenario: Forms outside the grammar are counted and never resolved

- **WHEN** a scanned file holds a call `import('./a')`, a call `require('./b')`, a top-level `import c = require('./c');`, a top-level `import d from './d\u0041';` whose operand holds a backslash, `import e from './e.json';`, `import f from '/abs/f';`, an `import g from './g';` inside a `declare module 'm' { }` block, the top-level statements `import h from '.';`, `import i from '..';`, `import j from '#internal';`, `import k from '';` and `import l from './dir/';`, and `import m from './m' with { type: 'json' };`
- **THEN** the file's `outsideGrammar` is 13 and its `statements` is 0, none of those operands is resolved, named or counted as a statement, no operand comes out `external`, and `coverage` is `partial`, never `complete`
- **AND** a property call `obj.require('./n')`, a `function require(p)` declaration, `import.meta.url` and a namespace alias `import o = N.o;` are neither statements nor outside-grammar forms, and the same file without the thirteen forms has `coverage` `complete`

#### Scenario: Only a whole body the screen admits is scanned

- **WHEN** three otherwise equal candidates differ in one way each: the first's body holds a value a secret detector matches, the second's path lies under a directory whose name a secret detector matches, and the third lies under a tests-path segment
- **THEN** the first is a named file Unknown with reason `secret-detector-match`, never scanned and with null counts; the second is counted in `secretPathsLeftOut` and named nowhere; and the third is counted in `setApart` and never named or read
- **AND** `coverage` is `partial` or `unknown` and never `complete` while the first two are present, no text of the first body reaches the record or the report, and removing only the first two candidates makes `coverage` `complete`

#### Scenario: An interrupted scan keeps what closed before it

- **WHEN** one candidate holds a complete `import { a } from '../x/a';` on line 1 and an unterminated template literal from line 3, and a paired candidate holds the unterminated template from line 1 and the same statement after it
- **THEN** the first file is `incomplete` with one statement kept and `coverage` `partial`; the paired file, alone in its tree, is `incomplete` with no statement and `coverage` `unknown`
- **AND** neither tree states zero edges as a claim about the tree, and in neither is text after the interruption read for statements

#### Scenario: An undecidable target address is Unknown, never passed over

- **WHEN** a statement `import { y } from '../lib/y';` has the address `lib/y.ts` held as a blob whose screen path outcome is `unknown-extraction-class` and the address `lib/y/index.ts` held as an admitted blob in another unit, and in paired replays the first address is absent, held as a tree, or held as a symbolic link, and a further statement `import { w } from './link/../w';` names a path through a directory segment `link` the listing holds as a symbolic link
- **THEN** the first case is `unknown` with reason `target-undecidable`, names no target or target unit and names neither address; the absent and tree replays pass the first address over, resolve to `lib/y/index.ts` and give an `edge` to that file's unit; and the symbolic-link replay and the `./link/../w` statement are `unknown` with reason `target-undecidable`
- **AND** no target body and no link target is read in any case, as an instrumented reader shows

#### Scenario: A package name resolves only against complete manifest facts

- **WHEN** `import { z } from '@w/z';` is checked in four trees: one where exactly one `package.json` names `@w/z`, one where two do, one where none does and the `npm-package-manifest` entry is complete, and one where none does and one `package.json` is Unknown (`unparsed`)
- **THEN** the outcomes are `edge` (or `same-unit` where the importing file lies in that package), `unknown` with `target-ambiguous`, `external`, and `unknown` with `target-undecidable`
- **AND** a subpath `@w/z/sub` resolves as `@w/z` does, and in two further trees, one holding a set-apart `package.json` under a tests-path segment and one where exactly one `package.json` names `@w/z` and another is Unknown (`unparsed`), the outcome is `unknown` with `target-undecidable`, never `external` and never `edge`

#### Scenario: An undecidable source unit is Unknown

- **WHEN** a scanned file's nearest enclosing `package.json` entry is a symbolic link, and in a paired replay the same directory holds no `package.json` and the root does
- **THEN** the first file's statements are `unknown` with reason `source-unit-undecidable`, and the replay's statements take the root `package.json` as their source unit
- **AND** a file under no `package.json` at all gives statements of outcome `no-unit`, which are no edge and are not Unknown

#### Scenario: Read limits are inclusive and stop in path order

- **WHEN** synthetic trees hold a candidate of exactly 262,144 bytes and one of 262,145 bytes; 2,000 and then 2,001 named files that no reason of version 2.7 reaches; and files whose bytes reach exactly 16,777,216 and then pass that total
- **THEN** the 262,144-byte file is scanned and the 262,145-byte file is Unknown (`too-large`); the 2,001st file in path order is Unknown (`over-read-limit`) and not read; and the file whose bytes pass the total, and every later file, is Unknown (`over-read-limit`) and not scanned
- **AND** each such file is counted in `unknown` and the `coverage` is `partial`

#### Scenario: Listing stops at the byte budget and counts the rest

- **WHEN** a synthetic tree's facts, files and edges together would take more than 1,900,000 bytes as listed entries
- **THEN** `edges` is listed first, then `files`, then `facts`, each as the longest prefix within the budget, the rest counted in its `NotListed` member, and the counts of `statements`, `scanned`, `incomplete` and `unknown` still count every statement and file
- **AND** the member takes fewer than 2,000,000 bytes, and the check record is within the read bound or written in its bounded form, which holds no `importFacts`

#### Scenario: A byte-order mark and CRLF lines keep line numbers citable

- **WHEN** a scanned file begins with U+FEFF, ends its lines with CRLF, and holds `import { a } from '../x/a';` on its line 2 and a statement spanning its lines 4 to 6
- **THEN** the file is `scanned`, the first statement's `lines` is `[2, 2]` and the second's `[4, 6]`, and the path-and-range check accepts both ranges as citations of the file's path
- **AND** the same file with LF lines and no U+FEFF gives the same facts and the same `lines`

#### Scenario: Earlier records and every other output keep their bytes

- **WHEN** a run's earlier check record written before this version is read back by review, render and close, and the same draft, whose full check record lies within the read bound under both versions, is checked and rendered under this version, in both modes
- **THEN** the earlier record keeps its bytes and digest and is read as before, with no `importFacts`; the new record differs from the version 2.38 record only by the `importFacts` member
- **AND** every rendered page, `machine.json`, `dossier.json`, `derived-structure.json`, the size report, the review packets, every relation witness state and the provider-mode pages are byte-identical to those version 2.38 gives

- **THEN** the boxes carry the ids `walk-f-1` and `walk-f-2` for `f` and `walk-f-1-1` and `walk-f-1-2` for `f-1`, each once on the page, and following `#walk-f-1-1` makes the box of `f-1`'s first step, and no other element, the page's target

- **AND** the boxes over `cache` and `queue` hold no link, and the drawing's bytes, checked by the inertness check of the drawing as before, carry no link
