- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v24`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v16`, and equals brief v23 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the walk paragraph placed immediately after the computed-map paragraph
- **AND** the success report and the brief record name brief v24


#### Scenario: A migrated model past the v12 bounds keeps no parity

- **WHEN** a schema-v11 draft that check passes holds six graph diagrams of 37 nodes each, 222 nodes in all, with 60 edges in all and no containment, and is migrated to schema v12
- **THEN** the migrated draft's `model` holds 222 elements, one per node, and check refuses it with a `schema` finding at `$.model.elements`, so the parity of version 2.24 is not stated for it, and the migration's output is the one the rules of version 2.24 give
- **AND** a schema-v11 draft that check passes holding six graph diagrams of 160 nodes and 400 edges in all and one containment of 40 groups, five at level 1, 200 elements in all, migrates to a draft check passes, with the parity stated, whose `model` holds 200 elements, the 35 nested groups each carrying `parent`, and 400 relationships, none for a nesting
- **AND** the same draft with one graph node more, 201 nodes and groups in all and 400 edges, migrates to a draft check refuses with a `schema` finding at `$.model.elements` alone, and with one graph edge more instead, 200 nodes and groups and 401 edges in all, to a draft check refuses with a `schema` finding at `$.model.relationships`

#### Scenario: An import statement corroborates an imports arrow

- **WHEN** a schema-v9 graph figure has nodes `web` and `core` whose units are `{"kind": "package-manifest", "path": "web/package.json"}` and `{"kind": "package-manifest", "path": "core/package.json"}`, and an Inferred edge `i1` from `web` to `core` with relation `imports`; and the check's `importFacts` member is `derived`, its first `edge` fact joining those two units being `_import-3`, of rule `typescript-relative-import` version 1
- **THEN** check reports no finding for `i1`, and the render draws it with the draft's marking, label and citations, never solid and never with `data-epistemic="observed"`, its arrow and list item carrying `data-relation="imports"`, `data-witness-state="corroborated"`, `data-witness-fact="_import-3"`, `data-witness-rule="typescript-relative-import"` and `data-witness-rule-version="1"`
- **AND** its list item's evidence text names `_import-3`, its form, rule `typescript-relative-import` version 1, the pinned revision and the digest of the `importFacts` member, and no path, line or operand of the fact; `machine.json` carries `i1` with `witness` `{"state": "corroborated", "reason": null, "fact": "_import-3", "rule": "typescript-relative-import", "ruleVersion": 1}`, labelled as the draft marks it; and the draft's counts and the figure's tally and legend equal those of the same draft whose `i1` is `unknown`
- **AND** the evidence text reads `a lexical import-type statement` where `_import-3`'s `form` is `import-type`, and `i1` is corroborated as before; a `typescript-package-import` fact joining the two units corroborates `i1` as `_import-3` does, naming that rule; and where the member's only `edge` fact runs from the unit of `core` to the unit of `web`, `i1` is `unknown` with `coverage-partial`
- **AND** the same draft migrated to schema v12 and checked in the same run with no refused read renders `i1`'s relationship corroborated by `_import-3`, with byte-identical evidence text, `import facts sha256:` digest and figure, and no byte of the evidence text names the check record

#### Scenario: A missing import fact is never read as no import

- **WHEN** the same edge `i1` is checked over an `importFacts` member that is `derived` and holds no `edge` fact from the unit of `web` to the unit of `core`, once with `coverage` `complete`, once with `coverage` `partial`, and once with `factsNotListed` above zero
- **THEN** `i1` is `unknown` with `coverage-partial` each time, never `challenged`, check reports no `relation-challenged` finding, and nothing on the page or in `machine.json` states that `web` does not import `core`
- **AND** a `same-unit` statement of the unit of `web` corroborates no `imports` edge from `web` to `web`, which is `unknown` with `coverage-partial`, and an `external` statement naming a package no unit declares corroborates nothing

#### Scenario: Import facts that cannot be read leave the arrow Unknown

- **WHEN** the same edge `i1` is rendered from a check record that holds no `importFacts` member, because it was written before version 2.39 or, in an injected record since a bounded record's revision never reaches a render, in its bounded form, or from one whose member is in `state` `unknown`
- **THEN** `i1` is `unknown` with `facts-unknown`, whatever the structure record and the `manifestFacts` member hold, and the render scans no file body for it
- **AND** an `imports` edge from a node whose unit is a `top-level-directory` to `core` is `unknown` with `endpoint-kind-mismatch`, an `imports` edge from a node with no `unit` to `core` is `unknown` with `endpoint-without-unit`, an `imports` edge the draft labels Unknown is `unknown` with `edge-not-inferred`, and a v12 derived relationship with relation `imports` still draws a `relationship-not-derivable` finding

#### Scenario: A corroborated relationship shown in two views

- **WHEN** a schema-v12 draft's model holds elements `web` and `core` with the two `package-manifest` units above and an Inferred cited relationship `i1` from `web` to `core` with relation `imports`, which the views of two produced graph diagrams, `deps` and then `layers`, both select, both rendering as figures on `index.html`; and the check's `importFacts` member is `derived`, its first `edge` fact joining the two units being `_import-3`
- **THEN** in `deps`, `i1`'s list item carries its claim anchor, its citations and the evidence text naming `_import-3`, and in `layers` its item reads `web → core: <label>, as shown in <a href="#figure-deps">…</a>` with no claim anchor, no citation and no evidence text
- **AND** in both figures `i1`'s arrow and list item carry `data-witness-state="corroborated"` and `data-witness-fact="_import-3"`, `i1` is counted once among the draft's claims, and `machine.json`'s `model` member carries its `witness` once, as `corroborated`

#### Scenario: A view's level refuses an element of another level

- **WHEN** a schema-v17 draft's model holds `app` of type `system`, `api` of type `package` whose `parent` is `app`, `router` of type `module` whose `parent` is `api`, and `user` of type `person`; and diagram `parts` is a graph whose view has `level` `container` and `scope` `app` and selects `api` and `router`
- **THEN** check reports one finding of kind `view-level` at `$.diagrams[<i>].view.elements[1]`, `<i>` the index of `parts`, with the detail `parts: a container view shows no element of type module`, and no `view-scope-outside` finding for `router`, a module being no part a container view bounds
- **AND** a context view of scope `app` selecting `app` and `api` draws a `view-level` finding at `api` naming type `package`, and a component view of scope `api` selecting `router` and `user` draws no finding of this version

#### Scenario: A view's scope is of its level and bounds what it shows

- **WHEN** the same model also holds `other` of type `system`, `lib` of type `package` whose `parent` is `other`, and `db` of type `store` with no `parent`; and four graph views, in diagram order, carry: `wrong`, `level` `component` with `scope` `app`, selecting `api` alone; `lonely`, `level` `context` with `scope` `app`, selecting `user` alone; `wide`, `level` `container` with `scope` `app`, selecting `api`, `lib`, `db` and `user`; and `ghosted`, `level` `container` with `scope` `ghost`, which names no element
- **THEN** `wrong` draws one `view-scope` finding at `$.diagrams[<i>].view.scope` with the detail `wrong: a context or container view is about a system, and a component view about a directory, package, service, job or store` and no other finding of this version; `lonely`, one `view-scope` finding there with the detail `lonely: a context view shows the system it is about`; `wide`, two `view-scope-outside` findings, at `lib` then at `db`, with the details `wide: lib lies outside app, the element this view is about` and `wide: db lies outside app, the element this view is about`, and none at `api` or `user`; and `ghosted`, one `unresolved-reference` finding at its `scope` with the detail `ghost: no element of the model has this identity` and no other finding of this version
- **AND** a view carrying `level` without `scope`, or `level` `system`, draws a `schema` finding at that member and no other finding of this version

#### Scenario: A base view's level types its base elements

- **WHEN** a v17 draft's containment view of the `directories` map, the map established at check with the repository root and two top-level directories, carries `level` `context` and a `scope` naming a `system` element the view selects, and shows every base element
- **THEN** check reports a `view-level` finding at `$.diagrams[<i>].view.base` whose detail names type `directory` and no base path
- **AND** the same view whose `select` names `.` alone, with its `restReason`, draws no finding of this version, and where the structure record is `unknown` at check no `view-level` finding at `base` is computed or reported

#### Scenario: A box whose element has its own view links to it

- **WHEN** a v17 draft holds, in this diagram order, the produced graph `ctx` (title `The system and its users`, `level` `context`, `scope` `app`, selecting `app` and `user`), homed at the overview, and the produced graph `parts` (title `Parts of the app`, `level` `container`, `scope` `app`, selecting `api` and `db`, every part below `app`), homed at deep dive `runtime`, each drawing below 1048 px wide
- **THEN** on `index.html` the list item for `app` in `ctx` ends with ` <a class="zoom-text" href="deep-dives/runtime.html#figure-parts">Zoom in: Parts of the app</a>`, and the `walk-layer` box for `app` holds `<a class="zoom-link" href="deep-dives/runtime.html#figure-parts">`, whose hidden text reads `Zoom in to <label>: Parts of the app`; the box for `user` holds nothing; neither page holds a script; and `ctx`'s `<figure>` carries `data-view-level="context"` and `data-view-scope="app"` before its `id`
- **AND** `ctx`'s layer sits in `<div class="walk-figure zoom-only">`, and `index.html` carries the positioning, zoom and pass-through rules; moving the keyboard focus forward from the start of `ctx` reaches the zoom link before any link of the figure's lists, it shows a focus outline, and following it opens `deep-dives/runtime.html` with `figure-parts` as the page's target; `ctx`, though first in draft order and about `app`, is not `app`'s zoom target, being a context view; `parts` draws no zoom link to itself, and `machine.json`, `dossier.json` and the check record equal those of the same draft with neither view carrying `level` or `scope` but for the bytes naming the schema or digest

#### Scenario: Zoom links and a walk share one layer

- **WHEN** the overview of the same draft also carries a walk over `ctx` whose first step names `app`, and in a second draft a layout supplied to check draws `ctx` 1048 px wide (the readable layout lays out no figure that wide)
- **THEN** `ctx` has one wrapper, `<div class="walk-figure">` as version 2.40 writes it, and one layer, and the box for `app` holds the badge, then its `walk-source` link where its first citation has one, then the zoom link, in that order, the walk's list following every link of the layer
- **AND** the 1048 px `ctx` carries no wrapper, no layer and no walk, and its list item for `app` still ends with the `zoom-text` link

#### Scenario: The zoom target is the first rendered container view, never a context view

- **WHEN** a v17 draft holds, in this diagram order, four produced graphs about `app`: `ctx`, of `level` `context`, homed at the overview; `hidden`, of `level` `container`, whose figure renders inside a collapsible section of deep dive `runtime`; `parts`, of `level` `container`, titled `Parts of the app`, homed at deep dive `runtime` outside every collapsible section; and `more`, of `level` `container`, homed at deep dive `storage`, drawn as a containment whose outer box is `app`
- **THEN** `app`'s zoom target is `parts`: `ctx`'s box for `app` holds `<a class="zoom-link" href="deep-dives/runtime.html#figure-parts">`, and `more`'s outer box for `app` `<a class="zoom-link" href="runtime.html#figure-parts">`, each list item for `app` ending with the matching `zoom-text` link `Zoom in: Parts of the app`
- **AND** no figure links to `ctx`, `hidden` or `more`, and `parts` gives `app` no zoom link

#### Scenario: A containment figure's layer holds a box per group

- **WHEN** a v17 draft's produced containment `nest`, below 1048 px wide, draws `app` as its outer group holding the groups `api` and `db`, in that order, and `api` is the scope of a produced, rendered component view `inside-api` on another page
- **THEN** `nest`'s drawing is wrapped in `<div class="walk-figure zoom-only">` with a `walk-layer` holding three `walk-node` boxes, for `app`, `api` and `db` in that order, each at the position and size of the box the layout draws that group in, in the drawing's own coordinates
- **AND** the box for `api` holds a `zoom-link` to `#figure-inside-api` on its page, the boxes for `app` and `db` hold nothing, and the drawing's bytes equal those of the same draft with no `level` or `scope`

#### Scenario: A walked figure and a zoom-only figure share one page

- **WHEN** the overview of a v17 draft holds the graph `flow`, which carries a walk and draws no element with a zoom target, and the graph `ctx`, which carries no walk and draws `app`, whose zoom target is `parts` on deep dive `runtime`
- **THEN** `flow`'s wrapper is `<div class="walk-figure">` and its layer, badges, `walk-source` links, `:target` highlight and box outline under the pointer behave as version 2.40 states, and `ctx`'s wrapper is `<div class="walk-figure zoom-only">`, whose layer and empty boxes pass the pointer to the drawing, which keeps its own emphasis of a node under the pointer, and whose only box with content holds the zoom link for `app`
- **AND** the page carries the positioning rules once, the walk rules, and the zoom and pass-through rules, and no walk rule outlines or takes the pointer for a box inside `ctx`'s wrapper

#### Scenario: No zoom target, no zoom link

- **WHEN** a v17 draft holds two views about `app`: the produced graph `ctx`, of `level` `context` and `scope` `app`, selecting `app` and `user`, and the graph `parts`, of `level` `container` and `scope` `app`, whose disposition is not `produced`; and a second draft carries no `level` or `scope` at all
- **THEN** `app` has no zoom target, no figure of the first draft carries a zoom link or a layer for one, no page of it carries the zoom, pass-through or (holding no walk) positioning rules, and every figure is rendered as it is with the `level` and `scope` of both views removed, but for `ctx`'s `<figure>`, which carries `data-view-level="context"` and `data-view-scope="app"` before its `id`
- **AND** the second draft is checked and rendered exactly as the same draft in schema v16, apart from the bytes naming its schema or digest

#### Scenario: A view's level and the mixed-levels advice read different things

- **WHEN** a v17 draft's graph `parts`, with `level` `container` and `scope` `app`, selects `app`, `api` and `web`, both of type `package` with `parent` `app`, and no `contains` relationship; a containment `nest` with the same level and scope draws `api` and `web` inside `app`; and a graph `pkgs` of `level` `container` and `scope` `app` selects `api` and `api-core`, of type `package` with `parent` `api`
- **THEN** check reports no finding of this version for any of the three views; the figure advice holds a `figure-mixed-levels` advisory for `parts` and one for `pkgs`, each with the detail version 2.33 gives, and none for `nest`
- **AND** removing every `level` and `scope` leaves the figure advice unchanged

#### Scenario: Earlier schemas take level and scope as a field-version

- **WHEN** a schema-v16 draft's graph view `parts` carries `level` `container` and `scope` `app`
- **THEN** check reports a `field-version` finding at `$.diagrams[<i>].view.level` and one at `$.diagrams[<i>].view.scope`, each with the detail ``parts: `level` and `scope` need draft schema polaris-dossier-local-draft-v17``, in place of the `schema` finding there, and every other finding version 2.44 reports for that draft

#### Scenario: Brief v25 offers levels and zoom

- **WHEN** a run is briefed under this version
- **THEN** the brief is `polaris-dossier-brief-v25`, issuing schema `polaris-dossier-local-draft-v17`, and equals brief v24 for the same run except its version line, its draft-schema line and the level paragraph placed immediately after the walk paragraph
- **AND** the success report and the brief record name brief v25

Form: event-response.

- **Case:** Submit controlled drafts against a fixture repository at a pinned commit, varying each quotation, citation, label, block kind, understanding item, execution marking and screening outcome independently, including exact, normalised, altered, elided, joined, out-of-range and excluded-file quotations, and altering recorded byte ranges between check and render; and with presentation elements, varying each claim an element carries, each short label's length, citations and markup, and each code excerpt's line count and screening outcome, separating paths excluded before reading from bodies read and then excluded, invalid UTF-8, HTML text escaping, Unicode code-point boundaries, contextual table-cell meaning and collapsed Unknown counts; and varying an uncited block's judgement mark and schema version, a claim block's or inventory entry's withheld spans, the committed policy script condition and its exact binding, a page's scripts on or off, and Pole counts and membership. With schema v5, vary each diagram's home kind, its target's existence and disposition, a second home and each malformed home, including a home whose deep dive is not produced, a `home` in a v4 draft and a withheld draft layer; and vary each figure's laid-out width, including exactly 1048 px, at both viewports, checking the same draft twice. With the derived structure, vary the pinned tree's top-level directories, root files, submodules and secret-matching paths, their number at one, past twelve and past 250, the repository's visibility and each exclusion ground, the committed currency policy's presence, digest and declaration for the rule, the record's presence, state, revision and facts, the tree listing's success at check and at render, a second render of one draft revision, a draft figure matching a derived fact, a draft element labelled `observed`, and the mode. With the reading ledger, vary the draft's Poles, why section, deep dives and their dispositions, figure homes, advisories of each standard and figure-fit entries, a section in no Pole, checking one draft twice and checking an inventory; and vary the state root's other runs: none, one of another repository, one of this repository with and without a ledger, two of this repository with different and equal check times, and pages added, removed, changed and unchanged between two ledgers. With shape packs, vary each `server-entry` basename and each exclusion ground, a secret-matching path, the trait profile's derivation, a brief record naming an unshipped pack or a digest the recomposition does not give, and the mode. With thesis basis, vary `thesisBasis` over its three values and its absence, the introduction's label, basis, quotations and citations, each cited path's rules, withheld spans, a withheld draft layer and the schema version. With figure kinds, vary a diagram's kind over the six graph kinds, `sequence` and `swimlane`; each kind's members present, absent and swapped for another kind's; each count at, below and past its bounds; each `from`, `to` and `lane` resolving in its own diagram, naming an element of another diagram and naming nothing; a message to itself; consecutive steps in one lane and in two; a participant or lane named by nothing; withheld participants, messages, lanes and steps; each home; the schema version from v2 to v7; the mode; and the width of the figure's box, at 390 and 1440 px and at each W and one pixel less. With manifest recognizers, vary each recognizer's file over a workspace, a single package, an empty file, a malformed file, a wrong-typed field, a repeated key and a form beyond the reader; each path rule's boundary (a basename differing in case, a workflow one directory deeper, a `.yml` and a `.yaml`); each disposition (a path over 512 code units or with a control character, a secret-matching path or prefix, a vendored, generated or tests segment, a symbolic link, a denied, indeterminate or unconsented path, the 250th and 251st readable file, a refused read, a non-UTF-8 body, a blob of 1,048,576 bytes and one byte more, a body a detector or the active-content rule excludes, a body a screen double retains with lines withheld); unread named files sorting before and after read ones; a value of 256 and 257 code units, one with a control character and one a detector matches; 1,000 and 1,001 facts; the bound policy (1.0.0 and 2.0.0) and the run's content classes; and the draft, parsing and not. With the version 2.8 repairs, vary a check record carrying a `manifestFacts` member at every cap in its widest form, and check records of 16,777,216 bytes and one byte more, of 2,000,000 values and one more, and with a value nested 16 and 17 levels below the root, each read by review, render, close, the reading difference and the end screen; a derived record, otherwise read, naming a non-secret top-level directory the tree does not hold, one the tree holds past the render's listing of 250, a rule other than `top-level-unit` or a version other than 1, and a stale record whose every path the render derives; a deep-dive identity holding a character outside the Basic Multilingual Plane, a lone surrogate, or only the permitted characters; and a schema-v7 graph diagram lacking `nodes`, lacking `edges`, and carrying each of `participants`, `messages`, `lanes` and `steps`. With the version 2.9 maps and advice, vary derived records (current, stale, missing and unknown; 0, 12, 13, 250 and 300 top-level directories; secret-matching paths and submodules), the bound screen (declared-source policy 1.0.0 and 2.0.0), manifest files of each recognizer (read, each Unknown reason, past the read and listing caps), workflows of 0, 1, 24 and 25 jobs with `needs` naming a job of the file or none, a planted `manifestFacts` member, a draft repeating a map's labels, and drafts whose figure-fit entries fall in each class, checked over several revisions with a check record past the read bound and one without `figureFit`. With the version 2.10 bound, vary drafts and inventories whose full records fall just within and just past the byte bound, and records just within and past the value and depth bounds through an injected measure, a draft with findings past it, and inventory check records of 262,144 and 262,145 bytes, 4,096 and 4,097 values, and past the read bound, each read by Syzygy deciding whether the inventory counts and by the supervisor; and, for the derived region, a record check wrote read by a render whose screen matches one of its directories, the record removed, a directory, a FIFO and a device standing at its path, and a path that cannot be examined; and a device, a FIFO and a growing file at a check record's path. For version 2.11, workflow files with and without a name, triggers, jobs and `job-needs` facts, among them one with no job and one with a need naming no job; check records beside the run file named for another revision or of another format; manifest maps whose recognizers count no Unknown file, list every Unknown file, list some and count more, and list none and count some; a draft whose full check record is past the bound with an earlier readable ledger in the state root; and graph figures of one layer, of chains and of fan-outs laid out by the readable layout and by a supplied layout that draws columns. With version 2.14, vary each infrastructure recognizer's path rule (each Compose basename, a YAML file under each Kubernetes directory name and under `templates`, a `.tf` file beside `.tf.json`, `.tfvars` and `.tfstate` files, each makefile name, `CMakeLists.txt`), each read field's type and form, each fault, streams of several documents, read counts at and past 100, secret-matching and set-apart paths, and trees whose top-level directories number at and past 250, under declared-source policies 1.0.0 and 2.0.0; and a draft that does not parse whose record would be past the read bound; and, at the path of the draft check is given, of a frozen draft revision, of `brief.json` and of `run-advice.json`, a regular file at and one byte past its bound, a FIFO, a device, a directory and a file grown after its size was taken. With inventory coverage, vary whether an inventory counts and each reason it does not, a highest-numbered revision with findings after one that passed, the frozen inventory's bytes after its check, a reader that fails, and checking an inventory; for one entry vary each citation's path and line range against each part of the draft that cites (introduction, section, deep dive, table cell, code excerpt, diagram node and edge, unresolved item, understanding record, discovery account), ranges that touch at one line and that miss by one line, a path named in a claim block's `text`, inside a longer path there, in a short label and in an understanding item, an entry citing nothing, an entry whose every cited file screening withholds, and a path a secret detector matches; and vary the omission record over absent, matching, unmatched, naming a covered entry, naming one entry twice, each bound exceeded by one, a missing or extra member, and each schema version. With task routes and the index, vary whether the draft carries `taskRoutes`; the number of routes at, below and past 8 and 15 and of steps at, below and past 1 and 8; each step's label, basis, citation count, cited path, cited range and quotation, each as for any block; each `see` naming a section, a deep dive with and without a page of its own, and nothing; a task with markup, with a quotation, and over only withheld steps; the schema version from v2 to the latest; blocks inside and outside a collapsible section; a term, path, directory, identifier and figure title each named by no block, by one and by several, in other cases, as part of a longer word or path, and in a quotation; a withheld item, a path a secret detector matches and a withheld draft layer; whether the derived structure is established; and the mode; and, for version 2.16, a schema-v8 draft whose sections carry two and three subsections, among them an answer of two sentences, one with an abbreviation, one quoting the project, a subsection whose claims are all withheld and a section that is not produced, the same draft without subsections, and drafts of schema v7 and earlier with and without `subsections`; and makefiles with recipe lines inside `ifdef` and `ifeq`/`else` blocks a tab-led line after an assignment, tab-led continuation lines of an assignment and of a rule, a tab-led `define` body, a tab-only line, and directive lines holding a `:`; and, for version 2.19, schema-v9 drafts whose graph edges carry each of the eleven relation types, with nodes bound to each of the six unit forms and unbound, over fixture trees that hold and that lack the top-level directory a `contains` arrow names, with a path a secret detector matches, with more than 250 top-level directories, with a top-level submodule and a directory holding only a submodule, with the currency policy's bytes missing, and with a manifest declaring the dependency an arrow names; edges labelled Unknown and edges citing only withheld spans; units in every `unit-reference` form and at a path a secret detector matches; and drafts of schema v8 and earlier with and without `relation` or `unit`; and, for version 2.20, schema-v10 drafts whose `hero` names a produced overview figure of each graph kind, a section-homed or deep-dive-homed graph figure, a `sequence` or `swimlane` figure, an unknown identity and a non-produced figure, and whose `hero` is absent, malformed, carries both forms or another member, or declares an omission with an Inferred, Unknown, non-normative or malformed block; the same drafts with their draft layer withheld; and drafts of schema v9 and earlier with and without `hero`; and, for version 2.22, operator-agent drafts with and without Poles, deep dives, a figure homed on the overview, run pages and Unknown or withheld claims on the landing page and on a Pole page, every stored run's latest passed draft drawn again, and a provider-mode render; and, for version 2.23, schema-v11 containment figures of one to five top-level groups and one to three levels, at and past each bound (depth, 40 groups, eight children, five top-level groups), with no nesting, with an empty `groups` list, with a group past level 3 carrying a malformed unit, with members of another kind, with Unknown and withheld groups, with and without units on each pair of nested groups and with complete and partial structure records, each rendered at 1440 and 390, and drafts of schema v10 and earlier with and without a containment; and, for version 2.24, schema-v12 drafts whose models hold elements of every type, with and without parents and units, cited and derived relationships, and views that share elements, that name missing elements or relationships, that show a relationship without its ends, that repeat an entry, and containment views at and past each bound; elements and relationships no view shows; parent chains of one to four and cycles; derived relationships over each pair of unit kinds with complete and partial structure records; withheld elements; every stored draft of schema v10 and v11 migrated; and drafts of schema v11 and earlier with and without `model` and `view`. For terminology, issue the ordinary brief from a synthetic pinned-run declaration, then separately remove only the instruction or restore only the v19 version; and, for version 2.33, schema-v14 drafts with complete, incomplete, repeated and malformed figure plans, planned figures named, unresolved, misplaced and of the wrong kind, and omitted places; figures on each side of each advice rule's boundary (a third and a fourth node, a third edge among three nodes, a third and a fourth step, a second and a third message, a third and a fourth group; equal, contained, extended and reversed keys and another family; a unit enclosing another with and without a `contains` arrow, a chain of them or a nesting, a directory that is only a prefix of a path, and an element beside its model parent or grandparent in a graph view and a containment view); views sharing elements; entries with an unresolved or non-string `target`; the same drafts with their draft layer withheld; schema-v14 drafts joining views, a containment, the worth-knowing mark and `questionKinds`; and drafts of schema v13 and earlier with and without `figurePlan`, `model` and `view`; and, for version 2.35, schema-v15 drafts whose graph and containment views start from the directory and unit maps with `hide`, with `select` and `restReason`, with neither, and with `group` and `names`, at and past each bound, with paths the map lists and does not list, manifest files declaring one and several packages, groups at each level, display names and draft labels equal to base names, with base ends on cited relationships, over complete, partial, stale and unlistable trees, homed on pages at each depth; drafts of schema v14 and earlier with and without `base`; and plans and figure advice over drafts with and without base views; and, for version 2.38, schema-v10 and v11 drafts that check passes with and without a withheld-end edge, witnessed, challenged and not witnessed, beside an edge the draft labels Unknown and a figure that draws none, migrated to schema v12; and, for version 2.40, schema-v16 drafts with well-formed and malformed walks in sections, subsections and deep dives; walks whose figure is unresolved, homed elsewhere, of a non-graph kind, both, not produced, withheld, rendered as the inertness notice or drawn 1048 px wide; steps naming a node, a view's element, an element a later view shares with an earlier one, an element the view does not show, an element twice, a paragraph of the place, of a subsection, of another place, inside a presentation element, one citing nothing and one whose citations are withheld; nodes that are withheld, cite no file with a source page, or cite several spans; steps naming a base element of a view with a `base`, one it hides, one of another map and one it does not list, over a base map established and not established at check; pages holding several walked figures whose identities share a prefix; and drafts of schema v15 and earlier with and without `walk`; and, for version 2.43, schema-v10 and v11 drafts that check passes whose graph nodes and containment groups number 200 and 201 in all and whose graph edges number 400 and 401 in all, spread over several diagrams, migrated to schema v12; and, for version 2.44, graph figures holding `imports` edges between `package-manifest` units, over `importFacts` members with and without a joining `edge` fact, with `complete`, `partial` and `unknown` coverage, with facts not listed, and check records without the member; and, for version 2.45, views carrying each `level` and `scope`, selecting elements and base elements of each type, inside and outside the scope, with zoom targets on the same page and on others, in a notice, inside a collapsible section and after a context view about the same element, graph and containment layers, and with and without a walk, a walked and a zoom-only figure on one page among them.
- **Observable:** The issued brief, the repair findings, the recorded byte ranges, the rendered quotations, anchors, understanding record and source pages, and the labels in human and machine output; the rendered elements, their HTML element names and header-cell scopes, the pages' scripts and content security policy, the reading view's and evidence view's text, the machine form, the appendix and its counts, and the fidelity verdict's entries. The page each figure renders on and its place there, and each check record's `figureFit` member and its bytes. The run directory's `derived/rev-<n>.json`, the `derived-structure` region, its figure's drawing, legend, tally and list, each derived element's and entry's attributes and evidence text, every figure's `data-visual-provenance`, the file `derived-structure.json` and its digest, and the `derivedStructure` member of `machine.json`. Each check record's `readingLedger` and its bytes, check's report's `readingDifference`, `unreadableRuns` and `unreadableCheckRecords`, and every file a check writes; the structure record's `traits.serverEntry`; the brief, its sentence before the standards, its reader-standards section and heading, the brief record's `readerStandards` and `shapePacks`, and the design packet's standards, their introducing sentence and its refusals; each `thesis-basis` and `field-version` finding, the thesis-basis line and its place, and `machine.json`'s `thesisBasis`. Each `unresolved-reference`, `figure-kind`, `figure-unused` and `field-version` finding and its location; each sequence and swimlane figure's attributes, drawing coordinates, laid-out width and height, phone form, legend, tally, list of every component and relationship and declarative source; which form is displayed, and the figure box's and the page's scroll width against their client width, at 390 and 1440 px in a browser with scripts off; the style rules each page carries; each check record's `figureFit` for these figures; `machine.json`'s `figureStructures` and claims; and brief v12. Each check record's `manifestFacts` member and its bytes, every recognizer entry and file entry and their counts, every fact and its line span, check's report, and which blobs the object reader is asked for; and every page, `machine.json` and published file of a render of such a run. Each check-record reader's outcome for each such record (review's packets, render's site or refusal, close, the reading difference and the end screen's counts) and the member's encoded size; for each such derived record, the `derived-structure` region, `machine.json`'s `derivedStructure`, whether `derived-structure.json` is published, and every place the directory's name appears; each reading-ledger page name; and each finding and its path for each such graph diagram. Each map page's section, note, coverage line, figures (caption, forms, W, legend, tally, element identities and marks), lists and `Not established.` sentences; the region's `derived-maps` line, `contents.html`'s items, `computedMaps` and the size report; each map figure's fit at 1440 and 390; and each check's report (`figureFit`, `mechanismAdvice`), check record and `run-advice.json`. For version 2.10, each check record's bytes, values and depth, its form, full or bounded, its outcome and findings, and the report's `findingsLeftOut`; whether each inventory counts and with what reason, and the supervisor's phase and stop; whether a reader reads any byte of a file past 16,777,216 bytes; and the region's and directory map's words, `derivedStructure.state` and `computedMaps.directories.state`. For version 2.11, the CI pipeline map's DOM as an HTML parser builds it; `run-advice.json`'s `checkRecords`; each manifest map's closing list; `machine.json`'s `pages`; check's report's `readingDifference`; each graph figure's laid-out width, its figure-fit entries and their class; and the map pages' counted sentences at a count of one. For version 2.14, each check record's `manifestFacts` recognizer entries, facts and `remainder`; the object reader's reads; `maps/infrastructure.html`, the `coverage-remainder` section of `maps/directories.html`, the region's `derived-maps` line and the map items of `contents.html`; and `computedMaps.infrastructure` and `computedMaps.manifests.remainder`; check's outcome and reason for each draft path, the reason review, render and close give for each frozen revision, check's and the reverification's refusal for each brief record, the inventory check's outcome, the run file reader's result, and how many bytes each reader reads from each path. Check's report's `inventoryCoverageAdvice` member, its `rule`, `inventory`, `counts`, lists and `note`, and its absence from an inventory check's report, the check record, the step log, every packet but for the draft's own `inventoryOmissions`, and every rendered file; the check's outcome, findings and exit status with and without an inventory; each `field-version` and schema finding for the omission record. Each finding under `$.taskRoutes` and its location; `find-by-task.html` and `name-index.html` whole; every `id` on every page and whether it lies inside a collapsible section; every link of the two pages and whether an element with that `id` is on the page it names; each page's navigation and the contents page; `dossier.json`'s pages; `machine.json`'s `taskRoutes`, `nameIndex` and claims; the brief's version line and task-routes section; the calls a render makes; and the provider mode's pages; for version 2.16, the `subsection-answer`, `field-version`, `schema` and `label-markup` findings at subsections and their locations, each check record's `readingLedger`, each subsection's markup, heading level, fragment identifier and place, each page's contents list and its links, and which pages carry the subsection style rules; for version 2.19, the `schema`, `field-version`, `unit-reference` and `relation-challenged` findings, their locations, order and details, each edge's drawing, label words, marks and `data-relation` and `data-witness-*` attributes, each figure's legend and tally, and each edge's `relation` and `witness` members in `machine.json`; for version 2.20, the `hero`, `unresolved-reference`, `schema` and `field-version` findings at `$.hero` and below with their literal details, the landing page's header and its open questions, the region of overview figures and its first element, its `data-hero` attribute and the omission line, `machine.json`'s `hero` member, the reading ledger's `index.html` entry and the brief v16 hero paragraph; for version 2.22, each page's navigation entries in order and its fold's class, summary, `open` state and links, the contents page's links before and after its run heading, the `pages` lists of `dossier.json` and `machine.json`, the order of the landing page's main content, every internal link of the site and whether it resolves, and each Unknown or withheld claim's element on the page `machine.json` names for it; for version 2.23, the `figure-kind`, `containment-depth`, `containment-size`, `containment-flat`, `unit-reference`, `relation-challenged`, `schema` and `field-version` findings with their details, each containment's laid-out boxes, its two forms and which the stylesheet displays at each width, its tally, list, witnessed-nesting items, declarative source and `figureStructures` entry, and figure fit's entries; for version 2.24, the `unresolved-reference`, `element-type`, `unit-reference`, `element-parent`, `relationship-not-derivable`, `view-duplicate`, `view-relationship`, `view-shape`, `model-unviewed`, containment, `relation-challenged`, `schema` and `field-version` findings with their details, each view's figure, each shared element's boxes, list items and single claim anchor, each derived relationship's arrow and item, `machine.json`'s `model` member, and the migrated draft and its rendered figures beside the original's. For terminology, record the understanding section, the full issued Markdown, the issuance report and the brief record including the schema identity and each file digest. For the question-kind floor, record check's report member `questionKindAdvice`, the check record and step log beside it, and the issued brief v21; for version 2.33, the plan findings with their paths, details and order, the report's `figureAdvice` member, the check record, `run-advice.json` and reading ledger beside it, the brief v22 figure-plan paragraph, and the rendered bytes of a v14 draft beside the same draft without `figurePlan`, and a v14 draft's `worthKnowing` advice, callout and `questionKindAdvice` beside the same members' in a v13 draft; for version 2.35, the `base-reference`, `base-conflict`, `base-root`, `base-group`, `base-size`, `base-label`, `schema` and `field-version` findings with their details, each base view's figure, its chip, legend entry, tally sentence, hidden items and note, each base element's label, mark, double border, identity and provenance attribute, each display name's line and anchor, the notices for an unknown or unresolved base, the `base` member of `machine.json`, the map pages beside those rendered with no draft, and each `figure-plan` finding and `figureAdvice` member of a draft with base views; for version 2.38, the relationship state and reason a withheld-end edge migrates to, and which `<figure>` elements, figure entries, figure-fit members and reading ledgers of the migrated draft keep the parity. For the choice floor, record check's `readerAdvice.choiceFloor` and the check record beside it; for version 2.40, the walk findings with their paths, details and order, the walked figure's page (its layer, boxes, badges, source links and their accessible names, list and the paragraphs' unchanged ids) beside the same draft rendered without the walk, the uniqueness of every id on each page, the walk's style rules on each page, and the browser's `:target` highlight, focus after a step's link and Tab order with scripts off, and the brief v24 walk paragraph. For the test-name recognizers, record the render's `testNames` member, `reference/test-names.html` and `machine.json`'s `testNames`; for version 2.43, each migrated draft's element and relationship counts and check's findings on it; for version 2.44, each `imports` edge's witness state and reason at check and at render, its attributes and evidence text, and its `machine.json` `witness` member; for version 2.45, each `view-scope`, `view-level`, `view-scope-outside` and `field-version` finding, each zoom link's place, address and text, the layer, the order of the page's links, and the keyboard focus moving through them.
- **Oracle:** Expected findings and byte ranges computed from the fixture blobs outside the checker, by an independent byte search over the fixture's own normalised text; expected labels, citation outcomes and Unknown reasons fixed in the fixture; and for presentation elements the expected HTML element names, header-cell scopes, empty-cell text, short-label findings and, with no recognized script condition in the bound policy, the absence of any script or content security policy change fixed in the fixture, with each code excerpt's expected bytes read from the fixture blob outside the renderer. For figure homes, the expected page and position of each figure and each home finding's kind and location fixed in the fixture; for figure fit, the expected entries computed by the stated arithmetic from each fixture figure's laid-out width read from the rendered drawing's own width attribute, never from check's code, with the two column widths (navigation open) and the drawn width of a 1048 px figure confirmed by measuring the rendered pages in a browser at both viewports. For derived structure, the expected facts and counts computed from the fixture tree's paths and modes by an independent walk outside Syzygy, the expected digest recomputed from the written file's bytes, and the expected currency outcome fixed in the fixture from the fixture policy bytes and their digest. For the reading ledger, the expected pages and numbers computed by hand from the fixture draft by the stated rules, never from check's code; for the difference, the expected entries fixed in the fixture from two hand-written ledgers and their runs' records; for shape packs, the expected selection fixed per fixture tree, the expected server-selection brief taken from the brief-v10 golden with the stated lines changed, and the default pack's composed lines fixed in the fixture as literals; for thesis basis, the expected findings and line fixed in the fixture. For figure kinds, the expected coordinates, laid-out widths and heights of the fixture's small sequence and swimlane computed by hand from the stated constants and written as literals, never read from the layout's code; the expected findings, phone-form text, tally and declarative source fixed in the fixture; the displayed form and scroll widths measured in a browser at both viewports, never computed by the renderer; and for unchanged bytes, the goldens of graph figures, of schema-v6 and earlier drafts and of the provider mode rendered before this version. For manifest recognizers, the expected facts, spans, dispositions, reasons, parse faults and counts of each golden fixture written as literals before the recognizer runs, never read from its code; a reader double that records every path it is asked for and fails on any path the stated dispositions do not read; the screen's outcomes for the fixture paths fixed by running the bound policy's screen, as the screening scenarios do, except the retained-body case, which runs under a screen double; and for unchanged bytes, the goldens of pages, `machine.json`, `derived-structure.json` and the provider mode rendered before this version. For the version 2.8 repairs, the widest member built by hand from the stated caps outside the code under test and measured by encoding it as the check record does; the expected page names, regions, findings and paths written as literals in the fixture, never read from the code under test; and for unchanged bytes, the goldens of check records, pages, `machine.json`, `derived-structure.json` and the provider mode rendered before this version. For version 2.9, the expected map pages, coverage counts, captions, element identities, marks and list entries written as literals in the fixture from the fixture tree and manifest files, never read from the code under test; the expected class of each advice item, the report's entries and the run file written as literals; and, for unchanged bytes, the goldens of check records, of the provider mode and of every page other than those version 2.9 adds or changes. For version 2.10, the expected record sizes measured by encoding the fixture's records outside the code under test, and the expected forms, findings, words, states and counting outcomes written as literals; and, for unchanged bytes, the goldens of check records within the bound, of the provider mode and of every render whose derived-record path holds a readable record or no entry. For version 2.11, the map pages' structure read with an HTML parser outside the code under test, the counts of Unknown files computed from the fixture's files, and the words, counts, widths and classes written as literals. For version 2.14, the expected facts, faults and spans of each fixture file written by hand as literals, and the remainder's counts computed from the fixture tree's paths and modes by an independent walk outside Syzygy, checked to sum to the tree's blob count; and each reader's files built at the stated byte counts, with its expected reasons written as literals. For inventory coverage, the expected covered, omitted and uncovered entries, paths and counts written in the fixture as literals from the fixture draft's and inventory's citations by the stated rule, never from check's code. For task routes and the index, the expected findings fixed in the fixture; the expected index of a fixture dossier written out by hand as literals from the fixture's own text, name by name with each home and each block, never read from the renderer; the expected find-by-task page of a fixture with routes and of one without, as literal markup for every element, attribute and sentence this requirement states; link resolution decided by an independent search of the rendered pages for each `id`, with the number of links examined stated; and for unchanged bytes, the provider golden rendered before this version; for version 2.16, the expected findings, headings, fragment identifiers and contents lists written by hand from the fixture draft before the checker or renderer runs, and, as the reference bytes, the version 2.15 renders of the same drafts of schema v7 and earlier, and for a schema-v8 draft without subsections the version 2.15 render of the same draft with its `schemaVersion` set to v7; for version 2.19, each edge's expected witness state and reason, the findings and the attributes, and, for a witnessed edge, its absent anchor, the draft's page and appendix counts without it and its kept fidelity-verdict entry, written by hand from the fixture tree and draft before the checker or renderer runs, and, as the reference bytes, the version 2.16 findings and renders of the same drafts of schema v8 and earlier; for version 2.20, each draft's expected findings with their paths and details, the omission line and the brief v16 paragraph fixed in the fixture as literals, the expected first element of the region and `machine.json`'s `hero` member under each draft layer state, and, for every draft of schema v9 or earlier, the bytes version 2.19 renders for it; for version 2.22, the navigation order, the run pages, the landing order, the reading order and the fixture's link count written as literals before the renderer runs, and each disclosure's page read from `machine.json` and its marking, reason or withheld words looked for in the element's own text, not its attributes; for version 2.23, each fixture tree's box coordinates, W and H computed by hand from the stated constants and fixed as literals, the findings' details, the list's items, the brief v17 paragraph and the declarative source fixed as literals, and, for every draft of schema v10 or earlier, the bytes version 2.22 renders for it; for version 2.24, each fixture's expected findings, list items and `model` member fixed as literals from the fixture by hand, each migrated fixture written by hand from its original and compared with the migration's output, and each migrated draft's figures, figure-fit member and `machine.json` figure entries compared byte for byte with those its original renders. For terminology, fix the paragraph, placement and v20 identity as literals, independently compose the full expected brief and hash the issued Markdown and schema for the manifest comparison. For the question-kind floor, fix each unit's missing kinds as literals from the fixture tree and the declarations, outside the advice code; for version 2.33, each fixture's expected plan findings and advisories written as literals before check runs, each label key computed by hand from each view's expansion, and a v14 draft's pages compared byte for byte with the same draft rendered without `figurePlan`; for version 2.35, each fixture's expected findings, chip text, hidden items and `base` member fixed as literals from the fixture's tree and draft by hand, and each map page compared byte for byte with the same render of the same tree with no draft; for version 2.38, each migrated fixture's figures and figure entries compared byte for byte with the original's for every figure that draws no withheld-end edge, and the state of each withheld-end edge's relationship fixed by hand as `unknown` (`edge-not-inferred`). For the choice floor, fix each group's count and detail as literals from the fixture's Poles, sections and understanding record, outside the advice code; for version 2.40, each fixture's expected walk findings and each box's position, size and `href` written as literals before check and render run, each box's position taken by hand from the layout's literal constants, and each `href` compared with the citation link the list of the figure carrying the node's claim carries. For the test-name recognizers, fix each fixture's expected names, lines, units and file dispositions as literals written from the fixture text, outside the recognizer code; for version 2.43, each fixture's node, group and edge totals counted by hand from the fixture, and the expected `schema` finding written before check runs; for version 2.44, each `imports` edge's expected state, reason and fact written as literals from the fixture's import facts before the rule runs; for version 2.45, each view's expected findings written from the type table and the model's parents, and each zoom link's expected address written as a literal from the fixture's pages, before check and render run.
- **Oracle independence:** The fixture's expected outcomes are written before the checker runs and are not derived from the checker's output.
- **Falsifier:** An altered, elided, joined or out-of-range quotation passes; a rendered quotation comes from the agent's text, the working tree or an altered stored range; an anchor names a class other than an evidence artifact identifier with integrity digest, or an identifier Syzygy did not recompute; a source page appears for an excluded or unread blob; a finding carries excluded bytes; an agent-labelled Observed claim renders as Observed; a non-normative block carries an anchor; a draft without its understanding record passes; a stale check or review survives a resubmission; a claim inside an element escapes a check a claim block meets, lacks its label marking or its entry in the fidelity verdict; an empty table cell renders anything but the marker "-", or has a label marking or an anchor; a table cell's fidelity judgment ignores its row or column labels; a collapsed summary hides the count of Unknown claims; a short label over 120 Unicode code points, or carrying a citation, a quotation or markup, passes, or renders with a label marking or an anchor; a code excerpt renders the agent's text, more than 60 lines, or any byte of an excluded blob; a path-excluded excerpt body is read to check its end; a missing path, malformed or reversed range, citation of more than 60 lines, or out-of-range read-then-excluded excerpt passes; a finding discloses an excluded blob's line count; an unverified withheld range is displayed as verified; code text becomes markup; an agent language hint is presented as a verified file fact; a page carries a script or a changed content security policy without the recognized bound renderCondition, or a script or policy that condition does not describe; text, a claim, a marking or a link is reachable only with scripts on; an element states a callout's tone by colour alone, or holds a fragment target inside a collapsible section; an uncited Inferred block passes with no judgement mark or in a schema before v4; a withheld item renders the agent's text, omits its count or counts as verified or supported; a short label over only withheld items renders the agent's words; a secret-matching path is named; a withheld inventory entry renders; a block with both admitted and withheld spans that quotes neither omits its withheld count from its evidence view or machine form; a citation of a file with a redacted line lacks `†` with that file's count, or a citation of a file with none carries it; a verdict's finding text about a withheld item renders; a rendered quotation is not set off; a range touching a redacted line renders its lines; a reading view's block, with its clipped elements removed, holds a label token or aside Syzygy wrote in its `innerText`, or its `textContent` or, with any collapsed ancestor opened, its accessibility tree lacks a distinction; a distinction RFC7-33 lists is missing from the evidence view, the machine form or plain-text extraction, or differs among them; a narrative page's control to the evidence view is missing, cannot be reached by keyboard or needs scripts; a narrative page's legend omits a sentence this requirement gives it, or says anything else; a page lacks the one sentence, its link or its machine disclosure; a Pole rule passes when broken. A v5 figure renders on a page other than its home's, renders twice, renders nowhere while its home's page renders that part of the draft, or lacks its legend or tally; a v5 diagram with an unknown, missing, doubled or malformed home passes, a home's `target` is counted as an identity, a figure homed on a deep dive with no page renders anywhere but where its section's figures render, or its finding does not name the diagram; a draft of schema v4 or earlier yields a different finding or page byte, other than the `field-version` findings versions 2.4, 2.5 and 2.15 state and the additions of version 2.15; a figure and viewport that fails a fit rule has no entry, a fitting one has an entry, or an entry's numbers differ from the stated arithmetic; a figure whose layout cannot be computed is reported as fitting; figure-fit advice changes an outcome or a finding; two checks of the same draft bytes write different `figureFit` members; a record without `figureFit` is read as every figure fitting; the box a figure renders in differs in width between pages at one viewport. A derived element is drawn solid or labelled Observed without a declared bound from the adopted policy bytes, for a record of another revision, or for facts the render's own re-derivation does not reproduce; a derived fact states a size, an object identifier, a body or anything its rule did not match, names a secret-matching path, or lacks its rule, version, path, record digest or revision in its attributes or evidence view; a figure lacks `data-visual-provenance`, a curated figure carries `computed` or draws a solid element, or the computed figure carries draft text; a draft element is upgraded to Observed, or a draft reaches the derived figure or record; the published record differs from the kept one or its digest from its bytes, or the record lacks its source, capture time, scope or provenance; a re-render of one draft revision changes a byte outside the review-status region; the derived figure's legend or tally disagrees with its drawing; a provider-mode byte changes. The reading ledger differs between two checks of the same draft bytes, places a unit, figure or advisory on a page these rules do not place it on, counts the derived figure, or appears in an inventory check record; a difference is written to a file, compares a run of another repository, finds its predecessor by directory name or URL, or reports a number where no predecessor exists; a difference claims an absence over a run or check record it could not read; a ledger or difference changes an outcome or a finding. The selection `default@1`, `server@1` changes a byte of the carried standards, their heading or `readerStandards`, or a brief for it differs from brief v10 beyond the stated lines; a brief of the default pack alone carries a server-only dimension; one tree yields two selections, a selection rests on anything but the trait profile, or a brief record lacks its packs, digests or reasons; a secret-matching path is named or counted for a trait, or alone makes it hold; a composed standards text is carried under version 1's digest or sentence, or a design packet falls back to version 1 when recomposition fails; a provider-mode byte changes. A `code-reading` thesis passes with fewer than two entry points, with a quotation, or not Inferred from sources; a `stated` thesis passes citing nothing; a `none` thesis passes Inferred; the thesis-basis line is missing for a rendered `code-reading` thesis, renders for `stated` or `none` or beside a withheld introduction, sits inside the introduction's block, or carries a label token; a draft of schema v5 or earlier yields a finding or a byte other than those stated. A sequence or swimlane passes whose `from`, `to` or `lane` names nothing in its own diagram, whose members are another kind's, or whose participant or lane nothing names; such a diagram in a draft of schema v6 or earlier yields no `field-version` finding; a `figure-kind` or `field-version` finding stands at a path other than the stated one, beside a `schema` finding at that path, or not in its place; a count outside its bounds passes; a laid-out width differs from 40 + 184 x n px, or a figure of these kinds whose layout resolves has a figure-fit entry at 1440 or 390, or one whose layout does not is reported as fitting; at some width of its box both forms or neither are displayed, either form scrolls sideways at 390 px, or the switch needs a script; the phone form carries an identity, citation, anchor or label token, or text other than the stated item form, or its order differs from the draft's; a declarative source differs from the stated statements, indentation or final newline; a message's or step's order arrow carries a claim or enters the tally; a graph-kind figure, a page holding no figure of these kinds, a draft of schema v6 or earlier, or its `machine.json` changes a byte other than by the additions of version 2.15; `figureStructures` appears in a render with no such figure, or omits one; brief v12 differs from brief v11 beyond the stated lines; a provider-mode byte changes. A manifest fact is recorded from a blob that is secret-matching, set apart, withheld, excluded, retained with lines withheld, past the read limit or not read whole through the pinned object reader, or the object reader is asked for such a blob; a secret-matching or set-apart path is named; a file that does not parse, or is withheld, yields any fact, is named Inferred, or has a count where null is stated; a fact lacks its rule, rule version, path, line span or `"epistemic": "inferred"`, states a version requirement, a size, an object identifier or a field the recognizer does not read, or carries a body-taken string the value checks refuse, or a raw path over 512 code units or with a control character; a line span is a range the path-and-range check refuses for its path, or does not cover what declares the fact; a manifest fact or recognizer entry is labelled other than Inferred, or a draft, model, agent session or run-directory file changes a label; two checks of one run write different `manifestFacts` bytes, or a draft changes them; the member appears in an inventory check record, or is missing from a draft check record of this version, its `unknown` form included where the tree could not be listed; `candidates` differs from the sum of its parts, or a listing cap or count differs from the stated rules, or a file that was read is not listed; a `go.mod` is read under declared-source policy 1.0.0 or 2.0.0; a page, `machine.json`, `derived-structure.json`, brief or provider-mode byte changes, or a manifest fact appears in any of them. A reader of a draft check record refuses one within 16,777,216 bytes, 2,000,000 values and 16 levels below the root, reads one past any of them, or counts or reports such a record as a passed draft or a draft check refused; the `manifestFacts` member at this version's caps takes 13,000,000 bytes or more (10,000,000 or more before version 2.14); a derived region, `machine.json` or published file names a path that no `top-level-unit` fact of the render's own re-derivation holds, or a rule or version other than `top-level-unit` version 1, or a record holding one is drawn, listed or published; a reading-ledger page name differs from the path the renderer's rule gives the same deep dive, or counts a surrogate pair as two characters; a schema-v7 graph diagram lacking `nodes` or `edges` reports a `schema` finding at that member's path or no `figure-kind` finding, or one carrying `participants`, `messages`, `lanes` or `steps` reports a `field-version` finding for it; a check record, page, `machine.json`, `derived-structure.json` or provider-mode byte changes for a run whose records check wrote under the same screen and Syzygy version as the render, whose check records each take at most 4,194,304 bytes, and whose deep-dive identities hold only the permitted characters. A map element or entry labelled Observed whose fact the currency rule does not make Observed, or a manifest-derived element or entry not Inferred; a map naming a path, name or count its derivation does not hold, or taking a fact from the draft or a stored check record; a coverage line whose counts do not partition its denominator, or a candidate file counted in no clause of its coverage line; a map drawing laid out in more than two columns or holding more than 25 boxes, scaled, or displayed in a box narrower than W, or a map figure that does not fit at 1440 or 390; a map figure without `data-visual-provenance="computed"`, or a drawn element without its fact's identity where it has a fact; an arrow between two packages or modules; a figure of the draft on a map page; `run-advice.json` listing one advisory twice, or differing from its function of the run's check records; or a check outcome, render, review or close that reads it; a check record Syzygy writes lies past the read bound, or a bounded form passes, lacks its one `check-record-bound` finding or holds a member it leaves out; a reader of a draft, inventory or derived record reads any byte of a file whose size is past its bound, reads more than one byte past its bound, blocks on its path, or reads from anything but a regular file there; a bounded check record's report whose `readingDifference` is anything but `unknown` with the bounded form's words and both counts 0; a count of one on a map page written in the plural, or a singular form other than those stated; or the readable layout laying out a graph of two or more layers in columns; a fact taken from a file no stated path rule names, from a field not stated, or from an excluded, withheld, denied or unread body; an infrastructure fact labelled Observed or drawn solid; a provider-mode byte changed; a bounded form of a draft that does not parse given the words for a draft that does not parse; or a reader of the draft check is given, of a frozen draft revision of `brief.json` or of `run-advice.json` that blocks on its path, reads more than one byte past its bound, reads from anything but a regular file, or takes such a file for a draft, a passed revision, a brief record or a run file. an assignment or one of the listed common words is an identifier; a file more than 24 places name draws a block link; an entry omits a block that names it by these rules or lists one that does not; a link of either page names an `id` its page does not hold, or a fragment identity lies inside a collapsible section, or a page holds one `id` twice; a withheld step shows a file or a dossier link beside it; the index shows a name the site shows on no other page, the agent's text of a withheld item, a path or span a secret detector matches, or any agent name while the draft layer is Unknown;
