an operator-agent page of a draft without task routes changes a byte other than by the stated identities and links, or a page other than the two finding pages carries their style rules; a provider-mode byte changes; a subsection answer of two sentences by rule `one-sentence-answer` version 1 passes, or one of one sentence by it is a finding, or a non-normative answer passes; a draft of schema v7 or earlier carrying `subsections` yields a reading ledger, reader advice or any finding other than the `field-version` finding that differs from version 2.15's; a section carrying `subsections` in a draft of schema v7 or earlier yields no `field-version` finding, or one beside a `schema` finding at that path; a subsection renders without its `h3`, out of draft order, before its section's own paragraphs, or with the agent's words in a title over only withheld claims; a contents list is missing from a page that renders a subsection, present on one that renders none, or links a fragment no element of its page carries; a page that renders no subsection, a draft of schema v7 or earlier, a schema-v8 draft without subsections, or a provider-mode render changes a byte; a makefile's recipe text inside a conditional yields a `make-target` fact, a conditional-directive line ends a rule's recipe context, or a tab-led logical line that is no recipe line and lies outside a `define` body yields no `syntax` fault, or a continuation line, a `define` body line or a tab-only blank line yields one; a conditional-directive line or a line whose first word is a directive the reader reads yields a `make-target` fact; an arrow is drawn solid or Observed whose relation differs from its witnessing fact's kind, whose end nodes are matched by label rather than by unit, whose witnessing fact does not render Observed at that render, or whose relation's rule is not in the eligibility table; a challenge is raised where coverage is partial, where the tree holds a submodule, where an end's unit drew a `unit-reference` finding, where the rule is not eligible, or for an edge labelled Unknown or withheld, or is not raised where coverage is complete and no fact of the identical kind joins the two units; a challenge or `unit-reference` finding names a unit's path or name; a relation outside the closed list or a unit outside the six forms passes; a witnessed arrow shows the edge's own label or lacks its fact's provenance, or carries a claim anchor or enters the draft's counts; a unit's value appears in a finding, page or `machine.json`, or a `unit-reference` finding reveals whether a blob exists at a path a secret detector matches; a box of a draft is drawn solid; a draft of schema v8 or earlier or a provider-mode render changes a byte; an eligibility row holds after a module its record names has changed; a schema-v10 draft with no `hero`, a malformed `hero`, a hero naming no diagram, a diagram not homed on the overview or one of no graph kind, or a non-normative omission passes check, or is refused with only a `schema` finding at `$.hero` or `$.hero.figure`; a passed v10 landing page places its region of overview figures anywhere but immediately after the header, opens it with anything but the hero or the omission, renders the hero twice or on another page, or omits the omission's reason or its line; an omission's block escapes a check a claim block meets or lacks its fidelity entry; a hero's disposition other than `produced` raises a finding; a hero, notice or omission renders where the draft layer is withheld, or `machine.json` carries a diagram's identity there; a finding's detail differs from its literal; or a draft of schema v9 or earlier, or a provider-mode page, changes a byte other than the `field-version` finding in place of the `schema` finding at `$.hero`; for version 2.22, a run page is linked from a navigation outside its fold or from the contents page before its run heading, a fold holds anything but the run pages the render holds, a run page is unreachable or not rendered, an internal link does not resolve, the landing page puts anything but the region of overview figures between its header and the Pole map or puts a section before the Pole map, the index stands anywhere but directly after find-by-task, a disclosure leaves its claim's element or page, or a provider-mode byte changes; a containment deeper than three levels, over 40 groups or with no nesting passes, or is refused only by a `schema` finding; a box's coordinates, W or H differ from the stated layout or depend on a label's width; a line, arrow or connector is drawn between groups; a box of a draft is drawn solid or labelled Observed; a nesting is witnessed by a fact of another kind, across other unit kinds, or by a recognizer outside the eligibility table, or challenged where coverage is partial; an unknown nesting raises a finding; a containment fails figure fit at 1440 or 390, or both forms, or neither, display at a width; the phone form carries an identity, citation, anchor or label token, or a script is needed; or a draft of schema v10 or earlier, or a provider-mode page, changes a byte other than the `field-version` findings in place of `schema` findings; a view names an element the model lacks, or a relationship without its ends, and passes; a shared element renders two labels, two markings or two claim anchors; an element or relationship no view shows passes; a derived relationship renders as the draft's claim, renders Observed without a witnessing fact, or passes where no eligibility row can derive it; a parent cycle, or a chain that runs into one, passes; a finding is computed beside a `schema` finding on the members it reads; a migrated draft of a draft check passes fails check, gains an advisory, or renders a figure byte its original does not; a shared element's later item links to a notice or to no `id`, or a witnessed relationship renders the draft's label; a view links to another view or a level; or a draft of schema v11 or earlier, or a provider-mode page, changes a byte other than the `field-version` findings in place of `schema` findings. For terminology, removing the paragraph or restoring v19 still passes, or the issued brief omits, alters or misplaces the paragraph, a version carrier is stale, or the brief record no longer binds the actual artifacts. For the question-kind floor: a unit lists a kind a counted block declares for it; a block labelled Unknown or non-normative counts; the advice is compared with nothing declared; it enters a check record, a step log, a page or an outcome; or the brief's paragraph names a regression question; for version 2.33, a v14 draft without a plan, with a place unplanned or planned twice, or with a planned figure that is missing, homed elsewhere or of another kind proceeds to review; a plan entry's text reaches a page or a published file; an advisory is a finding, changes an outcome or a record, or is counted by the reading ledger or `run-advice.json`; an advisory fires on a fixture outside its rule or is missing from one inside it; or a draft of schema v13 or earlier changes any finding but the `field-version` one at `$.figurePlan`, or any byte; a v14 draft loses the worth-knowing mark, its callout or its advice, or the question-kind advice; or the question-kind floor counts a diagram or a plan entry; a base element renders the draft's label, mark or identity, lacks its double border, or holds a display name inside its box, or a draft member adds, alters or removes a map element or a map page byte; an element of the draft takes a shown base element's name or double border; a view hides an element without a reason, or a hidden element is missing from the chip's count or the hidden items; the chip's numbers differ from the map's at that render, or a count the map does not list is missing from it; a figure resting on an unknown or unresolved base is drawn, or a path naming no listed element is rendered or carried in `machine.json`; a base element carries a claim anchor or enters the draft's counts; figure advice reads, counts or compares a figure with a base; or a draft of schema v14 or earlier, or a provider-mode page, changes a byte other than the `field-version` findings in place of `schema` findings; a figure that draws no withheld-end edge loses byte parity under migration, or a withheld-end edge's migrated relationship has a witness state other than `unknown` (`edge-not-inferred`) or renders Observed. For the choice floor: a group with three items on record is advised, or one with two is not; an Unknown item, or one sharing no file with the Pole's sections and their deep dives, counts; an item matched by a later citation, a nested citation or a deep dive of the Pole does not count; a section counts in a second Pole; or the advice enters a check record, an outcome or an earlier schema's advice; or a v14 or v15 draft is counted otherwise than a v13 draft; for version 2.40, a walk whose figure, element or paragraph does not resolve as stated proceeds to review; a box lies off its node; a walk renders over a drawing 1048 px wide; an id is carried twice on a page; a step's link does not highlight its element, or highlights it by script or by colour alone; a box's link lands anywhere but the cited lines its first citation's link lands on; a base element's box takes a link, or a finding's detail holds a base path the draft wrote; a step's item nests a link; a fragment target lies in a collapsible section; a paragraph's bytes, the drawing's bytes, `machine.json`, a check record, the reading ledger or the provider golden move; a page without a walk changes; or a draft of schema v15 or earlier changes any finding but the `field-version` one at a walk, or any byte. For the test-name recognizers: a name inside a comment, a docstring or a string is listed; a vendored, generated or secret-matching path is named or read; a body the screen excludes yields a name; a name is labelled other than Inferred; or a render without the member changes a byte; for version 2.43, check derives a finding from the migrated value of a draft whose nodes and groups number at most 200 and edges at most 400, or none from that of a draft past either bound, a nesting is counted as a relationship, or the migration yields no draft for it; for version 2.44, an `imports` edge renders Observed or solid, is `challenged` or draws a `relation-challenged` finding, is corroborated by a fact whose outcome is not `edge` or that joins other units, or a page or `machine.json` names an import fact's path or operand; for version 2.45, a view shows an element of a type its level does not admit, or a part outside its scope, with no finding; a box whose element has its own view carries no zoom link or one to another figure; a zoom link needs a script or is unreachable by keyboard; a context view is a zoom target; a walk rule outlines or takes the pointer for a box of a zoom-only layer; or a v17 draft without `level` and `scope` renders other bytes than in schema v16.

warrants:
  primary: VIS-2
  doctrine: [SEC-3, SEC-5, VIS-1, VIS-2, VIS-3, VIS-4, SEC-2, VIS-7]
  contracts: [RFC2-23, RFC2-24, RFC5-17, RFC7-2, RFC7-9, RFC7-10, RFC7-13, RFC7-19, RFC7-20, RFC7-33, RFC7-34, RFC4-19, RFC5-14, RFC2-9, RFC7-28, RFC9-38, RFC9-27, RFC9-44]
  policies: [CC-SPEC-2, CC-SPEC-4, CC-SPEC-3, CC-REV-2]
  decisions: [POLARIS-DOSSIER-LOCAL-AGENT-MODE-2026-10-05, POLARIS-DOSSIER-LOCAL-AGENT-RULINGS-2026-10-05, POLARIS-DOSSIER-LOCAL-AGENT-REVIEW-1-RULINGS-2026-10-05, POLARIS-DOSSIER-LOCAL-AGENT-RECORDS-2026-10-05, OWNER-DIRECTION-DOSSIER-RICH-ELEMENTS-2026-11-04, OWNER-DIRECTION-P115-LATER-SIGNED-INPUTS-2026-11-04, OWNER-DIRECTION-DOSSIER-VOICE-AND-SCRIPTS-2026-11-06, OWNER-DIRECTION-QUESTIONNAIRE-ANSWERS-2026-11-06, GOVERNANCE-SIMPLIFICATION-D10]
  topology: []
  parent_requirements: [REQ-polaris-generation-002, REQ-polaris-generation-003, REQ-polaris-generation-004, REQ-polaris-generation-006, REQ-polaris-generation-012, REQ-polaris-generation-019, REQ-polaris-generation-025, REQ-polaris-generation-033, REQ-polaris-generation-014, REQ-polaris-generation-035]

#### Scenario: A base-reference finding admits dynamic detail only through the existing screen

- **WHEN** an ordinary check receives fabricated missing or ambiguous base references through `select`, `hide`, `group`, `names`, and a base end in separate matching cases, with submitted path or diagram text admitted, withheld, or unclassified under the existing screen
- **THEN** the admitted cases keep their exact literal details. Every non-admitted case either carries only the fixed diagnostic detail or uses the existing fail-closed refusal. No submitted withheld value or unadmitted population count enters the check result, and the check performs the same source reads as its paired predecessor.

#### Scenario: Other derived-base finding kinds screen the submitted diagram identity

- **WHEN** separate ordinary checks raise `base-conflict`, `base-root`, `base-group`, `base-size` or `base-label` with the same admitted map and finding trigger, and a diagram identity whose existing screening result is admitted, withheld or unclassified
- **THEN** the admitted control retains its literal detail. Every non-admitted scoped result preserves its finding kind and structural field location with only the fixed detail in both the check record and report. An existing fail-closed refusal takes precedence and no submitted unadmitted identity appears. Each trigger stands independently of a `base-reference` finding.

#### Scenario: Unsupported derived-base fields screen their submitted identity

- **WHEN** a v12 or v14 draft supplies an unsupported view base or an unsupported relationship base end and the existing screen admits, withholds or cannot classify the identity in its `field-version` detail
- **THEN** each admitted control retains its literal detail. A non-admitted scoped finding retains its kind and structural field location with only the fixed detail in both the check record and report. An existing fail-closed refusal takes precedence. Other findings and the earlier schema's supported field behavior remain as previously specified.

#### Scenario: Containment findings computed over an expanded base use the same screen

- **WHEN** an established units base expands a containment into two or six ungrouped units, or an established directories base gives the repository root nine children, with no separate base finding and a diagram identity whose existing screening result is admitted, withheld or unclassified
- **THEN** the same `containment-flat` and `view-shape` findings apply as the expanded figure warrants. An admitted control retains the applicable literal details. A non-admitted scoped result uses the fixed detail in the check record and report with the same kinds, locations and order. An existing fail-closed refusal takes precedence. The late render branch retains its fixed notice, and an ordinary containment without a derived base keeps its existing diagnostic behavior.

#### Scenario: Hide and remainder reasons are screened before either derived-base output

- **WHEN** separate synthetic figures carry an admitted alternate reason, a detector-matching reason or a reason with unavailable screening, first as `hide.reason` and then as `restReason`
- **THEN** the admitted reason appears byte-equivalently after HTML text decoding and in the corresponding machine member. The affected figure refuses in both forms when the reason is withheld or screening is unavailable. Neither refused form can carry the submitted text or a derived `base` claim. The current whole-response fail-closed rule takes precedence where applicable.

#### Scenario: The screening boundary preserves admitted map and tally behavior

- **WHEN** a valid derived figure is rendered, with a separately resolved hidden population and admitted reason text, alongside a figure that would emit a withheld reason
- **THEN** on an independently admissible containing output the valid figure retains its counted tally, reason and fact identities, and the map page retains its existing bytes in both forms. The refusal makes no derived claim and does not infer an absent map or an empty hidden population.

#### Scenario: A supported draft with no selected example receives one immediate advisory

- **WHEN** separate structurally valid visible v13 and supported v14 operator-agent draft fixtures contain produced sections and no qualifying code selection, and a separately initialized predecessor run checks identical fixture inputs
- **THEN** the JSON report has exactly the `sourceExampleAdvice` member above, and the human report has the same message once, while all other report fields, outcome, exit status, readiness, findings, durable records, ledger and difference match the predecessor byte or value as appropriate

#### Scenario: Each eligible placement clears the advisory independently

- **WHEN** otherwise identical supported drafts each add only one structural `code` element to a produced section's block list, a produced deep dive's block list or a supported subsection's block list, respectively
- **THEN** each gives no source-example advice; in distinct controls, keeping similar prose or a code-naming string, or allowing a code element only in a non-produced part, leaves the single advisory

#### Scenario: A code selection inside an eligible details list clears the advisory

- **WHEN** the only selected code element in an otherwise matching supported draft is an immediate child of an available `details.elements[]` list in an eligible `paragraphs[]` placement
- **THEN** the report has no absence advisory; independently exercise a produced section, a produced deep dive and a supported subsection. A matched available list with the code replaced by permitted non-code content gives the single advisory. If existing visibility makes a relevant list structurally unavailable, the decision remains Unknown and the advice is absent without carrying any withheld detail

#### Scenario: A graph and its omission do not select a source example

- **WHEN** an otherwise matching supported draft without a code element supplies a produced graph hero, its reasoned omission or a v14 figure plan without a selected `code` item in separate runs
- **THEN** each immediate report carries the one advisory, and each corresponding graph, omission or figure-plan artifact and rendered output matches its equal predecessor without this advice

#### Scenario: Withholding preserves the difference between a known selection and Unknown

- **WHEN** normal screening withholds the cited text of a code element whose structure remains available, or a separate fixture withholds the whole layer or prevents classification of a relevant structural population
- **THEN** neither case emits an absence advisory; no withheld identity or value appears and the read trace equals the predecessor, while the equivalent available empty population emits the one advisory

#### Scenario: Other entry points and a bounded check report remain exact

- **WHEN** otherwise equivalent input exercises an earlier supported schema, inventory, provider check, refusal or the supported draft's existing bounded check-record path
- **THEN** no source-example advice is emitted, and the check output and records equal each exact predecessor for that entry point; the provider rendered golden retains its pinned bytes

#### Scenario: An independently requested report remains separate from source-example advice

- **WHEN** a supported draft receives this advice and another immediate assessment is independently available, first in the same invocation and then with the source-example advice disabled in a paired control
- **THEN** only `sourceExampleAdvice` and its matching human message differ, with the other assessment, findings and readiness, stored records, and render kept byte or value equal

#### Scenario: Pole below the choice floor advised

- **WHEN** a v13 draft declares a Pole naming a section whose claim cites `src/a.c` and a second Pole naming a section whose claim cites `src/b.c` and one whose code excerpt cites `src/c.c`, and its understanding record holds Inferred choices citing `src/a.c` and `src/b.c` and Inferred trade-offs citing `src/a.c`, `src/b.c` and `src/c.c`
- **THEN** check's `readerAdvice.choiceFloor` holds exactly one advisory, at `$.poles[0]` with `onRecord` 2 and its literal detail, and none for the second Pole, which has three on record
- **AND** the check's outcome is what it would be without the advice, and the check record carries no `choiceFloor`

#### Scenario: Choice floor counts Inferred items once per Pole

- **WHEN** a v13 draft records an Unknown choice and an Inferred choice citing a file no section cites, and declares a second Pole naming only a section the first Pole names
- **THEN** neither of those choices is on record for any Pole, and the second Pole's advisory has `onRecord` 0
- **AND** an item whose matching file is named only by its second citation, by a citation nested in a child or a list item of a section, or by a deep dive whose `sectionId` names one of the Pole's sections, is on record for that Pole
- **AND** a draft with no Poles has one group, at `$.sections`, counting every Inferred choice and trade-off, a schema-v14 or schema-v15 draft is counted as a schema-v13 draft is, and a draft of schema v12 or earlier has no `choiceFloor` member

#### Scenario: Test names read from code, never from comments or strings

- **WHEN** under declared-source policy 2.0.0 a render's pinned tree holds `test_root.py` declaring `test_at_root`; tests/test_api.py with a comment `# def test_commented():`, a function `test_get` whose docstring holds `def test_doc(): pass`, and a class `TestPost` with a method `test_create`; `web/src/app.test.ts` with a comment `// it('commented')`, a call `describe('app', ...)` holding `it('renders', ...)` and a string holding `it('in a string')`; `cmd/run_test.go` with a commented `func TestCommented(t *testing.T) {}` and `func TestRun(t *testing.T) {}`; and `java/src/FooTest.java` with a commented `@Test void commented()` and `@Test void works()`
- **THEN** the member lists eight names in order: `TestRun` (unit `cmd`), `works` (`java`), `test_at_root` (unit null), `test_get`, `TestPost` as a suite and `test_create` (`tests`), and `app` as a suite and `renders` (`web`), each Inferred with its path and line
- **AND** no name, member or page of the render holds `test_commented`, `test_doc`, `commented`, `in a string` or `TestCommented`

#### Scenario: Test files left out, set apart or Unknown

- **WHEN** a tree holds a pytest candidate under a directory a secret detector matches, one under `vendor/`, a JavaScript candidate under `dist/`, a symbolic link tests/test_link.py, tests/test_open.py whose body ends inside a triple-quoted string, tests/test_refused.py whose read the reader refuses, tests/test_bytes.py that is not UTF-8 text and tests/test_secret.py whose body a secret detector matches
- **THEN** `pytest-test-name` counts 7 candidates, 1 left out for its secret path, 1 set apart, none read for names and 5 Unknown, listed as `not-utf8-text`, `unparsed` with `unterminated`, `object-read-refused`, `secret-detector-match` and `symbolic-link`; `js-test-call` sets 1 apart
- **AND** the member names no unit, and neither the secret-matching directory, `vendor`, `dist/` nor any name from the excluded bodies

#### Scenario: Test names the value checks refuse

- **WHEN** a JavaScript test file calls `it` with a name of 257 characters, with an empty name and with `kept`
- **THEN** its file entry is Inferred with `names` 1 and `valuesLeftOut` 2, and only `kept` is listed

#### Scenario: Test-names page rendered

- **WHEN** a render holds the derived structure and a member whose units are the root, `tests` and `web`, one name of which is `renders <b>`, and one Unknown file tests/test_open.py (`unparsed`, `unterminated`)
- **THEN** `reference/test-names.html` holds the per-unit table with the row `Repository root`, its files read and its names per rule, each name entry `Test <name>, in <path> at line <n>.` with `Derived by Syzygy` and its evidence, the name shown as `renders &lt;b&gt;`, and the Unknown file's item opened by `Not established.` with `data-unknown-reason="mapping-coverage-absent"`
- **AND** `contents.html` links the page and `machine.json`'s `testNames` holds its page and the member's names, and the page holds no script

#### Scenario: Test names Unknown when the tree is not listed

- **WHEN** the pinned tree cannot be listed at a render
- **THEN** the member is `{"format", "state": "unknown", "why"}` and the page's section holds only its `Not established.` sentence naming the revision

#### Scenario: No test-names page without the member

- **WHEN** a render holds no `testNames` member, or holds one and no derived structure, or is a provider-mode render
- **THEN** it publishes no `reference/test-names.html`, no link to it and no `testNames` member, and its bytes are those the replaced version renders; the provider golden's bytes are unchanged
